# Theta curvature microprogramme

Author: `marici.Grothendieck`

Status: live research index. The historical compendium
`theta-mixed-sheet-green-clifford-obstruction.md` is frozen and receives no
further substantive append.

## Relative prime interface

[`the-relative-prime-likelihood-is-a-degenerate-martingale.md`](the-relative-prime-likelihood-is-a-degenerate-martingale.md)
sharpens the Kakutani obstruction dynamically. Finite Haar-to-arithmetic
Poisson densities form a mean-one martingale, but their logarithm has drift
$-\sum_{p\le X}p^{-1}$ and variance $2\sum_{p\le X}p^{-1}+O(1)$, so the
density tends to zero almost surely and is not uniformly integrable. The
primitive current is the divergent fluctuation and the square current its
deterministic compensator; neither can be completed as an independent scalar
correction. The next gate is a source-derived distributional interface for
this coupled pair before exponentiation.

## Labelled zero lift

[`the-evans-cancellation-line-is-a-self-adjoint-dirac-boundary.md`](the-evans-cancellation-line-is-a-self-adjoint-dirac-boundary.md)
corrects the boundary interpretation of Entry 4141. The oppositely oriented
tail principal operator
$A_0=-i\operatorname{diag}(1,-1)\partial_q$ has Green form of signature
$(1,-1)$, and the scalar cancellation line $(a,-a)$ is maximal isotropic.
Thus it is a self-adjoint boundary condition preserving interference. With
$z=-i\lambda$, real spectrum is exactly the critical seam. The sole remaining
operator obstruction is the finite-rank source/constant channel introduced
when the inhomogeneous tails are homogenized.

[`reciprocal-tails-turn-the-adjoint-residual-into-the-interference-square.md`](reciprocal-tails-turn-the-adjoint-residual-into-the-interference-square.md)
tests the only native repair left by Entry 4140. The two reciprocal tails
satisfy
$\int f(G_++G_-)=G_+(0)G_-(0)$. At an even scalar zero their endpoints
are opposite, so the combined reverse-arrow residual is $-b^2$, the square
of the surviving odd interference route. Reciprocal sewing therefore does
not make adjoint closure redundant; demanding zero residual changes scalar
cancellation into separate route collapse. Only a boundary quotient retaining
the interference channel remains viable.

[`adjoint-closure-of-the-tail-pencil-changes-the-zero-bridge.md`](adjoint-closure-of-the-tail-pencil-changes-the-zero-bridge.md)
tests spectralization on the actual affine tail system. The zero-to-state
pencil is triangular: the constant channel forces $G$ but has no reverse
equation. Symmetric completion adds $-\langle f,G\rangle=0$, which is not
implied by endpoint vanishing. The exact source
$f=e^{-q}-3e^{-2q}$ has a zero at $s=1/2$ with a two-endpoint tail, yet
$\langle f,G\rangle=-1/6$. Direct adjoint closure therefore changes the
divisor. Reciprocal sewing must supply precisely the missing reverse arrow,
or the spectralization lane closes.

[`hermitianization-is-not-spectralization.md`](hermitianization-is-not-spectralization.md)
separates the final operator-theoretic types. The universal Hermitian matrix
with off-diagonal entries $F(z)$ and $\overline{F(z)}$ is self-adjoint and
has a kernel exactly at every scalar zero, but $z$ remains an external complex
parameter and no location is constrained. Conversely, universal Dirac
doubling creates a fixed self-adjoint operator while changing the Evans
divisor. The actual Hilbert--Pólya burden is a fixed source-derived
self-adjoint operator whose spectral pencil preserves the framed zero
condition.

[`gaussian-adjoint-doubling-is-self-adjoint-but-changes-the-divisor.md`](gaussian-adjoint-doubling-is-self-adjoint-but-changes-the-divisor.md)
globalizes the adjoint observer in the Gaussian Bargmann topology, where
$X^*=P$. The quotient coordinate $W=X^2-1/4$ has exact Green defect
$P^2-X^2$. Its universal two-sheet Dirac doubling is self-adjoint, but its
kernel records singular values of $W$, not zeros of the framed Evans
cross-pairing. Thus automatic self-adjointization changes the divisor. The
remaining theorem must cancel the Green defect through a source-derived
quotient while preserving the zero-to-kernel bridge.

[`the-canonical-adjoint-weyl-observer-is-logarithmic-curvature.md`](the-canonical-adjoint-weyl-observer-is-logarithmic-curvature.md)
constructs the cheapest independently sourced dual observer: evaluation and
derivative evaluation applied to $F$ and $PF$. Its determinant is exactly
$FF''-(F')^2$. At a simple zero it equals $-(F')^2\ne0$, repairing the
Weyl center's invisibility and proving transversality, but its phase is
arbitrary off the seam. Thus every finite local adjoint lift collapses back
to the curvature obstruction. The next observer must be a global adjoint
tail lift with its kernel quotient and topology retained.

[`the-weyl-center-is-invisible-to-the-scalar-evans-zero.md`](the-weyl-center-is-invisible-to-the-scalar-evans-zero.md)
lifts the corrected three-rung tower to the Evans observer. The first
commutator evaluates to $-2F'(z_0)$, so the mandatory odd port detects
simple versus multiple incidence. But the central commutator evaluates to
$-F(z_0)=0$ on the zero locus. The tower closes algebraically while its
center remains invisible to scalar evaluation. The missing constructor is
therefore an independently sourced dual observer, not another source-side
commutator.

[`joint-mellin-character-and-heat-naturality-generate-the-weyl-tower.md`](joint-mellin-character-and-heat-naturality-generate-the-weyl-tower.md)
corrects the transport typing in Entry 4132. Source dilation becomes
multiplication by the centered Mellin character $X=M_z$, not
$z\partial_z$. With $P=\partial_z$ and Newman heat $L=P^2$, the
source generates the three-rung Weyl tower
$[X,L]=-2P$, $[X,P]=-I$, and $[P,L]=0$. Its joint commutant is
scalar, so hostile heat endomorphisms are rejected, but the full constructor
orbit necessarily retains the odd derivative channel hidden by even scalar
compression.

[`joint-heat-dilation-naturality-rigidifies-the-even-source.md`](joint-heat-dilation-naturality-rigidifies-the-even-source.md)
is superseded by the correctly typed Weyl-tower result above. Its
scalar-commutant conclusion survives, but its claimed $z\partial_z$
transport and affine coherence do not. On the even analytic
sector, commuting with dilation $D=z\partial_z$ makes an endomorphism
diagonal by monomial grade, while commuting with heat $L=\partial_z^2$
forces all grades to share one coefficient. Their joint commutant is scalar,
and the exact coherence is $[D,L]=-2L$. This rejects the known
divisor-changing heat endomorphisms and closes source ambiguity. The remaining
RH content is global incidence of the uniquely selected framed section.

[`the-lattice-scale-groupoid-repairs-orbit-descent-but-is-universal.md`](the-lattice-scale-groupoid-repairs-orbit-descent-but-is-universal.md)
repairs the finite-label obstruction by replacing a fixed comb with the
scale-indexed family $\Delta_a$. Dilation moves among its fibers and
Poisson summation sends $a\mapsto a^{-1}$, producing a genuine reciprocal
fundamental chamber before Mellin readout. But this groupoid exists for every
Schwartz source, including hostile Fourier-fixed carriers. The missing
selector must therefore enrich orbit descent by the Gaussian vacuum
connection and independently framed endpoint transport.

[`theta-reciprocal-descent-begins-only-after-infinite-label-aggregation.md`](theta-reciprocal-descent-begins-only-after-infinite-label-aggregation.md)
identifies the exact mismatch with Strominger's finite magnetic orbit.
Fourier transform sends an individual integer point mass to a global
character, so no finite theta-label module is Fourier-stable; reciprocal
scale descent appears only after the complete comb is aggregated. The theta
tail does canonically preserve the framed Evans endpoint $(X(s),1)$, not
merely its projective line, but that frame remembers winding without confining
it. The next object must combine an infinite primal--dual label
correspondence with an independently sourced boundary connection before
scalar aggregation.

[`antiunitary-reciprocal-sewing-preserves-the-order-current.md`](antiunitary-reciprocal-sewing-preserves-the-order-current.md)
closes the naive reciprocal bulk-sign route. Scale reflection reverses both
\(\Lambda\) and \(S\), while conjugation reverses the imaginary scalar in
the current; the physical antiunitary map therefore preserves both the order
current and its positive commutator slope. Any confinement mechanism must now
be a source-specific relative-boundary anomaly rather than an interior
reciprocal sign.

[`every-finite-mellin-zero-is-a-strict-upward-crossing-of-the-order-current.md`](every-finite-mellin-zero-is-a-strict-upward-crossing-of-the-order-current.md)
builds the canonical zero-state directly from a scalar Mellin zero and proves
that its order current crosses strictly upward, since its derivative is the
positive order--Mellin commutator. This closes the naive virial contradiction:
zero does not force zero commutator expectation. Because the orientation is
universal at every horizontal coordinate, the next gate is a source-specific
reciprocal-sheet transformation law for the two order currents.

[`the-order-mellin-commutator-is-strictly-positive-on-every-conductor-complement.md`](the-order-mellin-commutator-is-strictly-positive-on-every-conductor-complement.md)
provides the first native orientation--energy bridge. With logarithmic Mellin
generator \(\Lambda\) and ordered source port \(S\),
\([\Lambda,S]_{ij}=-|\log n_i-\log n_j|\). Conditional negative type of
line distance makes this commutator strictly positive on every nonzero
fiberwise zero-mean conductor-complement state. The next gate is deriving a
zero-to-state virial identity from the completed theta/Tate boundary system.

[`positive-mellin-windows-erase-the-phase-carried-by-the-source-order-port.md`](positive-mellin-windows-erase-the-phase-carried-by-the-source-order-port.md)
closes the positive-window lane as a direct orientation mechanism. Modulus
squares prove fixed-state faithfulness but erase the relative phase carried
by the framed Wronskian. The actual source order port has the reciprocal-odd
principal-value multiplier \(2i\,\operatorname{pv}(1/\xi)\) and is
indefinite. The next target must couple this oriented current to the positive
trajectory norm and explicit arithmetic boundary channels.

[`the-windowed-mellin-trajectory-is-faithful-but-not-coercive.md`](the-windowed-mellin-trajectory-is-faithful-but-not-coercive.md)
replaces growing finite jet bases by their full vertical Mellin generating
trajectory. Every positive almost-everywhere integrable window gives strict
energy for each nonzero finite-variation coefficient measure. But adjacent
label differences prove that no lower \(\ell^2\) frame bound exists. This
separates fixed-state faithfulness from uniform cutoff coercivity; the next
gate is deriving the actual window and boundary terms from theta completion.

[`the-mellin-jet-tower-generates-every-finite-conductor-complement.md`](the-mellin-jet-tower-generates-every-finite-conductor-complement.md)
closes the finite cyclicity gate. On a conductor fiber of size \(m\), the
first \(m-1\) centered Mellin jets form an invertible logarithmic Vandermonde
basis of the zero-mean complement. The tower is not fitted: it is the jet
tower of the single authorized transport \(e^{z\Lambda}\). The remaining
obstructions are completion stability and a conservation law connecting this
faithful Gram energy to scalar Wronskian orientation.

[`the-mellin-descent-commutator-is-the-canonical-off-diagonal-current.md`](the-mellin-descent-commutator-is-the-canonical-off-diagonal-current.md)
constructs the missing source-native coupling. If \(E\) is conductor-fiber
averaging and \(\Lambda=\operatorname{diag}(\log n)\), then
\((1-E)\Lambda E\) is the off-diagonal part of the failure of descent to
commute with Mellin transport. Its squared norm is the within-fiber
logarithmic variance. The next gate is whether its centered moment tower
cyclically generates the complete complementary conductor block.

[`finite-fourier-duality-separates-rather-than-sews-the-mackey-blocks.md`](finite-fourier-duality-separates-rather-than-sews-the-mackey-blocks.md)
proves the first obstruction after the Mackey lift. For a quotient with fiber
\(K\), Fourier duality sends the descended block to support on \(K^\perp\)
and its complement to the disjoint spectral support. It diagonalizes the
old/new conductor split rather than coupling it. Therefore any phase-bearing
sewing map must use extra theta/Tate source structure, not finite Fourier
inversion or pull--push alone.

[`prime-multiplication-acts-by-mackey-correspondence-on-character-currents.md`](prime-multiplication-acts-by-mackey-correspondence-on-character-currents.md)
classifies the authorized inter-character maps. Units modulo a conductor
permute character ports; prime divisors instead factor through a smaller
conductor and define a pull--push correspondence with composite
\(q_*q^*=|\ker q|\,\mathrm{id}\). The varying-conductor Mackey system is the
missing arithmetic transport type. It controls descent and multiplicity but
not yet the phase of the complementary character block.

[`character-completeness-turns-the-trivial-wronskian-into-a-balance-law.md`](character-completeness-turns-the-trivial-wronskian-into-a-balance-law.md)
derives the exact characterwise conservation law for framed route Wronskians.
Their normalized sum is the diagonal label current; for reciprocal Mellin
routes the total is the fixed negative logarithmic source moment. The scalar
crossing current is balanced by all nontrivial character ports rather than
destroyed. This is conservation, not confinement: the next gate is a
prime-multiplicative or Fourier--Tate relation restricting redistribution.

[`the-canonical-theta-route-wronskian-measures-interference-crossing.md`](the-canonical-theta-route-wronskian-measures-interference-crossing.md)
implements Strominger's crossing lift on the canonical positive/negative
theta half-line routes. The framed Wronskian
\(\Omega=AB'-BA'\) is even, real-entire, and has an exact two-source
integral. At a scalar zero, \(\Omega=A X'\), so it separates simple
nonzero-route interference from route collapse or tangency without fitted
kernelization. Its sign is automatic on the real Mellin axis but not on the
RH seam; the remaining target is an arithmetic character-cycle law
constraining its complex phase.

[`nested-conductors-make-the-complete-character-lift-refinement-compatible.md`](nested-conductors-make-the-complete-character-lift-refinement-compatible.md)
closes Strominger's finite-to-profinite lift gate on the angular side.
Factorial conductors \(Q_N=(N+1)!\) separate the first \(N\) labels and
form a divisibility tower; every coarse character is exactly the fine
character at multiplied frequency. Complete Fourier inversion plus strict
refinement compatibility eliminates the finite covector torsor rather than
moving it into completion. Any remaining escape is radial, in the
valuation/Fock topology and its primitive/square currents.

[`the-full-residue-character-lift-makes-every-finite-mellin-zero-interference.md`](the-full-residue-character-lift-makes-every-finite-mellin-zero-interference.md)
completes the finite analysis lift. For any finite distinct label set, a
conductor larger than all labels embeds it injectively in a cyclic residue
group, and the complete additive Fourier character table reconstructs every
coefficient. Mellin weighting is an invertible diagonal for every \(s\).
Therefore the full finite arithmetic transport never loses a state: every
scalar Mellin zero is nonzero interference in the trivial-character
projection. The one-prime seam kernels were artifacts of incomplete
observation. The remaining RH problem is orientation of interference, which
requires a cyclic or nonlinear coherence invariant.

[`the-one-prime-depth-two-mate-separates-interference-from-seam-route-loss.md`](the-one-prime-depth-two-mate-separates-interference-from-seam-route-loss.md)
constructs the first finite characterwise arithmetic mate. For one prime and
valuation depths one and two, the direct/reciprocal transport determinant is
\(xy(y-x)\). The scalar-even port always has a dark direction, but the sign
port evaluates it as \(-2xy(x-y)\). Thus off the seam scalar darkness is
nonzero interference detected by the complementary character, while genuine
route loss occurs only at the local critical-line resonances
\(p^{1-2s}=1\). The next gate is mixed-prime Ramanujan coupling and
cutoff-uniform generalized gain.

[`cross-sector-lifts-select-a-framed-characterwise-arithmetic-mate.md`](cross-sector-lifts-select-a-framed-characterwise-arithmetic-mate.md)
transfers the strongest current results from Strominger, Kitaev, Aspect,
Nima, and Benincasa. They jointly select a finite-cutoff arithmetic mate that
retains the direct/reciprocal route packet, determinant-line source frame,
dual analysis rows, Fourier-character blocks, and a cyclic holonomy before

as route loss or interference characterwise and measures their generalized

[`universal-comb-fourier-coherence-does-not-fix-null-events.md`](universal-comb-fourier-coherence-does-not-fix-null-events.md)
applies Nima's exact degree-twelve hostile carrier to the new equalizer.
Fourier self-duality, comb sampling, dilation-labelled prime recursion,
conjugation, common adelic height, and complementary homogeneity all survive,
yet its Mellin window has off-seam zeros. Hence those universal structures
cannot fix null events. Minimal carrier provenance rejects this witness but
only separates archimedean zeros from the arithmetic Euler divisor. The
remaining selector must act on the arithmetic determinant line relative to
the fixed carrier.

[`fourier-tate-sewing-reverses-boundary-grade-rather-than-preserving-it.md`](fourier-tate-sewing-reverses-boundary-grade-rather-than-preserving-it.md)
finds the real obstacle to the boundary-condition-number route.
Fourier--Tate transport sends homogeneous degree \(s-1\) to degree \(-s\);
the Euler boundary powers \(N^{1-s}\) and \(N^s\) are its expected
complementary grades. Hence sewing can remain invertible off the seam as a
map between differently graded fibers. Common adelic height synchronizes the
clocks but does not erase this degree shift. RH now requires a second
source-derived coherence forcing null events to be fixed objects, not merely
transported objects, of reciprocal sewing.

[`adelic-height-supplies-the-canonical-reciprocal-cutoff.md`](adelic-height-supplies-the-canonical-reciprocal-cutoff.md)
closes the global cutoff-synchronization gate. The multiplicative adelic
height \(H(r)=\prod_v\max(1,|r|_v)\) satisfies
\(H(r^{-1})=H(r)\) by the product formula, and restricts to \(H(n)=n\)
on positive integers. Thus the Euler cutoff and its reciprocal are one
source-native filtration, not independently chosen clocks. The sole remaining
premise in the boundary-grade route is invertible preservation of the
nonzero leading Euler class under completed theta/Tate sewing.

[`seam-selection-is-invariant-under-a-shared-cofinal-cutoff.md`](seam-selection-is-invariant-under-a-shared-cofinal-cutoff.md)
audits the regulator dependence of the boundary comparator. Seam selection
survives every shared cofinal reparameterization \(N=\phi(T)\), but can be
faked off the seam by independently tuning the two sector clocks. The missing
source datum is therefore a diagonal cofinality functor induced by one
absolute valuation scale. Local Fourier--Tate reversal \(q\mapsto-q\)
suggests such a diagonal; the unresolved global gate is its compatibility
with Euler label cutoffs.

[`the-reciprocal-boundary-comparator-is-uniformly-invertible-only-on-the-seam.md`](the-reciprocal-boundary-comparator-is-uniformly-invertible-only-on-the-seam.md)
turns equal boundary grade into an exact condition-number theorem. At common
cutoff \(N\), the direct-to-reciprocal zero-germ comparator is
\(R_s(N)=s(1-s)^{-1}N^{1-2s}\). It and its inverse are uniformly bounded
exactly on \(\Re s=1/2\), where its modulus is one. Horizontal displacement
is the real Lyapunov exponent of the comparison. RH would follow from the
source theorem that every admissible zero carries this comparison as a
cutoff-compatible uniformly invertible boundary morphism.

[`reciprocal-zero-germs-have-equal-boundary-grade-only-on-the-seam.md`](reciprocal-zero-germs-have-equal-boundary-grade-only-on-the-seam.md)
replaces the impossible ordinary zero-state by its explicit
Euler--Maclaurin relative germ. The direct and reciprocal boundary currents
grow with grades \(1-\Re s\) and \(\Re s\), respectively; equal grade is
equivalent to \(\Re s=1/2\). The new DPC is that completed theta/Tate
normalization identifies reciprocal zero-germs through a
filtration-preserving morphism. Functional symmetry alone does not supply
that theorem, so the hostile off-line quartet remains the decisive test.

[`a-nontrivial-zero-is-not-a-pre-completion-dirichlet-state.md`](a-nontrivial-zero-is-not-a-pre-completion-dirichlet-state.md)
finds the first hard obstruction to completion-stable realization. The formal
label packet at spectral parameter \(s\) is \((n^{-s})\), whose ordinary
partial sums grow like \(N^{1-s}/(1-s)\) throughout the critical strip.
Therefore no nontrivial zero, including an on-seam zero, is an ordinary
pre-completion Dirichlet state. The required carrier must instead be a
distributional, relative, or germ-level boundary object retaining the
primitive and square currents. Completion is not a nuisance around the zeros;
it is where every nontrivial zero necessarily lives.

[`prime-recursion-lifts-every-scalar-zero-through-filtered-endpoints.md`](prime-recursion-lifts-every-scalar-zero-through-filtered-endpoints.md)
finds the source coherence demanded by the two-label falsifier. Exact
prime-scale recursion gives
\(X_{p,r}=p^{-r(1/2+z)}(1-p^{-1/2-z})X\), so every scalar zero annihilates
every prime-filtered endpoint coordinate. This is genuine labelled descent,
but only after scalar continuation. It does not establish a common
pre-completion state or control either reciprocal primitive-current graph
norm. The remaining wall is completion-stable realization, not endpoint
compatibility.

[`scalar-zero-does-not-lift-through-prime-filtered-endpoints.md`](scalar-zero-does-not-lift-through-prime-filtered-endpoints.md)
separates scalar cancellation from prime-filtered admissibility. Total
augmentation may vanish while a primitive filtered endpoint remains nonzero;
the exact two-label witness has amplitudes \(1,-1\) across the
\(p\)-free and \(p\)-divisible sectors. Therefore the aggregate zero
equation cannot transmit the common primitive regulator threshold. The
missing RH-bearing wall is a source-derived coherence lift from augmentation
nullity to the full family of labelled prime-current domains.

## High-frequency endpoint obstruction

[`the-autocorrelation-orientation-is-forced-negative-at-high-frequency.md`](the-autocorrelation-orientation-is-forced-negative-at-high-frequency.md)
closes the direct scalar orientation route. The reciprocal weight has
\(W_x(0)=0\) and \(W_x'(0)=A_f(0)>0\), so its cosine transform is
\(-A_f(0)y^{-2}+o(y^{-2})\). It is therefore negative at sufficiently high
frequency even for the actual smooth positive theta source. Any surviving
conservation law must pair the coincidence germ before scalar compression.

[`the-coincidence-obstruction-opens-an-infinite-sobolev-jet-tower.md`](the-coincidence-obstruction-opens-an-infinite-sobolev-jet-tower.md)
derives the next endpoint coefficients explicitly. After the leading weighted
source norm come the seam-value square and the first weighted derivative
energy. Repeated Fourier integration exposes the entire odd endpoint jet, so
one additional scalar port cannot complete the repair. The next gate is
whether the theta heat equation closes this Sobolev tower to a finite labelled
recurrence.

[`the-infinite-coincidence-tower-has-a-finite-two-copy-differential-grammar.md`](the-infinite-coincidence-tower-has-a-finite-two-copy-differential-grammar.md)
corrects the independence interpretation of that tower. Every autocorrelation
jet is a matrix coefficient of one scale derivative, whose weighted adjoint is
the affine reflection (1-\partial_q) plus the endpoint trace. Combined with
the earlier one-primitive-line theta closure, the infinite carrier has a
finite two-copy heat grammar. The remaining obstruction is orientation under
reciprocal sewing before scalar compression.

[`the-critical-half-offset-and-the-coincidence-defect-are-one-adjunction-law.md`](the-critical-half-offset-and-the-coincidence-defect-are-one-adjunction-law.md)
identifies the centered derivative (K=\partial_q-1/2) as skew-adjoint modulo
the coincidence trace. The half-offset is the fixed point of the affine
adjoint reflection, while the negative spectral tail is its half-line boundary
anomaly. This universal law explains both structures but cannot select theta
zeros; the remaining discriminator must use the primitive labelled heat line.

[`the-primitive-heat-coherence-cycle-is-poisson-exact.md`](the-primitive-heat-coherence-cycle-is-poisson-exact.md)
tests that final candidate and finds an exact decomposition. The reciprocal
primitive pair is the differentiated boundary of the theta-vacuum Poisson
cell, not a new sign-bearing coherence class. The centered Green/heat/Poisson
architecture is therefore closed as an RH route; a new selector must live
outside this local complex, plausibly in genuinely arithmetic multi-label
incidence.

[`the-exceptional-prime-current-adjoint-relation-is-global-not-seam-local.md`](the-exceptional-prime-current-adjoint-relation-is-global-not-seam-local.md)
tests the first completion-at-infinity successor. Reality of the source gives
\(C_k(-\overline z)=\overline{C_k(z)}\) wherever the exceptional prime sums
converge, throughout the plane rather than only on the seam. The regularity
jump still detects every scalar zero, but separate primitive and square
adjoint limits cannot locate it. A new candidate must be a genuinely
mixed-depth extension class.

[`the-mixed-primitive-square-extension-is-an-exact-valuation-filtration-step.md`](the-mixed-primitive-square-extension-is-an-exact-valuation-filtration-step.md)
tests that mixed-depth successor. Primitive exclusion plus valuation degree is
exactly the square-and-higher current after removing the universal identity
line. The resulting positive operator has the entire squarefree sector as its
kernel. Hence the exceptional depths form one exact cyclic filtration, not a
new zero-orienting extension. The surviving global frontier is the nonlinear
theta-boundary versus Euler-determinant comparison.

## Source-bordered endpoint determinant

[`the-completed-xi-section-is-a-source-bordered-endpoint-determinant.md`](the-completed-xi-section-is-a-source-bordered-endpoint-determinant.md)
couples the reciprocal-symmetric theta bulk to the exact two-endpoint dilation
complex through its symmetric coaugmentation and antisymmetric incidence
lines. The resulting three-dimensional analytic family has determinant
`2 xi(s)` and is unique up to reciprocal port rescaling. Reciprocal symmetry
acts by distinct left and right comparison maps, not similarity. This earns a
source-derived zero-to-kernel bridge while leaving open-sector invertibility
entirely unproved.

[`the-bordered-xi-family-is-seam-symmetric-by-congruence-not-self-adjointness.md`](the-bordered-xi-family-is-seam-symmetric-by-congruence-not-self-adjointness.md)
shows that boundary incidence and symmetric--antisymmetric endpoint coordinates
turn the bordered family into a complex-symmetric form, and a constant phase
congruence makes it real symmetric on the critical line. However, the only
constant Hermitian metric making the original family self-adjoint along the
whole seam is zero. The finite bridge is intrinsically bilinear and two-sided;
any Hilbert--Pólya continuation must enlarge the bulk rather than reinterpret
the compressed three-dimensional family.

[`the-theta-bulk-has-a-conservative-dilation-realization-but-conservation-does-not-confine-zeros.md`](the-theta-bulk-has-a-conservative-dilation-realization-but-conservation-does-not-confine-zeros.md)
enlarges the theta bulk to the vacuum coefficient of the self-adjoint
multiplication generator \(A(q)=q\sigma_x\). Its centered transport is
unitary on the critical line, explaining the seam real form before scalar
compression. A one-atom positive spectral measure in the same conservative
architecture nevertheless gives a real off-seam determinant zero. Positivity,
unitarity, reciprocal doubling, and the endpoint border therefore do not
confine zeros without an additional theta-specific source law.

[`the-completed-circle-flow-has-exactly-one-short-range-monotonicity-defect.md`](the-completed-circle-flow-has-exactly-one-short-range-monotonicity-defect.md)
finds that the positive completed circle family is not Loewner-monotone.
Exactly the \(n=1\) winding mode increases on
\(1\le t<(15+\sqrt{105})/(8\pi)\); every higher mode decreases throughout
the completed chart. Modular evenness forces their scalar derivatives to
cancel exactly at \(t=1\). The theta-specific survivor is therefore one
primitive monotonicity defect repaired by the full higher-winding tail, not a
generic monotone positive operator.

[`scc-classifies-the-completed-circle-repair-as-static-coherence-with-dynamic-law-unselected.md`](scc-classifies-the-completed-circle-repair-as-static-coherence-with-dynamic-law-unselected.md)
registers the one-defect flow with Aspect's SCC. Packet, stratum, labelled
ports, and static modular coherence pass. The positive even hostile
\(q^2e^{-q^2}\) preserves the seam value and zero first derivative but has
positive off-seam derivative residual \(3/4\) at \(q=1/2\), so dynamic
variation coherence remains unselected. SCC's abstract higher-jet suggestion
is retained, while its finite-field wall-block realization is rejected for
failure of the coefficient-stratum pullback.

[`no-finite-seam-jet-selects-the-global-completed-flow.md`](no-finite-seam-jet-selects-the-global-completed-flow.md)
closes every finite-germ repair at once. For any declared jet order \(m\),
the positive even Schwartz hostile
\(q^{2K}e^{-q^2}\), with \(K=\lfloor m/2\rfloor+1\), vanishes through
that entire seam jet but increases on \(0<q<\sqrt K\). No finite seam
profile plus positivity, evenness, and decay selects global monotonicity. The
next admissible constructor must close the entire theta jet tower or act
globally on the labelled heat source.

## Reciprocal bivector normalization

[`reciprocal-bivector-doubling-has-a-normalization-anomaly.md`](reciprocal-bivector-doubling-has-a-normalization-anomaly.md)
shows that equalizing reciprocal-sheet bivector states transports the forcing
coefficient. The resulting exact anomaly balances the apparent seam term, so
state normalization alone has no zero-confinement force.

[`the-reciprocal-normalization-anomaly-is-not-a-finite-jet-boundary-current.md`](the-reciprocal-normalization-anomaly-is-not-a-finite-jet-boundary-current.md)
proves that no uniform finite-jet differential constructor turns the anomaly
into a boundary current. The required multiplier is inverse scale, so a repair
must be an independently sourced global homotopy rather than a renamed
antiderivative.

[`the-two-endpoint-homotopies-differ-by-a-seam-period.md`](the-two-endpoint-homotopies-differ-by-a-seam-period.md)
shows that the zero-normalized and infinity-normalized inverse-scale
homotopies differ by the total seam period. A positive anomaly forces that
period to be nonzero, refining the minimal architecture from `3+2+1` to
`3+2+2+1`.

[`the-seam-period-requires-a-pair-label-comparison-channel.md`](the-seam-period-requires-a-pair-label-comparison-channel.md)
shows that identical one-label periods can produce distinct aggregate seam
periods. The final comparison tower must therefore retain the off-diagonal
pair-label Gram current.

[`the-tail-seam-synthesis-supplies-the-pair-current-but-not-its-orientation.md`](the-tail-seam-synthesis-supplies-the-pair-current-but-not-its-orientation.md)

pairwise observability but remains invariant under prime transport, so the
relative Haar orientation is still a separate missing rung.

[`the-relative-haar-norm-ratio-canonically-produces-the-half-density-seam.md`](the-relative-haar-norm-ratio-canonically-produces-the-half-density-seam.md)

raw dilation in additive- and multiplicative-Haar sectors. The ratio is `p`,
and Mellin transport makes the additive channel isometric exactly at real
part one half.

[`the-half-density-conjugates-the-scale-differential-to-the-critical-centered-operator.md`](the-half-density-conjugates-the-scale-differential-to-the-critical-centered-operator.md)
constructs the operator-level mate: half-density conjugation sends
`partial_q+s` to `partial_q+s-1/2` while transporting the seam-period metric by
the same modular factor. The critical coefficient and relationship metric are
two faces of one source operation.

[`the-half-density-centers-the-bulk-but-is-transparent-to-the-bivector-endpoint.md`](the-half-density-centers-the-bulk-but-is-transparent-to-the-bivector-endpoint.md)

identity with nonzero bivector endpoint. Since the half-density equals one at
the finite seam, it cannot close the boundary packet by itself.

[`the-augmentation-bivector-is-the-canonical-complete-graph-seam-coboundary.md`](the-augmentation-bivector-is-the-canonical-complete-graph-seam-coboundary.md)

coboundary. Its Laplacian reconstructs every scalar-null endpoint, closing the

[`prime-transport-is-a-chain-map-for-the-complete-graph-seam-incidence.md`](prime-transport-is-a-chain-map-for-the-complete-graph-seam-incidence.md)

and commuting prime iterations preserve the incidence exactly. The remaining

[`fourier-poisson-sewing-rotates-the-augmentation-line-into-the-control-port.md`](fourier-poisson-sewing-rotates-the-augmentation-line-into-the-control-port.md)

zero-frequency control line. The exterior seam current remains natural only
after retyping its anchor, making the third state tower indispensable.

[`the-second-fourier-quarter-turn-reconstructs-but-does-not-cancel-the-seam-defect.md`](the-second-fourier-quarter-turn-reconstructs-but-does-not-cancel-the-seam-defect.md)

augmentation boundary. This closes the three-tower Fourier coherence but

[`two-independent-obstructions-forbid-pre-sewing-collapse-of-the-reciprocal-sector-packets.md`](two-independent-obstructions-forbid-pre-sewing-collapse-of-the-reciprocal-sector-packets.md)

retain equal nonzero bivector energies, while their opposite boundary

[`the-relative-mapping-cone-green-lemma-forces-the-centered-real-part-to-vanish.md`](the-relative-mapping-cone-green-lemma-forces-the-centered-real-part-to-vanish.md)

[`unitary-sewing-transports-the-entire-finite-green-packet-not-three-separate-invariants.md`](unitary-sewing-transports-the-entire-finite-green-packet-not-three-separate-invariants.md)

automatically preserves energy, forcing pairing, and exterior endpoint norm.

[`the-primitive-current-is-not-a-fourier-stable-subchannel.md`](the-primitive-current-is-not-a-fourier-stable-subchannel.md)

equality is therefore the wrong completion target.

[`the-finite-full-boundary-packet-closes-as-a-fourier-stable-pro-gram-extension.md`](the-finite-full-boundary-packet-closes-as-a-fourier-stable-pro-gram-extension.md)

## Cubic root transport

[`theta-cubic-root-transport.md`](theta-cubic-root-transport.md) identifies
`L_t=coth(theta_t)` as the eccentricity of a reciprocal real-root pair. It
also proves ordinary common interlacing too weak: cubic hyperbolicity retains
the oriented critical values of the cubic primitive and the lower-moment
integration constant invisible to its derivative quadratic.

[`theta-cubic-lobe-balancing-transport.md`](theta-cubic-lobe-balancing-transport.md)

lobe condition to the exact companion inequality
`|35 Z_2 Z_5^2 - 81 Z_4^3 + 27 Z_4 K_4| <= 6 K_4^(3/2)`.
It identifies the next faithful object as a completed three-label kernel.

[`theta-cubic-six-copy-certificate.md`](theta-cubic-six-copy-certificate.md)
polarizes the residual into a symmetric six-label kernel and proves
`R_34 = 12 Gamma(9/2)^4 Z_5^2 disc(J_2^(3))`. Thus the six-copy residual is
the cubic discriminant with a positive source factor, not yet an independent
positivity mechanism.

## Cubic Poisson--Hermite energy

[`theta-cubic-poisson-hermite-energy.md`](theta-cubic-poisson-hermite-energy.md)
writes the exact Hermite matrix of the exceptional cubic. Its first two
principal minors are already controlled, while the unique final Schur
complement is `27 R_34/(32 Z_5^4 K_4)`. The Gaussian carrier is the rank-one
triple-root boundary matrix.

## Primitive-scale susceptibility

[`theta-cubic-primitive-scale-susceptibility.md`](theta-cubic-primitive-scale-susceptibility.md)
computes the exact response to a second reciprocal scale orbit:
`R'(0)=-112 r^2(10r^3-27r^2+5)`. It proves that sufficiently separated
Poisson sectors point out of the cubic cone and identifies the theta heat
equation as the next non-additive discriminator to test.

## Two-sheet interference

[`theta-two-sheet-interference-hermite-biehler.md`](theta-two-sheet-interference-hermite-biehler.md)
formalizes the reciprocal planes as `X=F+F#`. RH would follow from the
Hermite--Biehler amplitude orientation `|F(-z)|>|F(z)|` in the upper
half-plane. Its exact two-copy kernel isolates the remaining oscillatory
cosine transform and shows why symmetry alone cannot confine cancellations.

## Dilation spectral gap

[`theta-interference-dilation-spectral-gap.md`](theta-interference-dilation-spectral-gap.md)
shows that the Gaussian-sharp weighted source inequality is exactly the
Mellin bandwidth bound `int |h'|^2 >= 2 int |h|^2` for
`h(log u)=-u Phi'(u)`. Gaussian scales saturate the frequency floor.

## Hard core

The completed theta source is fixed before decomposition:

\[
 \Phi(u)=\sum_{n\ge1}\phi_n(u),
 \qquad V(u)=-\log\Phi(u).
\]

Arithmetic labels, modular reflection, and scalar transforms are coordinate
presentations of this source. Positivity must be derived from the completed
source and may not be inserted through fitted measures, zero data, or a
source-changing positive realization.

## Proved frontier

The first infinite Jensen layer is complete. The source satisfies

\[
 -u(\log\Phi)''(u)+(\log\Phi)'(u)>0\qquad(u>0).
\]

Therefore the Gaussian-normalized even-moment sequence is strictly
log-concave, and every quadratic Jensen polynomial is hyperbolic with the
required negative-root orientation.

    self-adjointness, not mere symmetry, is the load-bearing theorem forcing
    the critical line.
60. [Dirac linearization with infinite boundary](theta-dirac-linearization-infinite-boundary-target.md)
    — proves that reciprocal doubling forces the first-order bulk
    H0=-i*sigma3*d/du with H0^2+1/4=A0; its Green form has the required

    modes.
61. [Dirichlet ingress Dirac-flux cocycle](theta-dirichlet-ingress-dirac-flux-cocycle.md)
    — computes each reciprocal integral-label jump as a Dirac-isotropic
    spinor whose polarized flux is
    2i*sin((conj(w)-z)log N)/N; division by the de Branges denominator yields
    the positive diagonal weight log(N)/N, and multiplicative composition is

62. [Connected prime-flux normalization gate](theta-connected-prime-flux-normalization-gate.md)
    — catches the decisive weight mismatch: raw reciprocal Dirichlet Gram

63. [Connected Dirac current equals the Weil gate](theta-connected-dirac-current-equals-weil-gate.md)
    — shows that endpoint, gamma, and correctly normalized connected prime

64. [Prime-shift Laplacian dilation gate](theta-prime-shift-laplacian-dilation-gate.md)

65. [Adelic Lagrangian incidence RH target](theta-adelic-lagrangian-incidence-rh-target.md)

67. [Prime-transport Lakatos closeout](theta-rh-prime-transport-lakatos-closeout.md)

68. [Metaplectic common analytic core gate](theta-metaplectic-common-analytic-core-gate.md)

69. [Adelic lattice tangent collapse](theta-adelic-lattice-tangent-collapse.md)

70. [Adelic Weyl comb rigidity](theta-adelic-weyl-comb-rigidity.md)

71. [Idele dilation carrier is not the RH operator](theta-idele-dilation-carrier-not-rh-operator.md)

72. [Overlap projective-incidence no-go](theta-overlap-projective-incidence-no-go.md)

73. [Rational-scale dense-seam no-go](theta-rational-scale-dense-seam-no-go.md)

74. [Idele seams separated before real projection](theta-idele-seams-separated-before-real-projection.md)

75. [Product-formula linear cocycle and quadratic energy](theta-product-formula-linear-cocycle-quadratic-energy.md)

76. [Euler Fisher metric selection gate](theta-euler-fisher-metric-selection-gate.md)

77. [Completed Fisher metric axis closure](theta-completed-fisher-metric-axis-closure.md)

78. [Kähler quarter-turn and projective zeros](theta-kahler-quarter-turn-projective-zero.md)

79. [Labelled Kähler collapse and affine connection](theta-labelled-kahler-diagonal-collapse-affine-connection.md)

80. [Moving-seam projection cocycle is non-Fredholm](theta-moving-seam-projection-cocycle-nonfredholm.md)
    — identifies the seam current exactly as the cocycle difference between a

81. [Heat-sandwiched seam trace-class gate](theta-heat-sandwiched-seam-trace-class-regulator-gate.md)

82. [Heat seam interpolates to rank one](theta-heat-seam-interpolates-nonfredholm-to-rank-one.md)

83. [Reciprocal seam determinant ratio is trivial](theta-reciprocal-seam-determinant-ratio-trivial.md)

84. [Mixed-sector angle Gram and vacuum-minor gap](theta-mixed-sector-angle-gram-vacuum-minor-gap.md)

85. [Character orbit forces infinite Schur dressing](theta-character-orbit-forces-infinite-schur-dressing.md)
    — proves that the orbit of any nonzero continuum-supported theta state

86. [Unitary vacuum defect determinant theorem](theta-unitary-vacuum-defect-determinant-theorem.md)
    — proves the universal coupled identity det(D*D)=|<Omega,U Omega>|^2 for
    the compression D of any unitary U to the vacuum complement. On real
    theta character transport, every critical-axis zero is therefore exactly

87. [Holomorphic J-unitary continuation no-go](theta-holomorphic-j-unitary-continuation-no-go.md)
    — proves that a holomorphic family preserving one fixed nondegenerate

88. [Schur-zero no-go and Hermite--Biehler correction](theta-schur-zero-no-go-hermite-biehler-correction.md)

89. [Derivative companion is defect velocity](theta-derivative-companion-is-defect-velocity.md)
    — derives the canonical companion from the logarithmic character

90. [Defect velocity two-copy sinh--cosine form](theta-defect-velocity-two-copy-sinh-cosine-form.md)
    — expands the oriented defect current exactly as a positive

91. [Product--ratio parity cone and half-density](theta-product-ratio-parity-cone-half-density.md)

92. [Prime parity cone resums to a Poisson kernel](theta-prime-parity-cone-resums-to-poisson-kernel.md)

93. [Positive Poisson products cannot converge normally to the xi divisor](theta-positive-poisson-products-cannot-converge-to-xi-divisor.md)
    — applies the normal-family zero-free limit obstruction to show that

101. [Adelic vacuum gives a canonical nuclear integrality bridge](theta-adelic-vacuum-convolution-canonical-nuclear-bridge.md)
    — constructs the periodized convolution of the standard self-dual adelic

102. [Adelic bridge factors through the arithmetic phase circle](theta-adelic-bridge-factors-through-arithmetic-circle.md)

103. [Reciprocal circles are the two sectors; half-planes are their Mellin shadows](theta-reciprocal-circle-correspondence-halfplanes-are-mellin-shadows.md)
    — shows that modular inversion exchanges reciprocal circle metrics rather

104. [Circle angle--winding commutator has one canonical seam channel](theta-circle-angle-winding-commutator-seam.md)
    — derives the rigged commutator `[N,Q]=-iI+i|delta><delta|`. Heat

105. [Mellin seam produces an Epstein coherence matrix](theta-mellin-seam-produces-epstein-coherence-matrix.md)
    — Mellin-transforms the full rank-one seam family before taking its norm.
    The heat bulk gives diagonal zeta weights, while the seam gives the full
    matrix `(m^2+n^2)^(-s/2)`. Their diagonals agree exactly; scalar trace
    compression erases all off-diagonal relative-winding data. The resulting
    zero-diagonal coherence is oriented rather than positive and is the next

106. [Epstein coherence reveals a Gaussian-integer sector](theta-epstein-coherence-reveals-gaussian-integer-sector.md)
    — identifies the two-copy norm with the Gaussian-integer norm. Full

    `4 zeta(s/2) beta(s/2)`, not Riemann zeta alone. The 90-degree intuition is
    therefore exact but enlarges the arithmetic object; an authorized source

107. [Completion differential selects a positive circle operator](theta-completion-differential-selects-positive-circle-operator.md)
    — derives the classical Riemann Fourier kernel as the trace of
    `B_t=pi t^(5/4) A^(1/2)(2 pi t A-3I)A^(1/2)exp(-pi t A)`. Integrality,
    zero-mode removal, and modular restriction to `t>=1` give the exact gap
    `2 pi t A-3I >= (2 pi-3)I`. This canonically selects the Riemann trace from
    the seam jet, while leaving variation across scale as the RH obstruction.
108. [Completed circle kernel is strictly sign-regular of order two](theta-completed-circle-kernel-is-strictly-sign-regular-order-two.md)
    — proves symbolically that `b(t,y)/b(t,x)` strictly decreases with scale
    whenever `y>x>=1`. Every ordered `2x2` scale--energy minor therefore has
    the same negative sign, or positive sign after reversing energy order.
    The completion polynomial reinforces the heat orientation; the first live
    falsifier is an oriented third-order minor.
109. [Completed circle kernel is strictly sign-regular of order three](theta-completed-circle-kernel-is-strictly-sign-regular-order-three.md)
    — factors the third Wronskian into its Vandermonde and a symmetric cubic
    `P`. On the physical domain `lambda_j t>=pi>3`, `P` is coordinatewise
    increasing and exceeds `P(3,3,3)=57`. The completed spectral family is
    therefore an extended Chebyshev system through order three; the first live
    falsifier moves to the general Wronskian at order four.
110. [Completed-circle total positivity fails first at order seven](theta-completed-circle-total-positivity-fails-first-at-order-seven.md)
    — derives the all-order Wronskian residual
    `P_r=sum (-1)^(r-j) 2^j (2r-2j+1)!! e_j` and the recursion
    `partial_i P_r=2P_(r-1)`. Exact induction proves positivity through order
    six. At order seven, `P_7(pi,...,pi)<P_7(22/7,...,22/7)<0` while the value
    at `7/2` is `66304`, forcing a physical sign reversal. Ordinary total
    positivity is therefore closed for the continuous relaxation. Its claim of
    a physical spectral failure is superseded by packet 111.
111. [Order-seven continuous falsifier is excluded by the integral spectrum](theta-order-seven-continuous-falsifier-is-excluded-by-integral-spectrum.md)
    — corrects the physical typing: seven distinct energies are squares and
    cannot cluster near the continuous crossing. Coordinate monotonicity and
    the exact bound at `3*(1^2,...,7^2)`, equal to `3218949258543`, prove
    faithful sign regularity through order seven. The live conjecture is the
    all-order discrete boundary sequence `Q_r=P_r(pi*1^2,...,pi*r^2)>0`.
112. [Integral square separation proves all-order sign regularity](theta-integral-square-separation-proves-all-order-sign-regularity.md)
    — proves the discrete conjecture by the exact mode-adjunction recurrence
    `P_r=(2y_r-(2r+1))P_(r-1)+4 sum_i y_i P_(r-2)(Y\i)`. Distinct winding
    labels force `y_r>=pi r^2`, so every coefficient and deletion minor is
    positive inductively. The labelled completed-circle kernel is sign-regular
    at every finite order; the remaining gate is preservation under scalar
    summation over winding labels.
113. [Integral-circle Gram kernel is strictly totally positive](theta-integral-circle-gram-kernel-is-strictly-totally-positive.md)
    — composes the labelled sign-regular kernel with its transpose. Infinite
    Cauchy--Binet expresses every Gram minor as a convergent sum of products of
    two identically oriented labelled minors, hence every minor is strictly
    positive. This is an all-order coupled positivity theorem; it also proves
    why the one-sided scalar theta sum remains a distinct, RH-bearing gate.
114. [Seam jet restores linear moment-tower orientation](theta-seam-jet-restores-linear-moment-tower-orientation.md)
    — observes that the canonical ports `A^k delta` have coefficients

    seam jet with the all-order labelled kernel, proving strict sign regularity
    of every finite linear moment tower. Scalar compression forgets the
    certificate because it retains only the zeroth port; modular transport to
    the Xi Laguerre tower remains unproved.
115. [Scale-derivative closure has one primitive heat channel](theta-scale-derivative-closure-has-one-primitive-heat-channel.md)
    — corrects the temptation to identify seam moments directly with scalar
    derivatives. Writing raw moments `H_k`, differentiation closes exactly on
    `H_1` plus the completed moments `M_k`; no further primitive channels
    appear. The apparent infinite defect tower is repeated presentation of one
