116. [Modular reflection fixes the primitive heat line](theta-modular-reflection-fixes-the-primitive-heat-line.md)
    — differentiates the theta functional equation to obtain
    `H_1(t)+t^(-5/2)H_1(1/t)=t^(-3/2)Theta(1/t)/(4pi)`. At the self-dual seam,
    `H_1(1)=Theta(1)/(8pi)`. The sole primitive derivative channel is therefore
    sewn to a positive source-fixed boundary value, not an adjustable repair;
    positivity of the resulting complex Schur complement remains open.
117. [Finite seam Schur complement cannot control the global Laguerre form](theta-finite-seam-schur-complement-cannot-control-global-laguerre-form.md)
    — gives an even positive deformation preserving any prescribed finite seam
    jet while changing the Fourier transform. Local modular closure therefore
    cannot determine global Laguerre orientation. The live object is the full
    transported two-copy source in sum--difference coordinates, now informed
    by the all-order integral sign-regularity theorem.
118. [Two-copy label pairs have canonical hyperbolic centers](theta-two-copy-label-pairs-have-canonical-hyperbolic-centers.md)
    — at fixed sum scale, centers each `(n,m)` pair at
    `D=log(m/n)`, turning its energy into `2nm exp(S) cosh(delta)`. The full
    completion product is even in `delta`. The positive-chart constraint
    `|D|<=S` truncates the centered interval asymmetrically, so label swap does
    not cancel the odd displacement locally. The entire residual is thereby
    localized to moving modular endpoints before the global `S` phase.
119. [Reciprocal quadrants sew by a sum--difference quarter-turn](theta-reciprocal-quadrants-sew-by-sum-difference-quarter-turn.md)
    — decomposes the full even two-copy source into four sign quadrants. The
    sewn bracket is exactly `D^2 cos(xS)+S^2 cos(xD)`: reciprocal sewing swaps
    sum and difference roles. It reduces to `4(B^2+AC)`, isolating the positive
    cross-quadrant square and one signed polarization. Modular geometry closes
    the endpoint bookkeeping but does not by itself prove its sign.
120. [First Laguerre form is phase-circle angular velocity](theta-first-laguerre-form-is-phase-circle-angular-velocity.md)
    — sets `gamma=(C,B)=(C,-C')` and proves
    `L_1[X]=4 det(gamma,gamma')`. Radial normalization places the value--flux
    portrait on `S^1`, where Laguerre positivity is precisely monotone angular
    transport. A scalar zero is a transverse coordinate crossing, not loss of
    the full state; higher Laguerre forms should encode the osculating jet flag.
121. [Higher Laguerre forms are even jet self-transvectants](theta-higher-laguerre-forms-are-even-jet-self-transvectants.md)
    — refines the tentative osculating-flag language: `(2n)! L_n` is the
    canonical even self-transvectant of the order-`2n` derivative jet, i.e. the
    invariant bilinear form on a symmetric power of the value--flux plane. Its
    two-copy source is `(u-v)^(2n)` against the common sum-coordinate phase.
122. [Theta transvectant is bilinear, not Hilbert-positive](theta-transvectant-is-bilinear-not-hilbert-positive.md)
    — realizes every Laguerre form as
    `<Omega^2,Delta^(2n) exp(ix Sigma) Omega^2>`. Although `Delta^(2n)` is
    positive, the commuting character makes this a matrix coefficient rather
    than a norm. Equivalently it is a complex-bilinear self-pairing; Hilbert
    conjugation erases the spectral phase. The missing RH datum is therefore a
    source-derived definite real/Krein polarization, not bare self-adjointness.
123. [Laguerre hierarchy is a reflection-Krein causal cone](theta-laguerre-hierarchy-is-a-reflection-krein-causal-cone.md)
    — finds the canonical polarization: reciprocal reflection `R` gives
    `(2n)!L_n=<J_n Delta^n Psi_x,Delta^n Psi_x>` with `J_n=(-1)^nR`. Equivalently
    each form is the cosine-channel norm minus the sine-channel norm. The RH
    hierarchy becomes prohibition of parity-cone crossing by every transported
    source jet; definiteness, not polarization existence, is the live theorem.
124. [Krein transport is conserved parity-weight transfer](theta-krein-transport-is-conserved-parity-weight-transfer.md)
    — uses `[Delta,Sigma]=0` to prove constant Hilbert norm of every transported
    jet. Its Laguerre reserve is `1-2p_minus`, where `p_minus` is the literal
    sine-parity probability. Cone crossing is precisely majority inversion;
    the missing arithmetic theorem must keep every source spectral measure's
    sine weight at or below one half.
125. [Krein reserve must vanish at high spectral frequency](theta-krein-reserve-must-vanish-at-high-spectral-frequency.md)
    — pushes the positive separation-weighted source to an absolutely
    continuous sum-coordinate probability measure. Riemann--Lebesgue forces
    every normalized Laguerre reserve to zero and both parity probabilities to
    one half at high frequency. Any proof must establish no crossing without a
    uniform coercivity gap.
126. [DPC: modular sewing supplies a source-derived parity spectral factor](theta-modular-parity-spectral-factor-dpc.md)
    — freezes the no-gap mechanism: each separation-weighted sum density should
    be an autocorrelation `h_n*tilde(h_n)` of an amplitude derived from one
    reciprocal chart and its modular adjoint. This would yield Laguerre forms
    as modulus squares while allowing decay to zero. Post-hoc spectral square
    roots are explicitly prohibited; order one is the first hostile gate.
127. [Naive separation amplitude is not a spectral factor](theta-naive-separation-amplitude-is-not-a-spectral-factor.md)
    — distinguishes the pointwise fiber norm
    `g_n(S)=||h_n(S,.)||^2` from an autocorrelation: Fourier transformation of
    the former is not the norm square of the transformed amplitude. The
    corrected DPC requires a fixed Hilbert module and a source-derived
    translation representation identifying moving sum-scale fibers.
128. [Dilation module fails at arithmetic diagonal sampling](theta-dilation-module-fails-at-arithmetic-diagonal-sampling.md)
    — constructs the continuous radial dilation carrier after hyperbolic
    centering, then shows that theta uses evaluation at `q=nm exp(S)`, not Haar
    overlap of transported sections. The missing operation is a Mackey-like
    pull--push correspondence retaining multiplicities of `(n,m)->nm`; the
    first hostile fiber is the multiple factorization of `6`.
129. [Product fiber six has an even blind channel](theta-product-fiber-six-has-an-even-blind-channel.md)
    — derives `q_!q^*=d(k)I` for the product correspondence and runs the hostile
    `k=6` test. Swap-even aggregation still kills `(1,-1,-1,1)`, which
    distinguishes centers `log 6` and `log(3/2)`. Product plus parity is not
    faithful; the ratio magnitude is a mandatory second arithmetic port.
130. [Product--ratio coordinate is faithful with a C2 Mackey norm](theta-product-ratio-coordinate-is-faithful-with-c2-mackey-norm.md)
    — proves `(nm,|log(m/n)|)` uniquely recovers the unordered pair. The swap
    pull--push norm is exactly two off the diagonal and one on it, retaining the
    stabilizer rather than imposing a false uniform factor. Common dilation
    acts only on radial product scale while the hyperbolic ratio port remains
    fixed; analytic evaluation-to-overlap remains the live gate.
131. [Radial sampling becomes a product character under Mellin rotation](theta-radial-sampling-becomes-product-character-under-mellin.md)
    — solves the moving-evaluation step exactly:
    `Fourier_S[F_rho(p exp S)]=p^(-ix) Mellin[F_rho](x)`. Since `p=nm`, the
    arithmetic phase tensor-factorizes bilinearly. A Hilbert Gram square instead
    carries the ratio character `(n/m)^(-ix)`. The remaining obstruction is an
    exact reciprocal product--ratio quarter-turn on the faithful two-port fiber
    at six, before any Gram-positivity claim is admissible.
132. [Product--ratio quarter-turn exits the integral cone](theta-product-ratio-quarter-turn-exits-the-integral-cone.md)
    — computes the requested intertwiner exactly: exchanging logarithmic
    product and ratio sends `(n,m)` to `(n^(-1),m)`. It therefore exits
    `N^2` on three branches of the product-six fiber. The internal Gram route
    is ill-typed; the correct object is a correspondence between integral and
    inverse-integral valuation cones inside the rational adelic label space.
133. [Two valuation cones have an exact relative seam norm](theta-two-valuation-cones-have-an-exact-relative-seam-norm.md)
    — conditionally constructs the doubled one-prime space. The cones
    `ell2(Z_>=0)` and `ell2(Z_<=0)` glue along one fixed valuation with exact
    norm `||f||^2=||f_+||^2+||f_-||^2-|f_0|^2`. This is the canonical rank-one
    relative repair, but the local Tate functional equation must still
    authorize the negative cone; additive Fourier self-duality alone does not.
134. [Local Tate gamma is unitary valuation-cone sewing on the critical line](theta-local-tate-gamma-is-unitary-valuation-cone-sewing-on-critical-line.md)
    — expands the Fourier-fixed local vacuum in the `s` and `1-s` valuation
    charts and derives `gamma_p=(1-p^(-s))/(1-p^(s-1))`. On `Re(s)=1/2`, paired
    coefficients are conjugate with norm `p^(-k/2)` and the transition is
    unimodular. This source-authorizes the opposite cone and explains the half
    offset, while leaving global restricted-product implementability open.
135. [Local Tate seam has a canonical first-cumulant renormalization](theta-local-tate-seam-has-a-canonical-first-cumulant-renormalization.md)
    — responds to Nima's global divergence by expanding
    `log gamma_p=2i sum_(k>=1) p^(-k/2)sin(kt log p)/k`. The entire nonsquare-
    summable part is the primitive `k=1` current. Subtracting it leaves local
    deviations `O(1/p)` and a convergent squared prime sum, yielding a
    projectively phased implementer candidate plus an explicit primitive phase
    defect.
136. [Prime-Fock plethystic logarithm splits off a distributional primitive current](theta-prime-fock-plethystic-logarithm-splits-distributional-primitive-current.md)
    — proves `gamma_p=a_p(-theta)/a_p(theta)` for the geometric Fock vacuum.
    Its plethystic logarithm identifies the subtracted term as the connected
    one-particle prime character. Globally that current has norm square
    `sum_p 1/p` and belongs only to the dual rigging, whereas the `k>=2` tail is
    Hilbert-implementable. The current must be retained in a relative
    determinant line, not discarded.
137. [Prime-Fock seam has an exact Schatten-three filtration](theta-prime-fock-has-an-exact-schatten-three-filtration.md)
    — separates three canonical summability types: the primitive `k=1`
    current is distributional, the prime-square `k=2` current is Hilbert but
    non-trace-class, and the `k>=3` connected tail is trace-class. One
    subtraction yields only a projective implementer candidate; absolute
    scalar convergence first appears after the two subtractions of an
    order-three regularized determinant. The finite-cutoff countercurrents
    remain part of the completed object.
138. [Tate seam is a lossless three-stage anomaly resolver](theta-tate-seam-is-a-lossless-three-stage-anomaly-resolver.md)
    — turns the operator's typed-resolver analogy into an exact reconstruction
    object `(C1,C2,gamma^[3])`. The three fields live respectively in a
    distributional, Hilbert/non-trace-class, and trace-class stratum. This
    reframes archimedean completion as a candidate anomaly-inflow boundary map
    that must absorb both residual currents without changing any finite-cutoff
    seam. Even successful anomaly cancellation would construct the determinant
    line, not yet prove its RH orientation.
139. [Off-seam puncture DPC is canonical acyclicity, not line triviality](theta-off-seam-puncture-dpc-is-canonical-acyclicity-not-line-triviality.md)
    — incorporates the operator's puncture-impossibility intuition and Nima's
    trivial-line counterexample. A zero is retyped as cohomology of a
    source-derived Fredholm/Koszul complex. The proposed symbolic theorem is
    an explicit contracting homotopy on each open half-plane; geometric
    algebra pairs every would-be puncture state into an exact pair. Canonical
    section rigidity and the hostile symmetric-multiplier lift are mandatory
    gates. No complex or contraction is yet claimed.
140. [An RH puncture can only enter through completion at infinity](theta-rh-puncture-can-only-enter-through-completion-at-infinity.md)
    — incorporates Nima's vanishing-differential limit falsifier. Finite
    Clifford/Koszul pairing can lose a partner at infinite norm, so the actual
    theorem is preservation of strict exactness under restricted-product
    completion. The `k=1,2` Tate currents are reinterpreted as candidate typed
    boundary partners preventing such escape. The packet separates bounded-
    inverse, closed-range, and derived-limit obstructions until the completed
    source category is explicitly constructed.
141. [Theta Ubersector should live in a nuclear rigged exact category](theta-ubersector-should-live-in-a-nuclear-rigged-exact-category.md)
    — proposes the Schwartz--Bruhat rigging as the completion category in
    which source-split exactness may survive restricted tensor formation. The
    primitive, square, and trace-class strata occupy dual, Hilbert, and
    nuclear/readout levels. The hostile vanishing-differential tower is
    rejected by failure of equicontinuity. Exactness is conditional on an
    explicitly typed restricted-product functor, strict morphisms, and
    source-derived continuous contractions; nuclearity alone proves nothing.
142. [Theta zero is first a pairing null, not yet a cohomology puncture](theta-zero-is-first-a-pairing-null-not-yet-a-cohomology-puncture.md)
    — the two-by-three rigging exposes a prior typing gate: the completed theta
    readout is source-derived as a matrix coefficient, so its zero first means
    destructive interference between nonzero perspectives, not singular
    transport or cohomology. The full geometric product retains a bivector
    channel when its scalar channel vanishes. A source orientation theorem for
    that Clifford packet is now logically prior to the puncture complex unless
    an independent determinant bridge is constructed.
143. [Local Tate comparator is not an off-seam acute semigroup](theta-local-tate-comparator-is-not-an-off-seam-acute-semigroup.md)
    — exactly falsifies the primewise acute-cone shortcut. Direct minimization
    of the scalar numerator gives `1/2-(r_++r_-)^2/4` at prime two. It
    vanishes on the seam and is negative for every nonzero displacement inside
    the critical strip, so local scalar projections can be negative off-seam.
    Any surviving acute Clifford structure must be created by full
    prime--archimedean reciprocal sewing, not asserted factor by factor.
144. [Theta tail flow gives a source-derived rank-two boundary system](theta-tail-flow-gives-a-source-derived-rank-two-boundary-system.md)
    — supplies the first forward-derived operator candidate after the
    Deutsch--Sommerfeld brief. The tail transform obeys an inhomogeneous
    first-order flow; adjoining its constant source channel makes a homogeneous
    triangular rank-two system. A scalar transform zero becomes a nonzero
    two-ended boundary solution. Its one-sided Green identity retains one
    indefinite forcing cross-term, so the decisive calculation is the exact
    doubled Fourier--Tate system with all three boundary-current levels.
145. [Doubled theta-tail flow isolates one modular forcing current](theta-doubled-tail-flow-isolates-one-modular-forcing-current.md)
    — incorporates the free constant channel and two-endpoint domain, then
    pairs the centered parameters `z` and `-conjugate(z)`. Exact differentiation
    yields `2 Re(z) N = -dJ-2F`, where `N` is the sum of tail norms and `F` is
    one explicit difference of source-forcing pairings. RH would follow
    conditionally if complete Fourier--Tate sewing turns `2F` into the
    derivative of the retained `k=1`, `k=2`, connected, and archimedean
    boundary current. Packet 146 corrects the initial overly strong demand
    that every non-derivative bulk term be absent.
146. [Modular forcing may renormalize the bulk norm](theta-modular-forcing-may-renormalize-the-bulk-norm.md)
    — corrects packet 145 using the already proved mixed-sheet reflection
    coboundary `X=(z+conjugate(w))R`. On the diagonal, a legitimate forcing
    component is `2 Re(z) M` and belongs in a completed bulk energy rather
    than an invented antiderivative. The finite residual is now
    `2F-dJ_boundary-2 Re(z)M`. Exact labelled decomposition and positivity of
    the resulting completed energy are separate gates; the latter is the same
    curvature obstruction previously found, not independent evidence.
147. [Theta trace plus spin two is a universal quadrature square](theta-trace-plus-spin-two-is-a-universal-quadrature-square.md)
    — proves the exact coupled theorem `|psi|^2 +/- Re(psi^2)=2(Re/Im psi)^2`.
    Hence a unit-normalized reflection-curvature correction to the invariant
    Gram trace is automatically positive; for coefficient `lambda`, universal
    positivity holds exactly when `|lambda|<=1`; at equality the form is rank
    one, not definite. The live gates are the source-fixed coefficient and
    exclusion or repair of its null quadrature. The older de Branges `x/y`
    factor is not automatically covered.
148. [Reciprocal theta sheets may supply complementary quadrature squares](theta-reciprocal-sheets-may-supply-complementary-quadrature-squares.md)
    — formulates the sharp closure: Fourier--Tate exchange should preserve the
    invariant trace, reverse the spin-two component, and retain a common
    transported frame. Then the two rank-one sheet energies sum exactly to a
    strict full norm. The finite certificate is `Q_X^+ + Q_X^- = 2 c_X I`
    with positive cutoff-stable `c_X`; frame mismatch, prime-two residuals, or
    degeneration under completion falsify the mechanism.
149. [Opposite Maslov eighth phases give complementary theta quadrature frames](theta-opposite-maslov-eighth-phases-give-complementary-quadrature-frames.md)
    — connects the independently derived oriented phases `exp(+/- i pi/4)`
    to the complementary-sheet certificate. Their relative amplitude angle is
    `pi/2`, so the associated rank-one projectors sum exactly to the identity;
    equivalently their spin-two components are negatives. The missing gate is
    a source comparison from the archimedean half-form frame to the labelled
    doubled-tail quadrature frame, including all prime and boundary currents.
150. [Maslov phase does not yet act on the theta-tail quadrature](theta-maslov-phase-does-not-yet-act-on-the-tail-quadrature.md)
    — audits the comparison gate and blocks immediate phase transfer. In the
    theta matrix coefficient, self-dual normalization makes both Gaussian
    source and arithmetic comb Fourier-fixed; the quarter-turn reverses
    dilation but supplies no `+/- pi/4` scalar-frame phase. The eighth phase
    belongs to the oscillator determinant/half-form level. Complementarity
    must first be derived directly in the theta frame; a later determinant
    functor could transport the Maslov phase, but none is yet constructed.
151. [Clark-sheet swap natively reverses theta spin two](theta-clark-sheet-swap-natively-reverses-spin-two.md)
    — derives the native comparison without a determinant phase. Clark
    orientation reversal swaps `P_a` and `Q_a`, preserving symmetric trace
    terms and reversing the exact Wronskian `W_a=2ia(FF''-(F')^2)` before
    diagonal restriction. Thus complementary sheet forms reduce to equality
    of their source-fixed trace and spin coefficients plus exclusion of extra
    bulk representation types. The sign mechanism itself is exact.
152. [Clark-sheet bulks sum to an exact faithful Gram trace](theta-clark-sheet-bulks-sum-to-an-exact-faithful-gram-trace.md)
    — resolves the remaining native finite-bulk gates by the parallelogram
    identity `|G+f+ia G_z|^2+|G+f-ia G_z|^2 = 2|G+f|^2+2a^2|G_z|^2`.
    Equal coefficients, spin-two cancellation, absence of a third quadratic
    bulk, and faithfulness on the two-feature packet are exact labelwise
    theorems. The unresolved RH content now lies only in primitive, square,
    archimedean, mixed-seam, and completion defect channels.
153. [Primitive theta forcing norm is a canonical reservoir current](theta-primitive-forcing-norm-is-a-canonical-reservoir-current.md)
    — closes the first named non-bulk defect without a spectral antiderivative.
    The fixed source reservoir `J_force(q)=2 integral_q^infinity |f|^2` has
    derivative `-2|f|^2` and retains the primitive norm as its endpoint charge.
    It is defined before any zero and is cutoff-functorial. This continuous
    forcing channel remains typed separately from the arithmetic `k=1` prime
    current; Jordan, seam, prime-square, and archimedean defects remain live.
154. [Theta seam cannot be a bounded function of the retained tail](theta-seam-cannot-be-a-bounded-function-of-the-retained-tail.md)
    — reaches the first genuine operator obstruction. For translated source
    features, the retained-tail norm tends to zero while the seam norm tends
    to the full positive source norm. Hence no bounded operator can reconstruct
    `h_p` from `g_p` on the unbounded arithmetic translation orbit. The seam
    is infinite-rank and must remain an independent state component or enter
    through an unbounded closed relation/full block determinant. The bare
    spectral seam wave also lacks an automatic packet-Hilbert domain.
155. [Theta tail--seam closability is an exact adjoint-range problem](theta-tail-seam-closability-is-an-exact-adjoint-range-problem.md)
    — reduces the surviving graph presentation to a sharp source theorem. If
    `G` is the Hankel tail synthesis and `H` the seam synthesis, then
    `Gamma_0=H G^(-1)` is closable exactly when
    `{y:H^*y in Ran(G)}` is dense, with `H^*` the explicit forward Volterra
    convolution. The diverging orbit norm ratio proves unboundedness but not
    nonclosability because seam features escape spatially. No current theta
    range theorem resolves this density gate.
156. [Full tail--seam Gram is faithful but not closed-range on unrestricted packets](theta-full-tail-seam-gram-is-faithful-but-not-closed-range-on-unrestricted-packets.md)
    — audits the preferred direct-sum architecture. The seam exactly repairs
    translation escape and the autocorrelation Gram is faithful under the
    analytic source hypotheses. But high-frequency modulations have fixed
    coefficient norm and vanishing full energy because the source Fourier
    multiplier decays. Thus the synthesis has nonclosed range on unrestricted
    `L2` packets. Completion stability is meaningless until the physical
    arithmetic coefficient completion is derived from source constructors.
157. [Mellin covariance is the theta coefficient-topology gate](theta-mellin-covariance-is-the-coefficient-topology-gate.md)
    — distinguishes fixed-state faithfulness from an unnecessary uniform
    height bound. In the source-Gram norm, a Mellin modulation is bounded
    exactly when the shifted weight ratio
    `|Phi_hat(xi+N)|^2/|Phi_hat(xi)|^2` is essentially bounded. In a discrete
    source-weighted label module, `n^(iN)` is instead exactly unitary. The live
    theorem is to derive the arithmetic coefficient completion and prove
    Mellin covariance, cutoff convergence, and injective full synthesis.
158. [Logarithmic label accumulation forbids a diagonal Riesz norm](theta-logarithmic-label-accumulation-forbids-a-diagonal-riesz-norm.md)
    — computes the discrete full tail--seam kernel
    `K(n,m)=A(|log(n/m)|)` and gives a two-label no-go. Single atoms have fixed
    positive norm, while adjacent differences tend to zero because
    `log(n+1)-log n -> 0`; hence no positive diagonal weight supports both
    frame bounds, even after scalar source rescaling. Continuous boundary rows
    cannot repair this. A genuinely label-discrete authorized port or a
    non-diagonal/quotient coefficient topology is necessary.
159. [Fixed-prime divisibility does not descend to the analytic Gram quotient](theta-fixed-prime-divisibility-does-not-descend-to-the-analytic-gram-quotient.md)
    — resolves the topology census using an authorized constructor. For
    `x_k=e_(pk+1)-e_(pk)`, the analytic Gram norm tends to zero, while the
    fixed-prime projector sends it to `-e_(pk)` of constant positive norm.
    Thus divisibility and valuation operations are discontinuous on the pure
    analytic quotient. A mixed continuous Gram state plus discrete
    valuation/Fock port is source-required, not merely optional.
160. [Constructor-generated pro-Gram topology for theta labels](theta-constructor-generated-pro-gram-topology.md)
    — replaces a guessed coefficient norm by the seminorm family
    `q_C(c)=||U(Cc)||` generated by all authorized valuation, Mellin, Fock,
    and boundary constructors. Composition makes constructors continuous,
    and finite valuation cylinders isolate every atom in a finite packet, so
    the topology is source-derived and faithful on `c_00`. The live obstacle
    is whether its completion remains Hausdorff and supports the full Green
    identity without invisible boundary states.
161. [Theta endpoint readout is not continuous in the full tail--seam Gram](theta-endpoint-readout-is-not-continuous-in-the-full-tail-seam-gram.md)
    — gives an exact Fourier witness: the bulk norm controls
    `y=chat*conjugate(Phihat)` in `L2`, whereas the endpoint `G_c(0)` is the
    unbounded functional `integral y`. Unit bulk packets can therefore have
    endpoint magnitude tending to infinity. The scalar transform must remain
    an explicit source-derived boundary port; valuation cylinders alone do
    not make the zero boundary condition completion-stable.
162. [Theta Clark derivative is the sharp endpoint-trace repair criterion](theta-clark-derivative-is-the-sharp-endpoint-trace-repair.md)
    — connects the endpoint obstruction to the already source-derived
    `a^2|partial_z G|^2` bulk channel. If its exact Fourier form weights
    `y=chat*conjugate(Phihat)` by
    `w_a=1+a^2|Phihat'/Phihat|^2`, endpoint continuity is equivalent to the
    sharp scalar condition `integral 1/w_a < infinity`. Thus the Clark channel
    may simultaneously orient the bulk and prevent loss of the zero boundary
    condition; the remaining gate is the exact labelled multiplier audit and
    theta logarithmic-derivative asymptotics.
163. [Theta endpoint repair must use the scale-flow graph norm](theta-endpoint-repair-must-use-the-scale-flow-graph-norm.md)
    — corrects packet 162's variable conflation. The actual `partial_z G`
    inserts the relative tail coordinate and is not yet a coefficient-space
    logarithmic-derivative multiplier. Endpoint continuity instead follows
    canonically from the `q`-flow `H1` graph norm. Since
    `G+f=-partial_q G+(1-s)G`, the native positive bulk is already a
    first-order graph energy; the live gate is whether the completed doubled
    energy controls the full graph norm and hence the boundary trace.
164. [Theta native first-order bulk exactly controls the endpoint](theta-native-first-order-bulk-exactly-controls-the-endpoint.md)
    — closes that graph-norm gate. With `1-s=a+ib` and
    `G=e^(ibq)h`, the source square obeys the exact identity
    `||G+f||^2=||h'||^2+a^2||h||^2+a|h(0)|^2`. Hence throughout the critical
    strip, where `a=1-Re(s)>0`, the native bulk controls the scalar endpoint
    uniformly in spectral height. The surviving RH burden is the typed global
    boundary-current closure, not endpoint continuity.
165. [Theta first-order tail channel has no completion escape](theta-first-order-tail-channel-has-no-completion-escape.md)
    — upgrades endpoint control to a closed-range theorem. For
    `A_s=-partial_q+(1-s)` on the decaying half-line,
    `||A_sG|| >= (1-Re(s))||G||` throughout the critical strip, with explicit
    inverse and locally uniform bounds. Cutoff-Cauchy bulk images therefore
    have convergent tail partners and endpoint traces. Any remaining escape
    must live in the inter-sector modular/boundary coupling, not inside either
    oriented first-order tail channel.
166. [Theta zeros reduce to unit eigenvalues of the seam return map](theta-zeros-reduce-to-unit-eigenvalues-of-the-seam-return-map.md)
    — uses the proved invertibility of both one-sided tail operators to Schur
    reduce the completed two-sector kernel to
    `R_s=A_-^{-1} C A_+^{-1} B`. A zero is therefore a nonzero interface state
    surviving one full source route with eigenvalue one; it cannot originate
    inside either sector. RH becomes exclusion of unit return off the unitary
    Fourier--Tate seam. The real construction gap is now explicit: derive the
    completed seam incidence maps `B,C` from labelled modular sewing.
167. [Theta three-stage seam--reservoir--Green repair compiler](theta-three-stage-seam-reservoir-green-repair-compiler.md)
    — supplies Strominger's smallest source-derived compiler subsystem with a
    typing correction: use seam retention `S`, the continuous forcing
    reservoir `R_f`, and doubled Clark--Green operation `D`; the arithmetic
    primitive map `P` is not yet constructed. Exactly `S<D`, while `S` and
    `R_f` commute with identical domains, graph norms, boundary deltas, and
    capabilities. The two authorized orders `S R_f D` and `R_f S D` have
    zero typed braid residual. Extension to arithmetic `P,Q` is blocked on
    their missing boundary incidence maps.
168. [Theta primitive and square incidence are atomic scale currents](theta-primitive-and-square-incidence-are-atomic-scale-currents.md)
    — constructs the previously missing finite-cutoff maps:
    `I_k(p,k)=p^(-k/2) delta_(k log p)/k`, with `P=I_1` and `Q=I_2`; odd
    character evaluation recovers the exact connected Tate countercurrents.
    The completion types differ sharply: `Q` is a tempered positive measure,
    while `P` grows as `e^(q/2)/q` and is not a Schwartz distribution. The
    primitive port therefore requires an exponential Mellin/Laplace rigging.
    The next nontrivial compiler braid is primitive incidence versus
    archimedean completion.
169. [A fixed archimedean current cannot absorb the primitive theta prime port](theta-fixed-archimedean-current-cannot-absorb-the-primitive-prime-port.md)
    — falsifies the naive `P` then fixed-`A` anomaly cancellation. PNT partial
    summation gives
    `sum_(p<=X) p^(-1/2+it) ~ X^(1/2+it)/((1/2+it)log X)`, so the primitive
    odd current has an unbounded `sqrt(X)/log X` envelope along subsequences.
    Any cutoff-independent gamma/archimedean term leaves it intact. `P` and
    fixed `A` may commute with zero braid residual while completion still
    fails; the legitimate operation must be joint, cutoff-covariant, or an
    inseparable Poisson/explicit-formula transform.
170. [Theta Tate-current grades unify before completion as one log-Euler measure](theta-tate-current-grades-unify-before-completion-as-one-log-euler-measure.md)
    — identifies the joint source object
    `mu_log=sum_(p,k) p^(-k/2) delta_(k log p)/k`, equivalently the
    `Lambda(n)/(log n sqrt(n))` prime-power measure. Its Laplace transform is
    `log zeta(1/2+z)` only in the Euler half-plane, and spectral
    differentiation gives the von Mangoldt current. A global scalar log would
    import the unknown divisor, so `P,Q,k>=3` must reassemble into a
    determinant-line object before archimedean/modular continuation. The next
    gate is construction of that correspondence without using `xi` or its
    zeros.
171. [The global Tate integral is the legal joint theta completion operation](theta-global-tate-integral-is-the-legal-joint-completion-operation.md)
    — supplies the noncircular replacement for scalar log continuation. The
    adelic zeta integral of the standard Schwartz--Bruhat source factorizes
    into gamma and Euler factors only in the convergence chamber, while
    Poisson reciprocal splitting continues the whole section without choosing
    a logarithm around its divisor. `P,Q,k>=3` are typed provenance of that
    Euler chart, not independently continuable scalar currents. The remaining
    gap is an operator-valued lift from global Tate sewing to the seam
    incidence maps `B,C`.
172. [Theta ideles are a null boundary for additive Hilbert completion](theta-ideles-are-a-null-boundary-for-additive-hilbert-completion.md)
    — finds the exact obstruction to lifting scalar Tate sewing into a naive
    Fourier block operator. Under additive Haar measure, an adele is a unit at
    almost every prime with probability `product_p(1-1/p)=0`; hence the ideles
    form an additive-null locus. Restriction from additive `L2(A)` to
    multiplicative boundary states is not defined on equivalence classes, so
    `B,C` cannot be obtained by conjugating Fourier through a bounded trace.
    Seam data must be retained in a source-level rigged trace correspondence
    before Hilbert completion.
173. [Theta local trace restricted product exists but misses the primitive anomaly](theta-local-trace-restricted-product-exists-but-misses-the-primitive-anomaly.md)
    — constructs the explicit local targets. At finite `p`, `T_p` consists of
    locally constant multiplicative functions vanishing at sufficiently
    negative valuation and becoming uniformly constant at sufficiently
    positive valuation; the constant is the retained value at zero. Together
    with the matching-jet archimedean trace, these form a Fourier-stable
    restricted tensor product with unramified vector `1_(Z_p)|_(Q_p^x)`.
    Every finite subsystem passes, but the primitive cutoff coordinate diverges
    on the infinite unramified net, so it is not a continuous scalar
    functional. The full target must be an anomaly torsor/determinant-line
    chart over this trace space, not an ordinary strengthened Hilbert space.
174. [Theta primitive anomaly completes as a unitary direct-limit line](theta-primitive-anomaly-completes-as-a-unitary-direct-limit-line.md)
    — constructs that relative value type explicitly. Each finite cutoff has
    a boundary line `L_X`, with bonding map
    `U_XY=product_(X<p<=Y) gamma_p`. On the critical seam these maps are
    unitary and telescope exactly, so the Hilbert direct limit exists even
    when its coordinates in one fixed scalar trivialization diverge. The
    primitive and square currents are transition cocycles, not failed scalar
    values. The next gate is the off-seam two-metric/closability audit and
    coupling of this line to the distinguished Tate section.
175. [Theta off-seam anomaly lines require two inequivalent metrics](theta-off-seam-anomaly-lines-require-two-inequivalent-metrics.md)
    — proves a fixed-metric no-go on the nonoscillatory displacement
    `s=1/2+delta`. The local Tate ratios have a prime product diverging to
    infinity for `delta>0` and, by reciprocal reflection, to zero for
    `delta<0`. No cutoff family of metrics uniformly equivalent to the seam
    trivialization can control both orientations. Each side instead admits
    its own transported isometric metric, and these two metric polarizations
    coincide exactly on the unitary seam. The next gate is an
    archimedean-normalized relative pairing whose Green boundary current is
    cutoff invariant.
176. [Theta reciprocal anomaly lines have an exact cutoff-invariant pairing](theta-reciprocal-anomaly-lines-have-an-exact-cutoff-invariant-pairing.md)
    — uses the exact finite-place identity
    `gamma_p(1-conj(s))=1/conj(gamma_p(s))` to construct a sesquilinear
    pairing between the two cutoff-line systems that is preserved by every
    bonding map. Reciprocal norm escape therefore preserves the full
    relationship exactly. The pairing is perfect and nowhere zero on
    transported nonzero vectors, so it is a transition unit rather than the
    Riemann-zero section. The divisor must come from an additional
    source-derived incidence state. Its failure to commute with cutoff
    transport is measured by an explicit typed anomaly.
177. [Theta zero-bearing incidence is additive Poisson sewing, not Tate transition](theta-zero-bearing-incidence-is-additive-poisson-sewing-not-tate-transition.md)
    — separates the invertible multiplicative transition system from the
    divisor-bearing operation. The standard global Tate split expresses the
    completed section as two reciprocal outer-chamber integrals plus two
    polar boundary currents. Thus local gamma transitions protect the value
    type, while additive Poisson sewing forms the scalar section that may
    cancel. The next exact residual is the failure of the four-channel
    incidence map to descend through finite-cutoff Tate transport.
178. [Theta nontrivial divisor is created only at restricted-product completion](theta-nontrivial-divisor-is-created-only-at-restricted-product-completion.md)
    — closes the finite tensor descent: adjoining one unramified prime is
    multiplication by the invertible zero-free local Tate factor, so the
    correctly typed incidence residual vanishes exactly. Finite Euler and
    gamma products have no nontrivial divisor. Hence Riemann zeros cannot be
    finite-stage kernels. Completion-stable injectivity is necessary but, as
    the identity-map orthogonality witness shows, does not prevent the
    distinguished scalar pairing from vanishing. The frontier therefore has
    two gates: locally uniform no escape for the operator correspondence and
    source-derived detector transversality, unless a separate
    determinant--kernel theorem identifies them.
179. [Theta tail and seam have one common source trace](theta-tail-and-seam-have-one-common-source-trace.md)
    — instantiates Kitaev's projective incidence gate before arithmetic
    aggregation. The canonical split atoms satisfy `g_q(0)=h_q(0)=Phi(q)`
    and have opposite normal derivatives. Therefore the tail and seam
    endpoint rows are the same labelled trace, their kernels agree, and the
    two-cutoff projective slope residual vanishes identically. The two
    purported incidence maps reduce to one common seam trace followed by one
    still-missing arithmetic aggregation map into the anomaly line. Detector
    transversality remains independent.
180. [Theta anomaly-line incidence closes but rank-one detection is unfaithful](theta-anomaly-line-incidence-closes-but-rank-one-detection-is-unfaithful.md)
    — constructs the missing arithmetic aggregation by sending the common
    seam trace to the transported cutoff vacuum line. Its coherence square
    and quotient-surjectivity gate close exactly. But the detector is
    necessarily unfaithful: two nonzero labelled atoms already admit an
    explicit coefficient combination with zero endpoint trace. Thus the
    rank-one anomaly line is the correct scalar readout but cannot identify a
    scalar zero with a full source kernel. The minimal replacement is the
    complete Cauchy-jet detector or seam germ, whose finite and completed
    faithfulness are the next gates.
181. [Theta finite translate germs are source-faithful](theta-finite-translate-germs-are-source-faithful.md)
    — proves finite faithfulness of the enlarged detector. The `n=1` theta
    atom gives a super-exponential triangular asymptotic: among finitely many
    distinct positive shifts, the least shift dominates all others. Hence no
    nontrivial finite linear combination of theta translates has zero seam
    germ. Equivalently the full Cauchy-jet tower is injective, and some finite
    jet truncation suffices for each fixed finite packet. Uniform completion
    stability and a source-authorized germ norm remain open.
182. [Theta full-germ detector is not uniformly faithful on discrete labels](theta-full-germ-detector-is-not-uniformly-faithful-on-discrete-labels.md)
    — supplies the adjacent-label completion falsifier. The nonzero packets
    `e_(n+1)-e_n` have fixed discrete arithmetic norm, while their full theta
    germs tend to zero in every compact-open smooth seminorm because
    `log(n+1)-log(n)->0` and theta derivatives decay super-exponentially.
    Hence finite germ faithfulness has nonclosed range against a discrete
    label completion. A faithful carrier must retain both analytic germ and
    exact arithmetic type, and any determinant--kernel bridge must couple the
    latter into the source operator rather than leave it as a silent register.
183. [Theta Mellin orbit couples arithmetic type but point readout remains unfaithful](theta-mellin-orbit-couples-arithmetic-type-but-point-readout-remains-unfaithful.md)
    — constructs the canonical active coupling with label generator
    `Q e_n=(log n)e_n` and detector
    `K_c(t,z)=sum_n c_n n^(iz) Phi(t+log n)`. Distinct Mellin frequencies make
    the full orbit faithful on every finite packet. Yet evaluation at one
    spectral point remains unfaithful, with an explicit two-label
    cancellation. Therefore enlarging to a faithful orbit cannot prove RH;
    the remaining theorem must constrain the distinguished positive-Fock
    state and Poisson detector pair. Adjacent labels require unbounded
    spectral reach, restating the completion obstruction dynamically.
184. [Theta finite Fock state variety avoids the scalar detector kernel](theta-finite-fock-state-variety-avoids-the-scalar-detector-kernel.md)
    — corrects the domain of the detector question. Arbitrary sparse
    coefficient packets can lie in the scalar kernel, but the distinguished
    finite-prime Euler state is a nonlinear multiplicative rank-one Fock
    tensor. Its detector factorizes into finitely many nonzero local Euler
    factors, so it never meets the kernel away from local poles. Sparse
    two-label cancellations violate multiplicative/occupation closure and are
    tangent witnesses, not admissible source states. The RH target is now
    disjointness of the completed Fock-state variety from the off-seam
    detector divisor.
185. [Theta normalized Fock vacuum completes exactly off the critical seam](theta-normalized-fock-vacuum-completes-exactly-off-the-critical-seam.md)
    — applies the exact incomplete-infinite-tensor criterion to normalized
    local occupation states. Their vacuum overlap is
    `sqrt(1-p^(-2 Re(s)))`, so the direct global Fock state exists exactly
    when `sum_p p^(-2 Re(s))` converges, namely `Re(s)>1/2`; the reciprocal
    dual state exists exactly for `Re(s)<1/2`. The seam is the representation
    threshold. This closes completion-stable existence of the distinguished
    state in both open sectors, while leaving its source-derived Poisson
    detector transversality as the sole unresolved gate.
186. [Theta Fock state completes before its Euler detector](theta-fock-state-completes-before-its-euler-detector.md)
    — types the remaining gate. The local Euler coefficient-sum covector has
    coordinates `(1,1,...)` and is distributional, not Hilbert-bounded. On the
    normalized local Fock state its amplitude is
    `sqrt(1-p^(-2 sigma))/(1-p^(-s))`. The global state completes for
    `sigma>1/2`, whereas the ordinary detector product is absolutely defined
    only for `sigma>1`. Poisson/Tate sewing therefore extends the detector,
    not the state, into the RH half-sector. Constructing that source-derived
    rigged covector and proving its transversality is the remaining gate.
187. [Theta no common Mellin Hilbert level pairs state and Euler detector in the critical strip](theta-no-common-mellin-hilbert-level-pairs-state-and-euler-detector-in-the-critical-strip.md)
    — makes the detector obstruction exact on the Hilbert scale generated by
    `Q e_n=(log n)e_n`. The Euler detector is continuous only for weight
    `m>1/2`, while the normalized Fock state belongs only for
    `m<Re(s)-1/2`. A common level exists exactly for `Re(s)>1`, never in the
    critical half-strip. Therefore Poisson continuation there must be a
    genuinely relative, boundary-bearing pairing between two regularity
    sectors, not ordinary Riesz duality on one weighted Hilbert space.
188. [Theta heat finite part constructs the relative Euler detector](theta-heat-finite-part-constructs-the-relative-euler-detector.md)
    — constructs the missing relative pairing with the source Gaussian
    regulator `exp(-pi epsilon N^2)`. The regulated Euler sum has one explicit
    divergent heat-boundary term, and its Hadamard finite part is exactly
    `zeta(s)`; source-fixed archimedean factors give the completed readout.
    Detector construction therefore closes without ordinary Riesz duality.
    The remaining RH content is orientation/nonvanishing of this finite part:
    positive regulated bulk does not prevent cancellation against its typed
    boundary current.
189. [Theta relative Euler finite part is regulator-universal](theta-relative-euler-finite-part-is-regulator-universal.md)
    — proves that the relative detector is not a Gaussian convention. For any
    smooth rapidly decreasing regulator with `rho(0)=1`, Mellin inversion has
    a regulator-dependent boundary pole at `(1-s)/2` but a universal
    zero-pole residue `zeta(s)`. After subtracting the typed boundary current,
    every admissible regulator has the same finite part. This gives
    canonical-section rigidity under smoothing-chart changes while leaving
    orientation of the universal finite part unresolved.
190. [Theta finite-part extraction is not positive](theta-finite-part-extraction-is-not-positive.md)
    — supplies the exact obstruction behind that unresolved orientation.
    Eventual positivity of a regulated bulk does not constrain its constant
    finite part: `epsilon^{-a}+c` is positive at every sufficiently small
    scale for arbitrary real `c`. Therefore heat positivity plus regulator
    universality cannot imply RH orientation. A surviving proof must carry a
    source-derived order or conservation law on the coupled bulk--boundary
    pair, rather than order only the bulk before subtraction.
191. [Theta boundary-quotient positive cone is not pointed](theta-boundary-quotient-positive-cone-is-not-pointed.md)
    — strengthens the finite-part no-go categorically. If the boundary
    subspace contains a positive divergent germ `b`, then both `b+c` and
    `b-c` are eventually positive for every constant `c`, while their quotient
    classes are `c` and `-c`. Thus the cone inherited on the boundary quotient
    is non-pointed on the entire detector line. The missing RH orientation
    must be extra relational structure on the coupled bulk--boundary object,
    not the quotient of ordinary regulated positivity.
192. [Theta Deutsch constructor class must reject divisor multiplication](theta-deutsch-constructor-class-must-reject-divisor-multiplication.md)
    — turns the remaining search into an impossible-construction test. Any
    proposed admissible class closed under symmetry-compatible entire scalar
    multiplication also admits a hostile factor inserting an off-seam zero
    quartet. Therefore the missing invariant must reject that multiplication
    through a source law visible before scalar divisor inspection. The live
    DPC is that the complete labelled theta/Tate constructor groupoid detects
    every nonconstant divisor multiplier before compression; one hostile
    source lift preserving all declared laws falsifies it.
193. [Theta hostile quartic forces a derivative-delta source](theta-hostile-quartic-forces-derivative-delta-source.md)
    — finds the first source-level discriminator demanded by packet 192.
    Polynomial multiplication of a Mellin readout is the Mellin shadow of the
    transposed Euler differential operator on its source. Applying the hostile
    quartic to a positive atomic Euler comb creates fourth-order derivatives
    of every delta mass, which cannot cancel between distinct labels. It
    therefore violates the order-zero positive-Fock source type before its
    zeros are inspected. This rejects the standard hostile multiplier but
    does not yet exclude hostile positive atomic sources with genuine
    off-seam zeros.
194. [Theta connected prime-power Dirac flux is exact at finite support](theta-connected-prime-power-dirac-flux-is-exact-at-finite-support.md)
    — performs the next source-current calculation before completion. The
    determinant-line logarithm weights each `p^k` ingress circuit by `1/k`,
    exactly cancelling the `k` from the Mellin-character derivative and
    producing the von Mangoldt coefficient `log p`. The divided diagonal
    energy at a prime is the positive quantity `log p/(p-1)`. Finite Euler
    support therefore has a canonical connected Dirac current; its all-prime
    sum diverges, locating the remaining work in typed archimedean/seam
    completion rather than prime-power normalization.
195. [Theta finite Fock detector square has a nonconvergent target cocycle](theta-finite-fock-detector-square-has-a-nonconvergent-target-cocycle.md)
    — formulates the actual finite Beck--Chevalley square on the distinguished
    Fock lines. Source inclusions tensor normalized local vacua, while target
    transport multiplies by the nonzero Euler amplitude cocycle. Every finite
    detector is injective, but the target cocycle has no ordinary convergent
    limit in the critical half-strip. Inverse-detector trivialization is
    tautological and erases divisor data. Strict exactness is therefore not
    yet false but ill-typed until a four-channel relative target with
    convergent bonding maps is constructed.
196. [Theta connected Euler completion is an L2 primitive port plus an absolute remainder](theta-connected-euler-completion-is-an-l2-primitive-port-plus-an-absolute-remainder.md)
    — constructs the smallest non-lossy arithmetic target in the right open
    sector. Primitive coordinates form a canonical prime-labelled l2 vector
    for `Re(s)>1/2`, while every connected contribution with
    `k>=2` aggregates absolutely. Scalar summation of the primitive port is
    the unique discontinuous operation; the same primitive term is exactly
    the divergent part of the connected Dirac flux. This is a downstream
    connected coordinate; Poisson sewing must still occur on the aggregate
    theta source before this logarithmic decomposition.
197. [Theta primitive L2 port is not Poisson closed](theta-primitive-l2-port-is-not-poisson-closed.md)
    — prevents a false next construction. The additive Fourier transform of
    a prime-supported distribution is a global oscillatory distribution, not
    another prime-supported vector, because the primes are not a lattice.
    Hence no source-native reciprocal sewing acts directly on the primitive
    l2 port. The forced order is aggregate theta formation, Poisson completion,
    determinant readout, then determinant-line logarithm and connected prime
    currents. The required full block must therefore be built before the
    connected transform.
198. [Theta primitive seam current leaves an explicit mixed bulk residual](theta-primitive-seam-current-leaves-an-explicit-mixed-bulk-residual.md)
    — computes the forced doubled-flow identity in Nima's exact
    stable/unstable frame. The common theta forcing couples only to the
    antisymmetric polarization in the flux law. Its canonical primitive seam
    antiderivative removes that forcing term but creates a specific mixed
    seam--bulk residual involving the symmetric and antisymmetric channels.
    Primitive seam retention alone therefore cannot close the conservation
    law. The square, Clark, or archimedean current must differentiate to the
    negative of this residual as a typed identity.
199. [Theta no finite antiderivative tower closes the mixed seam residual](theta-no-finite-antiderivative-tower-closes-the-mixed-seam-residual.md)
    — proves that the primitive residual cannot be repaired by adding finitely
    many successive seam antiderivatives. Each new local bilinear current
    cancels one level and creates the next residual with row multiplied by the
    homogeneous doubled-flow matrix. That matrix is invertible for every
    nonzero centered spectral parameter, so the recursion cannot terminate.
    The complete infinite-dimensional theta shift state, or an independently
    derived closed source dynamics, is therefore mandatory.
200. [Theta Clark sheets are the minimal quarter-turn-closed forcing port](theta-clark-sheets-are-the-minimal-quarter-turn-closed-forcing-port.md)
    — resolves the quarter-turn incidence obstruction source-natively. The
    common theta forcing lies along the symmetric sheet direction, while the
    Clark sheet-odd derivative lies along its quarter-turn. These orthogonal
    columns form the smallest invariant source port, and their Euclidean norm

    therefore supplies the missing cofactor quadrature without an imported
    Maslov phase, while infinite shift dynamics remain necessary for boundary
    propagation.
201. [Theta full tail-seam shift is an exact source isometry](theta-full-tail-seam-shift-is-an-exact-source-isometry.md)
