213. [Theta Clark jet tower is source-closed but zero-loop supply is positive](theta-clark-jet-tower-is-source-closed-but-zero-loop-supply-is-positive.md)
214. [Theta direct and dual tail systems carry opposite incidence arrows](theta-direct-and-dual-tail-systems-carry-opposite-incidence-arrows.md)
    — identifies the direct tail equation's forcing-to-endpoint arrow and
    proves that Clark differentiation supplies a static endpoint sensor, not
    the missing reverse dynamics. The adjoint/Fourier--Tate sector carries the
    opposite endpoint-to-forcing arrow. Bidirectional closure therefore
    requires an explicit sewing map; it cannot be obtained by silently
    identifying the reciprocal carriers. Pointwise tail decay also rules out
    a uniform local incidence bound, leaving an accumulated Gramian or sewn
    boundary-current identity as the next admissible target.
215. [Theta transported incidence loop is nonzero off and on the seam](theta-transported-incidence-loop-is-nonzero-off-and-on-the-seam.md)
    — inserts explicit endpoint and forcing sewing maps before composing the
    direct and dual arrows. In rank one the transported loop vanishes exactly
    when one source arrow vanishes, so every invertible Tate sewing leaves it
    nonzero both on and off the critical seam. Scalar bidirectional closure is
    therefore source-faithful but carries no RH selection law. Seam-sensitive
    information can first appear in a larger labelled forcing module, where
    the transported dual image may meet the direct incidence kernel. The
    smallest rank-two unitary hostile realizes that meeting exactly, reducing
    the live question to a source-derived principal-angle reserve.
216. [Theta principal angle is perfect before aggregation and tautological after](theta-principal-angle-is-perfect-before-aggregation-and-tautological-after.md)
    — applies the existing common-source-trace theorem to the new angle target.
    Before arithmetic aggregation, the direct and reciprocal rows coincide,
    so their normalized angle reserve is exactly one; this rejects arbitrary
    rank-two rotations but holds on and off the seam. After aggregation, angle
    failure is just scalar zero formation. Since the common trace image has
    rank one, the restricted arithmetic map is one scalar multiplier: it is
    either injective or kills the whole line. The RH burden therefore lies in
    an independent law for that multiplier, not in raw two-sector
    transversality.
217. [Theta four-channel Poisson lift has a three-dimensional readout kernel](theta-four-channel-poisson-lift-has-a-three-dimensional-readout-kernel.md)
    — constructs the genuine pre-scalar Poisson lift from two reciprocal bulk
    and two polar boundary channels. Its fixed summation readout has rank one
    and a three-dimensional kernel. Nima's exact degree-twelve Fourier-fixed,
    vacuum-null, integral-sampling-positive hostile enters that kernel at
    off-critical Mellin zeros. Therefore no current depending only on universal
    Fourier--Poisson coherence can prove RH; the surviving lift must retain and
    use theta-specific prime-power boundary-current provenance.
218. [Theta standard finite Fock data do not reject an archimedean hostile](theta-standard-finite-fock-data-do-not-reject-an-archimedean-hostile.md)
    — tensors Nima's degree-twelve Fourier-fixed real carrier with the exact
    standard unramified source at every finite place. The resulting adelic
    source retains all prime-power/Fock provenance and finite Euler currents
    while inheriting off-critical zeros from its archimedean Mellin factor.
    Finite arithmetic labels therefore do not suffice. The first exact
    discriminator is the standard Gaussian annihilation equation; the live
    target is a coupled annihilation--Euler boundary-current identity.
219. [Theta Gaussian annihilation produces only an archimedean recurrence](theta-gaussian-annihilation-produces-only-an-archimedean-recurrence.md)
    — computes the Mellin commutator of the oscillator annihilator exactly.
    For the Gaussian it is the ordinary gamma recurrence, multiplied by the
    unchanged finite Euler readout. The local identity therefore rejects
    archimedean excitations but imposes no constraint on zeta zeros. Any
    genuine continuation must use a nonseparable global operation coupling
    real scale to finite lattice labels without dividing by the zeta readout.
220. [Theta prime-dilation oscillator energy selects the seam but annihilates the source](theta-prime-dilation-oscillator-energy-selects-the-seam-but-annihilates-the-source.md)
    — combines Nima's labelled annihilator covariance with the Mellin
    character. Raw oscillator energy transforms by
    \(p^{1-2\Re s}\), making the critical line exactly its isometric locus and
    producing the logarithmic increment \((1-2\Re s)\log p\). However every
    labelled Gaussian atom is a vacuum, so the diagonal energy annihilates the
    entire physical source module. The next required capability is a faithful
    off-diagonal or relative energy between prime-related vacua.
221. [Theta finite boundary currents do not repair prime-orbit Følner escape](theta-finite-boundary-currents-do-not-repair-prime-orbit-folner-escape.md)
    — combines Nima's local-adjacency no-gap packet with the actual primitive,
    square, seam, and finite archimedean ports. On normalized long prime-power
    blocks, every fixed-range difference, fixed-grade boundary coordinate, and
    summable theta trace has energy of order \(N^{-1}\). Their finite sum has
    no completed lower bound. A viable repair must be nonlocal, nonamenable,
    topologically stronger for a source-derived reason, or globally coupled to
    an archimedean cost that grows under arithmetic escape.
222. [Theta full seam Gram anchors the positive prime Følner block](theta-full-seam-gram-anchors-the-positive-prime-folner-block.md)
    — distinguishes the decaying seam trace from the retained infinite-rank
    seam history. For normalized positive blocks on one prime orbit, all seam
    Gram entries are nonnegative and the diagonal average converges to
    \(\|\Phi\|_2^2\), so the full seam state cannot escape. Uniform control of
    phased packets is exactly the lower-frame problem for the periodized
    Fourier-power symbol \(W_p\); its sign in the doubled Green residual remains
    separate.
223. [Theta von Mangoldt weight uniformizes prime seam frame bounds](theta-von-mangoldt-weight-uniformizes-prime-seam-frame-bounds.md)
    — bounds each periodized prime-orbit symbol by its zero alias. All zero
    aliases lie in the fixed baseband \(|\xi|\le\pi/\log2\), below the first
    real zero of the completed theta readout, giving
    \(W_p\ge c_{\Phi}/\log p\). Multiplication by the primitive von Mangoldt
    weight makes the lower bound uniform over primes. The arithmetic logarithm
    is simultaneously transport generator, current weight, and seam-frame
    renormalizer; the remaining gate is its exact sign in the doubled Green
    identity.
224. [Theta faithful seam Gram and oriented oscillator occupy decoupled blocks](theta-faithful-seam-gram-and-oriented-oscillator-occupy-decoupled-blocks.md)
    — audits the required Green sign. Oscillator energy has the exact
    \(p^{1-2\Re s}\) multiplier but vanishes on all labelled Gaussian vacua;
    the weighted seam Gram is faithful but logarithmic translation lacks the
    dilation factor \(p\). The established bulk operations commute on separate
    tensor factors, so their direct sum cannot transfer orientation to the
    faithful block. The unique next target is the moving-boundary
    oscillator--seam commutator.
225. [Theta prime-shift defect is a full-rank Hankel boundary window](theta-prime-shift-defect-is-a-full-rank-hankel-boundary-window.md)
    — replaces the failed finite jet by the complete moving-cut history. The
    bilateral difference kernel is translation-covariant, but half-line
    compression leaves the full interval swept across the origin. In the
    reflected window coordinate its kernel is \(\Phi(q+u)\): a source-native
    Hankel boundary operator whose finite translate families have full rank.
    Yet its norm tends to zero on a single cut sent to infinity while the seam
    norm tends to \(\|\Phi\|_2\), so no uniform generalized frame bound exists.
    Reciprocal sewing is now the remaining source-native way to return distant
    history to the fixed seam without collapsing it to finite boundary data.
226. [Theta cumulative prime boundary windows exactly reconstruct seam energy](theta-cumulative-prime-boundary-windows-exactly-reconstruct-seam-energy.md)
    — identifies the apparent packet-225 escape as loss of causal history.
    Successive width-\(\log p\) boundary innovations tile the full seam interval,
    and their squared norms add exactly to the seam norm. The cumulative
    innovation tower is therefore an isometric, completion-stable boundary
    state. Reciprocal Mellin weighting gives every positive-depth innovation
    the common sign of (1-2\Re s\), producing an explicit positive relationship
    energy. The remaining gate is the zero-state-to-total-boundary-flux bridge.
227. [Theta scalar zero does not annihilate oriented innovation energy](theta-scalar-zero-does-not-annihilate-oriented-innovation-energy.md)
    — proves the exact bridge obstruction. Scalar Mellin vanishing is one
    linear cancellation, while the reciprocal innovation form is definite off
    the seam. Its scalar kernel therefore contains nonzero states with strictly
    signed energy, already on two channels. An independent Green, Evans, or
    canonical-system law must make scalar nullity an executable boundary
    condition on the full state.
228. [Theta half-density is the relative Haar interface but not a zero-confinement law](theta-half-density-is-the-relative-haar-interface-but-not-a-zero-confinement-law.md)
    — transports atom, measure, coefficient, and readout together. The unitary
    map (Jf=x^{1/2}f) identifies additive (L^2(dx)) with multiplicative
    (L^2(dx/x)), and the complete dilation cocycle is (a^{1/2-s}). Hence the
    critical line is exactly the relative-Haar unitary locus. On that locus the
    Mellin readout is a Fourier matrix coefficient, which can still vanish by
    orthogonality; the zero-to-boundary-state bridge remains independent.
229. [Theta relative Haar form excludes the constant zero-state channel](theta-relative-haar-form-excludes-the-constant-zero-state-channel.md)
    — separates the unitary half-density map from the closed identity inclusion.
    The latter has modular operator \(1/x\) and the exact
    \(p^{1-2\Re s}\) energy cocycle, but its domain excludes the nonzero constant
    channel required by the affine tail Evans state. Positivity and restriction
    compatibility forbid a finite positive local extension. A source-derived
    relative boundary block must couple the constant to the Haar tail.
230. [Theta boundary-control system gives an exact Evans bridge but leaves forcing bulk](theta-boundary-control-system-gives-an-exact-evans-bridge-but-leaves-forcing-bulk.md)
    — retains the affine constant as an independent scalar control mapped to
    the source (f), avoiding the Haar-domain divergence. The resulting closed
    first-order system has a nonzero two-endpoint kernel exactly when the
    Mellin transform vanishes, giving the missing noncircular Evans bridge.
    Its doubled Green identity leaves one explicit mixed-sheet source--tail
    forcing difference; converting that term into typed modular boundary flux
    is now the singular RH-bearing gate.
231. [Theta Evans forcing residual is the oscillatory separation transform](theta-evans-forcing-residual-is-the-oscillatory-separation-transform.md)
    — expands the final forcing difference as the cosine--hyperbolic transform
    of the positive two-source separation measure. The hyperbolic factor has
    the correct half-plane sign, but the cosine oscillates. Evans, curvature,
    and mixed-sheet separation are therefore three presentations of the same
    obstruction. The remaining theorem must use structure special to the
    modularly completed theta separation measure.
232. [Theta Evans residual is a forward versus backward Poisson comparison](theta-evans-residual-is-a-forward-versus-backward-poisson-comparison.md)
    — applies Wiener--Khinchin to the separation autocorrelation. Its forward
    Poisson transform is universally nonnegative, while the Evans residual is
    twice the difference between the forward value and its backward analytic
    continuation. This is exactly the earlier vertical-modulus obstruction.
    The missing source theorem is a theta-specific orientation of backward
    Poisson evolution.
233. [Theta global backward-Poisson orientation is forbidden by mass conservation](theta-global-backward-poisson-orientation-is-forbidden-by-mass-conservation.md)
    — corrects packet 232's overly global target. Forward and backward Poisson
    continuations have the same total spectral mass, so their integrable
    difference has zero integral and cannot have a nontrivial fixed pointwise
    sign. Orientation must be sought only on the source-derived zero-state
    submanifold after reciprocal backreaction is installed.
234. [Theta reciprocal adjoint completion changes the Evans zero equation](theta-reciprocal-adjoint-completion-changes-the-evans-zero-equation.md)
    — extracts the finite labelled incidence column (B_+c=cf_X) and its
    required adjoint row (B_+^*G=\langle f_X,G\rangle). The one-way system's
    adjoint residual is exactly \(-B_+^*\). Adding the return row restores block
    symmetry but introduces a new scalar Schur equation that is not the endpoint
    Evans condition. The missing constructor is a source-derived scalar block
    proving Schur--Evans determinant agreement.
235. [Theta history adjoint is causal reflection, not one Fourier quarter-turn](theta-history-adjoint-is-causal-reflection-not-one-fourier-quarter-turn.md)
    — computes the full history conjugation. One Fourier turn diagonalizes the
    anti-causal convolution; causal reflection (R=\mathcal F^2\) gives
    (RH_+R=H_+^*\). On the doubled half-line sectors this is an anti-diagonal,
    variance-reversing adjoint pairing between matching infinite-rank history
    ports. The remaining gate is the source-derived diagonal block required for
    Schur--Evans determinant agreement.
236. [Theta history correlation has essential spectrum touching one](theta-history-correlation-has-essential-spectrum-touching-one.md)
    — diagonalizes the bilateral correlation as multiplication by
    \(|m(\xi)|^2\). After normalization the essential spectrum reaches (1) at
    zero frequency, generally without an eigenvector there. Hence the raw
    history completion defect is a low-frequency essential-spectrum mode, not
    a finite Fredholm eigenstate, and no compact correction can open a uniform
    gap.
237. [Theta history Dirac is bounded and cannot carry the Riemann ordinates](theta-history-dirac-is-bounded-and-cannot-carry-the-riemann-ordinates.md)
    — applies Young's inequality to the canonical conormal Dirac. Its spectrum
    is bounded by \(\|\Phi\|_1\), whereas the Riemann ordinates are unbounded.
    The history Dirac therefore encodes reciprocal incidence and sheet
    character, not spectral height. Any Hilbert--Pólya realization must add a
    source-derived unbounded translation, dilation, or differential generator.
238. [Theta translation generator plus history Dirac has continuous, not Riemann, spectrum](theta-translation-generator-plus-history-dirac-has-continuous-not-riemann-spectrum.md)
    — adds the canonical logarithmic momentum (K=-i\partial_q\). Since the
    history Dirac is convolutional, the sum diagonalizes into the continuous
    branches \(\xi\pm|m(\xi)|\). It supplies unbounded scale but no confinement
    or quantization. A viable spectral operator needs a source-derived
    nontranslation-invariant potential, boundary condition, or adelic quotient.
239. [Theta no local L2 metric identifies source forcing with endpoint readout](theta-no-local-l2-metric-identifies-source-forcing-with-endpoint-readout.md)
    — computes the feedback-port residual. In a weighted local (L^2) metric,
    equality of source adjoint and endpoint trace would require
    (w\overline f=\delta_0\), impossible for an ordinary weight and smooth theta
    source. A Sobolev repair is authorized only if an independently derived
    graph operator has (f) as its boundary Green kernel. Interior forcing and
    endpoint observation must remain separately typed until then.
240. [Theta transform is a two-port cross-transfer, not yet a Weyl determinant](theta-transform-is-a-two-port-cross-transfer-not-yet-a-weyl-determinant.md)
    — realizes the scalar source transform as the cross resolvent entry between
    the boundary distribution and theta-source port of a self-adjoint
    logarithmic transport operator. The full two-port Weyl determinant also
    contains both diagonal self-energies and the reciprocal cross entry. A
    transmission zero is therefore not yet an eigenvalue or determinant zero;
    a source-derived exteriorization identity is the next gate.
241. [Theta cross-transfer has a bordered determinant, not a self-adjoint pencil](theta-cross-transfer-has-a-bordered-determinant-not-a-self-adjoint-pencil.md)
    — gives the exact finite-cutoff Schur-complement exteriorization
    \(\det L=-\det(A-zI)F\). It preserves the cross-transfer divisor, but the
    noncollocated source and endpoint borders prevent a self-adjoint
    holomorphic pencil. Chiral doubling restores pointwise self-adjointness
    only by introducing \(\bar z\), while the full Weyl determinant acquires
    Herglotz geometry by changing the divisor. The remaining gate is a
    source-derived port identification or divisor-preserving self-adjoint
    linearization.
242. [Self-adjoint linearization is equivalent to real-rootedness unless source-derived](theta-self-adjoint-linearization-is-equivalent-to-real-rootedness-unless-source-derived.md)
    — proves at finite cutoff that a manufactured self-adjoint matrix has the
    bordered cross-transfer divisor exactly when that divisor is already
    real-rooted. Existence of such a matrix is therefore circular unless its
    operator and metric precede the scalar divisor. Positive port colocation
    gives a genuine Herglotz mechanism, but the native theta source and
    endpoint ports are distinct. The live gate is an independently derived
    theta/Tate metric or boundary correspondence that collocates them.
243. [Translation-commutant metric cannot collocate theta source and endpoint](theta-translation-commutant-metric-cannot-collocate-source-and-endpoint.md)
    — classifies positive carrier-preserving metrics by spectral blocks. For
    logarithmic translation such a metric is a Fourier multiplier, and
    collocation of the endpoint distribution with the theta source forces its
    multiplier to equal the source Fourier transform. Source integrability
    makes that multiplier decay to zero, forbidding an equivalent positive
    Hilbert metric. The missing construction must enlarge or change the
    operator system rather than reweight the native carrier.
244. [Cayley crossing form orients full Weyl rank, not cross-transfer zeros](theta-cayley-crossing-form-orients-full-weyl-rank-not-cross-transfer-zeros.md)
    — derives the native finite two-port Hermitian Weyl path and proves its
    differentiated Cayley form is positive on every real resolvent interval.
    The resulting phase density detects zeros of the full Weyl determinant,
    not zeros of its theta cross entry. Primitive, square, archimedean, and
    pole-current matching cannot repair this lower-minor mismatch; an
    independently derived Pluecker or rank relation must come first.
245. [Spectral infinity forbids a cross-minor to full-Weyl unit](theta-spectral-infinity-forbids-cross-minor-to-full-weyl-unit.md)
    — proves that independent ports with nonzero incidence give a theta cross
    entry of order \(z^{-1}\) but a full two-port determinant of order
    \(z^{-2}\). No nowhere-zero rational unit can relate them. Dependent ports
    instead make the determinant vanish identically. The minimal faithful
    determinant promotion is exactly the one-coordinate border, which retains
    the divisor but remains non-self-adjoint.
246. [Theta bordered divisor has a canonical Krein realization](theta-bordered-divisor-has-a-canonical-krein-realization.md)
    — constructs the companion operator and coefficient-derived Hermite form
    of every finite real bordered numerator. The companion is self-adjoint in
    this canonical form; nonreal conjugate roots appear exactly as indefinite
    directions, while positivity is equivalent to real-rootedness. The live
    source theorem is a labelled theta/Tate Gram factorization of the Hermite
    forms, compatible with cutoff completion.
247. [Theta retarded--advanced product loses the inner factor](theta-retarded-advanced-product-loses-the-inner-factor.md)
    — derives the native reciprocal Hardy pair and its two-sided
    autocorrelation product. Upper-half-plane inner factors preserve that
    product and Hardy support while changing the retarded zero divisor.
    Therefore exterior delay positivity cannot recover RH-bearing phase;
    source sewing must rigidly determine or exclude the inner factor through
    labelled primitive, square, and archimedean data.
248. [Theta half-plane zero-freeness is causal cyclicity, not zero leakage](theta-half-plane-zero-freeness-is-causal-cyclicity-not-zero-leakage.md)
    — shows that the Hankel leakage block vanishes for every analytic inner
    factor and is therefore blind to half-plane zeros. The correct invariant
    is the model space \(K_U=H^2_+\ominus UH^2_+\), whose dimension counts
    finite Blaschke zeros. Trivial inner factor, vanishing model-space defect,
    and cyclicity of the theta tail under causal shifts are equivalent under
    the declared Hardy hypotheses.
249. [Theta characteristic-model orbit is universal, not RH-selective](theta-characteristic-model-orbit-is-universal-not-rh-selective.md)
    — closes the Hardy-factor, model-space, compressed-shift, Julia-unitary,
    and characteristic-transfer orbit, but shows that it works for every
    scalar inner function. Standard scalar model contractions have defect
    indices \((1,1)\) regardless of Blaschke degree, so the five theta current
    classes cannot be independent defect ports. A theta-selective contraction
    must be constructed from labelled currents before Hardy factorization.
250. [Theta cross zeros are incidence-divisor crossings, not operator failures](theta-cross-zeros-are-incidence-divisor-crossings-not-operator-failures.md)
    — interprets the scalar theta readout as a labelled matrix coefficient of
    the full transfer. Its zero is a crossing of the source ray into the
    endpoint-orthogonal incidence divisor while the full relation may remain
    unitary and invertible. A \(90^\circ\) rotation is the minimal hostile.
    The RH-bearing target becomes source-derived confinement of the transfer
    path to the corresponding open cell in each reciprocal sector.
251. [Theta forcing is the exact parabolic-confinement residual](theta-forcing-is-the-exact-parabolic-confinement-residual.md)
    — proves that an invariant endpoint hyperplane makes its scalar pivot obey
    a multiplicative ODE and therefore prevents zero crossings. In the native
    two-channel theta tail system, the endpoint covector fails invariance by
    exactly the source-forcing entry \(f(q)\); only the irrelevant constant
    channel is invariant. The live gate is a reciprocal source enlargement
    whose labelled currents assemble an invariant adjoint flag.
252. [Theta reciprocal double acquires an invariant flag exactly on the seam](theta-reciprocal-double-acquires-an-invariant-flag-exactly-on-the-seam.md)
    — doubles the direct and reciprocal-conjugate tail systems over their
    common forcing channel. The antisymmetric sheet covector cancels forcing
    and is invariant exactly when \(-z=\bar z\), equivalently
    \(\operatorname{Re}s=1/2\). This explains the critical line as the locus
    where reciprocal weights coalesce and a multiplicative relative
    coordinate becomes meaningful, while leaving open-sector confinement
    unresolved.
253. [Theta framed-pencil symmetrizer is exactly a port-collocation metric](theta-framed-pencil-symmetrizer-is-exactly-a-port-collocation-metric.md)
    — solves the fixed real-axis \(J\)-symmetry equations for the bordered
    Rosenbrock pencil. The spectral coefficient forces \(J\) to be block
    diagonal; its carrier block must commute with \(A_X\) and map the endpoint
    port to the source port. Modular reflection anticommutes with logarithmic
    translation and therefore belongs to a parameter-reversing reciprocal
    double, not a same-sheet symmetrizer. No smallest explicit authorized
    cutoff fixture is currently declared for a numerical witness.
254. [Theta causal-prefix flag propagates but does not anchor the spectral pivot](theta-causal-prefix-flag-propagates-but-does-not-anchor-the-spectral-pivot.md)
    — constructs the exact source-derived moving covector from the causal
    prefix and proves it cancels the one-sided forcing residual. Its invariant
    pivot is \(Y(q,z)=e^{-zq}F(z)\), so a spectral zero yields a scale
    trajectory lying in the moving hyperplane for every \(q\). The flag proves
    scale coherence, not spectral confinement; the next missing object is a
    source-derived connection in the \(z\)-direction.
255. [Theta flat spectral-jet connection detects but does not confine the divisor](theta-flat-spectral-jet-connection-detects-but-does-not-confine-the-divisor.md)
    — extends the causal-prefix pivot to the canonical flat connection
    \(\nabla_q=\partial_q+z\), \(\nabla_z=\partial_z+q\), and derives the
    triangular source-jet ladder. The connection records zero multiplicity but
    exists for hostile sources as well. A holomorphic rank-one connection that
    makes the pivot parallel necessarily contains \(F'/F\), so its regularity
    is equivalent to the desired zero-free theorem rather than an explanation
    of it.
256. [Theta positive even spectral transport still allows off-seam zeros](theta-positive-even-spectral-transport-still-allows-off-seam-zeros.md)
    — moves spectral dynamics before scalar compression by realizing the
    transform as \(\langle\Omega,e^{zQ}\Omega\rangle\) with self-adjoint
    \(Q\), a positive cyclic source, and reciprocal reflection. The positive
    even four-atom source \(\cosh z+\tfrac1{10}\cosh 2z\) nevertheless has an
    explicit off-seam zero. Source positivity and reciprocal spectral
    transport therefore require an additional theta-specific constraint on
    shell weights and spacings.
257. [Theta two-shell seam confinement requires nondecreasing outer weight](theta-two-shell-seam-confinement-requires-nondecreasing-outer-weight.md)
    — completely classifies the hostile family
    \(\cosh z+c\cosh 2z\). All zeros lie on the imaginary seam exactly for
    \(c\ge1\); every decaying choice \(0<c<1\) has an explicit off-seam pair.
    Hence finite positive shell decay points in the wrong direction, and any
    theta confinement mechanism must be irreducibly global or use a stronger
    all-shell coherence law.
258. [Theta completion can rescue finite off-seam zeros only by collapse or escape](theta-completion-can-rescue-finite-off-seam-zeros-only-by-collapse-or-escape.md)
    — applies local uniform convergence and Rouché stability to the completed
    source. Finite off-seam zeros cannot disappear inside a bounded open
    sector: they must approach a completed seam zero or escape every compact.
    Hence an all-shell explanation needs a source-derived divisor-transport
    law, not merely positivity of separate finite truncations.
259. [Theta outer-shell addition drives the hostile zero monotonically to the seam](theta-outer-shell-addition-drives-the-hostile-zero-monotonically-to-the-seam.md)
    — follows the off-seam zero of \(\cosh z+c\cosh 2z\) as the outer-shell
    weight increases. Its horizontal displacement decreases strictly from
    infinity to zero as \(c\) rises from zero to one. This realizes the only
    completion rescue allowed by packet 258 and suggests a falsifiable theta
    divisor-flow law: source-authorized completion must move every off-seam
    branch inward or introduce it only from spectral infinity.
260. [Theta third positive shell reverses the inward zero flow](theta-third-positive-shell-reverses-the-inward-zero-flow.md)
    — immediately attacks packet 259 by adding \(\varepsilon\cosh 3z\). At
    the hostile zero \(a+i\pi\), the exact first-order horizontal velocity is
    positive, so the zero moves away from the seam. More generally shell
    parity alternates the velocity sign. The viable transport primitive must
    therefore be a canonically paired or modularly sewn block, not an
    individual positive shell.
261. [Theta uniform parity-paired inward flow requires outer-even dominance](theta-uniform-parity-paired-inward-flow-requires-outer-even-dominance.md)
    — solves the smallest paired-shell compiler exactly. An even-to-odd block
    can never be uniformly inward. An odd-to-even block is uniformly inward
    exactly when the outer even coefficient is at least the inner odd
    coefficient. Raw decay violates this gate, so any viable dominance must be
    created by modular sewing, half-density normalization, or a typed
    arithmetic repair current.
262. [Theta discrete shell parity is not a native theta label](theta-discrete-shell-parity-is-not-a-native-theta-label.md)
    — audits the toy paired-shell compiler against the actual completed source.
    Toy parity belongs to discrete spectral support positions; theta arithmetic
    labels each carry a continuum of support coordinates. Native orientation
    is instead the moving sign-band partition of \(\cos(bu)\) and
    \(\sin(bu)\). Correct typing reconnects the route to the previously
    falsified pointwise adjacent-band shift, leaving only conditional or
    larger canonical blocks viable.
263. [Theta band boundary current is tilt derivative of translation-defect energy](theta-band-boundary-current-is-tilt-derivative-of-translation-defect-energy.md)
    — rewrites the first surviving aggregate conditional-band requirement as
    \(\partial_a\mathcal C_a(L)\ge0\), where \(\mathcal C_a(L)\) is the
    squared translation defect of the tilted completed source. Gram geometry
    proves \(\mathcal C_a\ge0\), while reflection makes it even in \(a\);
    neither orients its derivative. The smallest exact falsifier is negative
    curvature at \(a=0\).
264. [Theta log-concavity forces positive local band-boundary curvature](theta-log-concavity-forces-positive-local-band-boundary-curvature.md)
    — evaluates the smallest falsifier exactly. Strict log-concavity of the
    completed source gives
    \(\mathcal C_0''(D)>0\) for every \(D>0\), so the conditional boundary
    current has the correct sign for sufficiently small positive tilt. This is
    a local oriented flag theorem, not global monotonicity; the next falsifier
    is the first finite-\(a\) sign crossing.
265. [Theta log-concavity proves global tilt monotonicity of translation defect](theta-log-concavity-proves-global-tilt-monotonicity-of-translation-defect.md)
    — centers the full defect and writes it as a
    \(\cosh(2ay)\)-weighted midpoint log-concavity slack. Differentiation then
    proves \(\partial_a\mathcal C_a(D)>0\) for every \(a,D>0\), closing the
    first aggregate conditional-band boundary gate globally. The remaining
    obstruction is the weighted correlation term
    \(D W(D)-(D+L)W(D+L)\).
266. [Theta band-entrance repair is positive but nonuniform in port ratio](theta-band-entrance-repair-is-positive-but-nonuniform-in-port-ratio.md)
    — expands the full conditional residual at the band entrance. The proved
    \(J\)-current repairs the necessarily negative weighted-correlation term
    for every fixed \(\alpha,\beta>0\), but the protected layer shrinks like
    \(\beta/\alpha\). On the face \(\beta=0\), pointwise positivity fails
    immediately. The minimal surviving mechanism is positivity of the
    canonical block integral or a source-derived coefficient relation.
267. [Theta canonical adjacent block has a high-frequency falsifier](theta-canonical-adjacent-block-has-a-high-frequency-falsifier.md)
    — folds one canonical adjacent-band pair into exact cosine and weighted
    sine moments. Its fixed-port high-frequency asymptotic is correct, but its
    interpretation for the physical angular ports is retracted in packet 268.
268. [Theta physical-port high-frequency reduction](theta-physical-port-high-frequency-reduction.md)
    — restores the exact source relation \(\beta\sim2\pi/L\). The two channels
    then meet at order \(L^2\), and the physical block reduces to
    \(4[J_a''(0)-aW_a(0)]L^2/\pi+O(L^4)\). A strict source Hardy inequality
    proves that this coefficient is negative for sufficiently small positive
    \(a\). Thus the physically coupled one-block explanation fails at high
    \(b\), although the scalar Pick inequality remains untouched.
269. [Every fixed finite theta band block fails at high frequency](theta-every-fixed-finite-band-block-fails-at-high-frequency.md)
    — proves that a block of any fixed \(m\) complete phase periods has leading
    term \(4m[J_a''(0)-aW_a(0)]L^2/\pi\). The strict Hardy obstruction is
    merely multiplied by \(m\). Hence every fixed finite regrouping fails;
    only a band count growing with frequency or a pre-projection source law
    can still carry the explanation.
270. [Theta macroscopic band block reduces to terminal endpoint current](theta-macroscopic-band-block-reduces-to-terminal-endpoint-current.md)
    — takes \(m\sim R/(2L)\) and proves that rapid oscillation extracts the
    terminal current
    \(2L[J_a'(R)-aR W_a(R)]/\pi+O_R(L^2)\). Small macroscopic widths retain
    the negative Hardy orientation. Any viable block must cross a nonzero
    source coherence length, extend to infinity, or acquire a pre-projection
    modular current.
271. [Theta terminal band current is globally negative](theta-terminal-band-current-is-globally-negative.md)
    — uses strict log-concavity to show that the centered separation score is
    negative and reflection-even. Pairing it under the positive Mellin tilt
    proves \(J_a'(R)<0\), hence
    \(J_a'(R)-aR W_a(R)<0\), for every \(a,R>0\). There is no finite coherence
    length that rescues consecutive bands; only an endpoint escaping to
    infinity or a pre-projection identity survives.
272. [Theta boundaryless band sum is beyond all algebraic orders](theta-boundaryless-band-sum-is-beyond-all-algebraic-orders.md)
    — proves that parity kills every origin jet exposed by integration by
    parts and completed decay kills the endpoint at infinity. The full source
    is therefore beyond every algebraic high-frequency order, while every
    finite cutoff carries a negative terminal defect. The remaining sign is
    nonperturbative modular data. It also types the prime-square jet moments
    as distinct from a diagonal number operator.
273. [Quartic log-concave carrier is a nonperturbative hostile source](quartic-logconcave-carrier-is-a-nonperturbative-hostile-source.md)
    — constructs \(e^{-x^4-cx^2}\) and proves real Fourier oscillation, but
    its interpretation as an off-axis hostile witness is retracted: real
    transform zeros are compatible with RH-style confinement.
274. [Modulated Gaussian is an exact off-axis hostile source](modulated-gaussian-is-an-exact-off-axis-hostile-source.md)
    — constructs a positive, even, uniformly strictly log-concave Schwartz
    carrier with explicit off-axis transform zeros. It shares every local and
    algebraic boundary property used so far, proving that a labelled
    modular/arithmetic correspondence is the missing discriminator.
275. [Canonical half-line antipode is source-fixed but universal](canonical-half-line-antipode-is-source-fixed-but-universal.md)
    — derives the two half-line routes and equal-weight readout directly from
    the completed contour, making the antipode canonical in that frame. The
    hostile modulated Gaussian has the identical architecture and reaches it
    off seam. RH force must therefore enter through labelled modular coherence
    before half-line aggregation.
276. [Positive translate labels and a complete moment tower do not constrain the divisor](positive-translate-labels-and-complete-moment-tower-do-not-constrain-the-divisor.md)
    — constructs a positive, uniformly strictly log-concave packet of three
    translated Gaussians whose complete finite logarithmic moment tower is
    faithful by Vandermonde inversion, yet whose transform has explicit
    off-axis zeros. Algebraic label recovery and translation by entire units
    observe and transport the divisor but do not orient it. The first surviving
    candidate is an infinite-label primal--dual Poisson boundary current before
    scalar aggregation.
277. [Positive self-Fourier Poisson sewing still allows off-critical Mellin zeros](positive-self-fourier-poisson-sewing-still-allows-off-critical-mellin-zeros.md)
    — perturbs the Fourier-fixed Gaussian by a small Fourier-fixed degree-twelve
    Hermite direction. The carrier remains strictly positive, even, and
    Schwartz, while exact Poisson sewing holds. Its Mellin window is a cubic
    whose discriminant is negative for small positive perturbation, producing
    off-critical zeros. Poisson orbit restriction is therefore too large; the
    next operation must select the Gaussian vacuum and couple it to the comb.
278. [The Gaussian vacuum law is label-diagonal and cannot orient comb cancellation](the-gaussian-vacuum-law-is-label-diagonal-and-cannot-orient-comb-cancellation.md)
    — proves that the transported oscillator annihilator selects the Gaussian
    but annihilates every sampled label independently. Its scalar Ward identity
    is termwise zero, while dilation only generates the infinite even-moment
    observer tower. Any RH force must come from a mixed commutator residual
    coupling the oscillator ladder to Poisson label transport.
279. [The Gaussian derivative-comb current reduces to one seam port](the-gaussian-derivative-comb-current-reduces-to-one-seam-port.md)
    — performs the smallest faithful jet lift. Parity cancels the odd current,
    and the positive even number current is exactly one dilation derivative of
    the comb amplitude. Its half-Mellin transform factors through the existing
    half-line amplitude plus the single seam value (A(0)). Any new force must
    come from reciprocal-sheet orientation of that seam port.
280. [Reciprocal sewing makes the Gaussian seam port an observable, not a barrier](reciprocal-sewing-makes-the-gaussian-seam-port-an-observable-not-a-barrier.md)
    — sews the two number-current formulas exactly. The seam value doubles in
    the symmetric channel and cancels in the antisymmetric channel. At a scalar
    zero, the transverse route and number channels remain nonzero and
    well-defined. The seam port explains which information scalar aggregation
    forgets but supplies no zero-exclusion law.
281. [Local Fock confinement is broken only by the Gaussian boundary coupling](local-fock-confinement-is-broken-only-by-the-gaussian-boundary-coupling.md)
    — identifies the exact mixed relation (Q_pS_p=p^2S_pQ_p). Canonical
    half-density Fock truncations have all zeros on the seam and their infinite
    right-sector limit is zero-free. Gaussian sampling rigidly deforms the

## Superseded and prohibited branches

- Smooth reflection compression is not the Xi spectral operator: its spectral
  density is too sparse.
- Ordinary labelled augmentation followed by asserted positivity is circular.
- Positive mixtures of individually Lee--Yang divided-sinh atoms need not
  remain Lee--Yang.
- Pointwise prime-shift repair fails in the primitive tail; modular sewing is
  essential.
- A source-changing positive canonical-system completion is inadmissible.
- Pairwise score stiffening proves degree two but cannot be iterated to degree
  three without an adjacent-coherence law.

## Live theorem and falsifier

For adjacent quadratic deficits `u_r,u_{r+1}`, degree-three hyperbolicity is
equivalent to

\[
 (u_r-u_{r+1})^2
 \le u_ru_{r+1}(2u_r+2u_{r+1}-u_ru_{r+1}).
\]

The live source theorem asks whether theta tilt-covariances satisfy this cone
condition at every order. The smallest falsifier is one index `r` at which
the adjacent pair leaves the cone.

## Evidence boundary

The degree-two result is an analytic theorem derived from explicit theta
labels, rational inequalities, positive Taylor lower bounds, and convergent
tail majorants. It is not RH. No higher Jensen degree is claimed here.

Graph coordination: extraction acknowledged in event
`ev-000000002975-e100f449-adfa-4d10-81fa-316310ff4738`.

The aging-spectrum refinement gives a sharper live target. Even if complete

Centering at the exponential boundary exposes the hidden algebra. With

That packet now has an exact scalar generator:

The generator then collapses further. For

Integration by parts identifies the same function as the expected restoring

third logarithmic-tilt response of one explicit source force, not a generic

Writing $T_{\exp}=\partial_q^3\mathbb E(e^{2R})$ and

A direct $25\times25$ reconnaissance grid over

wall-pole channel appears to repair rather than oppose the confining

Third-response symmetrization exposes the hidden geometry. For centered

reduce to source-weighted dominance outside the canonical variance ellipse

The two source channels are complementary ports on that ellipse:
$e^{2R}$ amplifies the upper exterior tail, while
$(2ce^{2R}-3)^{-1}$ amplifies the lower wall tail. A two-point logistic
family gives an exact counterexample to generic third-response
monotonicity, so the proof must exploit this primitive two-tail geometry.

The first canonical transport candidate is Kelvin inversion after rescaling

Hostile sampling rejects both Kelvin inversion and the milder adjacent-

Thus the ellipse remains the correct signed geometry, but its proof cannot

Angular aggregation yields a cleaner invariant. Weighting source pairs by

$\mathbb E_{\Pi_0}(\rho^2)=\mu_4/v$. Thus positive log-residual excess

The covariance gate is false. The exponential port generally shifts the

The coupled budget appears to sit inside a stronger Bernstein-response law:
$h(q)=qM_{q-1}/M_q$ may satisfy
$(-1)^{n-1}h^{(n)}(q)\ge0$ for every $n\ge1$. The first four signs hold on
all $625$ reconnaissance pairs; higher floating-point jets are unreliable

Lévy--Khintchine measure of $h$, or return the first exact derivative

The Bernstein conjecture is exactly an exponential-functional constructor
claim. Exponential functionals of subordinators satisfy
$M_q/M_{q-1}=q/\phi(q)$, so the only possible Laplace exponent is

monotone Lévy scale flow whose exponential survival functional has the
primitive residual law. At $c=200$, $q=4$, stable logarithmic-coordinate
Decimal jets have the alternating sign through order twenty.

The subordinator's killing rate is already source-derived:
$h(0+)=F(a)/M_0$ for $c>3/2$, while the primitive zero at $c=3/2$ forces

At $c=200$, $q=4$, the signed derivative jet also passes the positive-measure
gate: ordinary and shifted Hankel forms through size nine have positive

a truncated positive Lévy measure, not merely alternating in sign.

The carrier now has an exact truncated-Gamma score normal form. With
$x=ce^{2R}$ and $L=\log(x/c)$,

\[
M_q=2^{-q-1}\int_c^\infty L^q x^{1/4}(2x-3)e^{-x}\,dx,
\]

and the primitive score factor is

\[
x^{1/4}(2x-3)e^{-x}\,dx
=-2x^{-1/4}d\!\left(x^{3/2}e^{-x}\right).
\]

The next theorem is therefore precise: decide whether this derivative-
generated truncated-Gamma transport preserves the Bernstein property of
the adjacent logarithmic moment ratio for every $c\ge3/2$.

The preservation theorem now has a necessary-and-sufficient constructor
form. For the normalized residual density $k_c$ and
$\kappa_c=k_c(0)=F(a)/M_0$, recover the Lévy tail $\overline\Pi_c$ from

\[
k_c(r)-\kappa_c\int_r^\infty k_c(y)\,dy
=\int_0^\infty
\overline\Pi_c(t)k_c(re^t)re^t\,dt.
\]

The conjecture closes if the unique deconvolution is a nonnegative,
nonincreasing Lévy tail for every $c\ge3/2$. The endpoint killing term is
therefore exact source data, not a heuristic interpretation.

Mellin transformation makes the candidate unique:

\[
\mathcal L\overline\Pi_c(s)
=\frac{h(s)-\kappa_c}{s}.
\]

The source theorem is now positivity, monotonicity, and integrability of
this forced inverse Laplace transform.

Two further Lévy parameters are fixed without inversion. Unbounded residual
support forces drift
$\lim_{s\to\infty}h(s)/s=0$, while the finite part of the Mellin pole at
$s=0$ gives

\[
\int_0^\infty\overline\Pi_c(t)\,dt=h'(0+).
\]

Together with killing $\kappa_c=k_c(0)$, the source now fixes the killing,

Strict concavity of the large-order moment phase gives a unique concentrating
saddle and the Lambert-$W$ asymptotic

\[
h(s)\sim\frac{2s}{W(s/c)},
\qquad
\int_0^t\overline\Pi_c(u)\,du
\sim\frac{2}{W(1/(ct))}.
\]

Thus the candidate is expected to have zero drift but borderline infinite

$2/(t\log^2(1/(ct)))$ still requires a second-order Tauberian argument and
must not be inferred from leading slow variation alone.

The primitive score now admits an exact adjacent-ratio Darboux reduction.
For unscored moments

\[
A_q=\int_c^\infty\log(x/c)^q x^{1/4}e^{-x}\,dx,
\qquad
b(q)=qA_{q-1}/A_q,
\]

one has

\[
h(q)=2b(q)\frac{b(q-1)-1/4}{b(q)-1/4}.
\]

The live theorem is now preservation of the required response sign under

simpler unscored generator $b$.

Integration by parts explains the quarter-shift:

\[
b(q)=\mathbb E_q(x-5/4),
\qquad
b(q)-1/4=\mathbb E_q(x-3/2).
\]

The transform is therefore adjacent-tilt transport of expected distance
from the primitive wall $x=3/2$.

Keeping the wall parameter exposes an exact differential-difference system:

\[
c\partial_cA_q=-qA_{q-1},
\qquad
\Psi_q=c^{1/4}A_q,
\qquad
h(q,c)=2q\frac{\partial_c\Psi_{q-1}}
{\partial_c\Psi_q}.
\]

Thus wall motion is the lowering operator in exponent degree, and the

The first universal sign-regular consequence is now proved. Positivity and

\[
\frac{d}{dq}\left(\frac{h(q,c)}q\right)<0,
\qquad
qh'(q,c)-h(q,c)<0.
\]

A positive two-atom flux model retains this law but its higher logistic

That extra structure proves the first Bernstein sign. In logarithmic wall
coordinate the flux density has

\[
(\log w_c)''
=-x-\frac{(3/2)x}{(x-3/2)^2}<0.
\]

Therefore $C_c(q)=B_q(c)/\Gamma(q+1)$ is log-concave by Berwald-Borell, and

\[
h(q,c)=2C_c(q-1)/C_c(q)
\]

is strictly increasing. The cubic gate is reduced to the higher normalized-

\[
\delta_3+3\delta_1\delta_2+\delta_1^3>0,
\qquad
\delta_j=f_c^{(j)}(q-1)-f_c^{(j)}(q).
\]

The tempting log-concave Bernstein upgrade has a typing obstruction. Its

The Gamma-wall potential has a canonical one-fold higher-curvature

V_c'''(y)
=x\left(1-\frac{3(x+3/2)}{(x-3/2)^3}\right),

and changes sign exactly once at

That hostile theorem is now falsified. The smooth family

The exact Gamma-wall equation has now been isolated in the bounded successor

The bivariate array is strictly totally positive at every finite order by

The $j=0$ recurrence now gives an exact two-port boundary formula. Its

The bounded successor packet `gamma-wall-riccati-transfer.md` constructs the

At exponent zero the corrected transfer has an exact three-dimensional

This is not yet a metric geometric-algebra theorem. It supplies an oriented

The symbolic all-degree audit now succeeds in

That second-direction audit now yields a typing obstruction in

Exponent shift is multiplication by the wall coordinate. On the polar Rees

The first such correspondence is now exact in
`mellin-rees-gysin-sequence-is-exact-and-degree-natural.md`. At jet order

\(0\to A_{N-1}\xrightarrow{y}A_N\to\mathbb R\to0\), not a square

The analytic attachment is now source-exact in

The full Pearson combination is \(-\partial_y\) of the shifted source

The proposed metric-selection step is falsified in

Source naturality then reveals a missing linear comparison coordinate in

The finite source adjoint is now explicit in
`pearson-source-fixes-the-finite-contragredient-but-not-its-strip-adjoint.md`.
The inverse transpose of the Gamma-wall transfer contains a forced
tail-covector-to-wall-covector injection, exactly dual to the primal

That duality is selected in

Mellin–Parseval pairs `q` with `-q-1`, reflecting the base strip about

The required graph space is constructed in

The bounded successor packet `gamma-wall-integer-stack-flag.md` extends this

tail two-planes plus the wall direction. This is a source-derived rank count,

The bounded successor packet `gamma-wall-mellin-residue-port.md` identifies

The proposed finite two-prime commutator has now been tested and closed in
`mellin-wall-jet-prime-cocycle-commutes.md`. Prime translations commute on

That first completion gate now has an exact negative answer in the ordinary
Fréchet topology of entire germs. The packet
`unbounded-prime-resegmentation-in-the-entire-germ-topology.md` proves that
the unbounded family of logarithmic prime translations is not
equicontinuous; moving-wall evaluations fail for the same reason. A viable

That covariance is now separated in
`the-cut-is-weyl-covariant-only-when-the-origin-is-retained.md`.
Differentiation commutes with the cut, whereas the global wall coordinate

general first-order source operator exactly covariant. Cut origins add under

differentiation and endpoint residue.

That three-operation subsystem closes in
`clark-differentiation-turns-the-affine-origin-into-the-prime-repair.md`.
Differentiating the cut phase `exp(i z L)` forces the Clark cocycle
`-a L F_L`; at `L=log p` this is exactly the earlier logarithmic prime

kernel `(r+L)^q`, which is regular at the internal cut. Resetting it to
`r^q` manufactures a false Mellin wall and false residues at `y=L`.
Therefore Fourier–Laplace transport, Clark differentiation, and endpoint
residue have no finite typed residual on the affine boundary-bearing cut.

The first completion-class consequence appears in
`a-zero-is-exactly-when-the-cumulative-seam-enters-h1.md`. The cumulative
seam profile is `B(L)=F-G(L)` and belongs to `H1(0,infinity)` exactly when
the scalar transform `F` vanishes. Moreover logarithmic prime sampling is
bounded on `H1`: the sum over primes of `|B(log p)|^2/p` is at most three
times the graph norm. Thus a zero removes the constant asymptotic mode and
upgrades the primitive sampled seam to a Hilbert vector. This characterizes
zeros but does not confine them; reciprocal two-sector compatibility remains
the RH-bearing gate.

That gate is corrected in
`a-zero-upgrades-only-the-symmetric-two-sector-quotient.md`. A scalar zero
makes only the symmetric aggregate of the two cumulative seam profiles enter
`H1`; generically neither individual sector does, because their nonzero
constant modes cancel. The antisymmetric boundary mode survives. Requiring
simultaneous individual admission is therefore a false target. RH must show
that the source-generated boundary line can meet the antisymmetric subbundle
only on the unitary Fourier–Tate seam.

That incidence target is identified in
`the-two-sector-boundary-incidence-is-the-de-branges-krein-norm.md`. The
boundary pair carries the signature `(1,1)` form
`|F_-|^2-|F_+|^2`; the antisymmetric zero line is null. Strict timelikeness
in each open sector is exactly Hermite–Biehler/de Branges modulus dominance,
not a new condition. Its source expansion contains a positive hyperbolic-sine
factor multiplied by the oscillatory cosine of source separation. The seam,
Krein, de Branges, and adjacent-band formulations are therefore coordinate
views of one unresolved orientation invariant.

The affine-origin surprise removes one ambiguity from the surviving
metaplectic mechanism in
`the-affine-origin-fixes-the-fourier-center-phase-but-not-the-maslov-grade.md`.
The origin forced by Weyl and Clark covariance supplies the exact compensator
for the Fourier minor's center phase, making the centered half-angle minor
translation-covariant. Its sign still flips at phase-space areas `2 pi n`;
an integer Maslov crossing grade is the remaining datum. High frequency makes
crossings unavoidable, so the global theorem must transport their grade into
the primitive, square, seam, and archimedean currents rather than assuming a
zero grade.

Finite grade transport is classified in
`the-maslov-carry-is-finitely-coherent-and-globally-divergent.md`. Writing
area in `2 pi` units as an integer grade plus a residual fraction turns

commutative, so no finite prime-order anomaly exists when all cross-area
cells are retained. At expanding cutoff the grade grows linearly with total
phase-space area and generally diverges. The next source object must therefore
be a relative or renormalized reciprocal-sector grade; the universal carry
alone contains no arithmetic orientation.

Reciprocal normalization is exact in
`reciprocal-maslov-divergence-cancels-before-arithmetic-currents.md`.
Signed sector areas `t` and `-t` have floor grades summing to `-1` away
from crossings and to zero at exact crossings. Adding the forced half-boundary
convention makes the centered grades reciprocally odd. Thus the extensive
Maslov divergence cancels before primitive or square currents enter. What

The smallest coefficient-sensitive hostile is in
`maslov-and-label-data-do-not-orient-the-two-atom-krein-form.md`. Two fixed

Therefore labelled crossing topology still carries no RH orientation; the

That coefficient-sensitive pairwise route is itself closed in
`actual-theta-source-enters-the-local-two-point-krein-negative-cone.md`.
The forbidden two-atom roots collapse to hyperbolic ratios, and their

The actual completed theta source enters this corridor near the modular seam
at high matching frequency. Hence a surviving orientation law must be

The first scalar global replacement is closed exactly in
`theta-separation-density-polya-convexity-no-go.md`. Pushing the Krein kernel
to source separation produces one positive density whose cosine transform is

between vertical mass and source-decay energy. The proved theta logarithmic

Thus the ordinary decreasing-convex Pólya certificate fails for every positive

The missing-port calculation is exact in
`bilateral-completion-leaves-one-antisymmetric-tail-fiber.md`. Full bilateral
autocorrelation splits into two reciprocal tails and one moving seam cell.
The completed sum together with the seam has rank two on these three cells;

measured by the Krein form. Thus an additional comparison channel is genuinely

Finite compression of the required odd port is ruled out in
`no-finite-odd-moment-port-recovers-krein-orientation.md`. For every finite
depth, two strictly positive separation measures have identical moments
through that depth and opposite cosine readout signs. The construction is the

The natural finite-recurrence escape is closed in
`theta-native-dilation-orbit-has-infinite-rank.md`. After removing its fixed

polynomial and each native dilation raises its degree by one with nonzero
leading coefficient. The resulting Krylov orbit has infinite rank. Dominance
of the primitive label transfers the constant-coefficient no-go to the
completed source. Any remaining finite description must therefore be
nonlinear, use transformed arguments, or introduce independently derived

The nonlinear source constraint that changes the fiber count is identified in
`theta-tail-seam-coherency-is-a-two-sheeted-light-cone.md`. Fourier transform
assembles the two tail powers and complex seam amplitude into a positive
rank-one coherency matrix. Its Stokes coordinates satisfy a null-cone identity.
Consequently the reciprocal-even completed data reconstructs the magnitude of
the missing Krein coordinate; only its sign remains. The linear antisymmetric
fiber collapses on the source image to two light-cone sheets. The RH-bearing
problem is now sheet preservation under authorized theta/Tate transport.

That first sheet-preservation proposal is immediately superseded by
`raw-theta-tail-coherency-crosses-sheets-off-seam.md`. The exact rank-one
light-cone geometry survives, but the raw theta-tail Stokes coordinate changes
sign repeatedly inside an open sector. Hence the protected sheet, if it
exists, belongs to a completed Clark/boundary connection rather than the raw
tail presentation.

The first finite transport audit is exact in
`arithmetic-block-transport-has-a-determinant-line-coherencer.md`. Multiplying
by any source-derived block transports the value--flux jet by a triangular
matrix whose alternating boundary form scales by the square of that block.
After extracting this determinant line, the remaining shear has determinant
one. For an Euler block, the logarithm of the coherencer splits exactly into
the doubled primitive current, doubled prime-square current, and connected
determinant-three tail; the archimedean block follows the same jet law. Thus
the apparent fifth wall is an arrow-level coherence line, not another state
observer. The remaining question is whether the completed Clark/boundary
connection supplies a nonvanishing, endpoint-compatible global section of
that line.

Finite state-sensitive repair is ruled out in
`no-finite-sequential-jet-orbit-protects-the-divisor.md`. Given any finite
collection of real probes and finite jet depths, an explicit real-even
polynomial multiplier equals one to every requested order while inserting a
prescribed off-axis zero quartet. The interpolation determinant is simply the
imaginary part of the squared hostile point and is nonzero for every genuine
quartet. Hence adding more finite observers or deeper finite jets cannot
protect the divisor. The remaining invariant must retain the complete
function-valued labelled orbit or impose an equivalent global source
recurrence, conservation law, or uniqueness theorem.

The first proposed behavioral replacement is itself corrected by
`universal-zero-propagation-and-joint-faithfulness-are-incompatible.md`.
Universal preservation of the scalar-zero kernel forces every value--flux
transport to be lower triangular. All future scalar rows then remain
collinear, so their joint kernel contains the entire flux line. Euler,
archimedean, and cutoff jets pass propagation precisely by failing to observe
that line; reciprocal exchange or Clark differentiation observes it only by
breaking propagation. The viable global target is therefore not universal
future silence, but a source conservation law constraining the nonzero
transverse current produced by a scalar zero.

That missing crossing is typed exactly in
`a-scalar-zero-enters-the-boundary-as-a-conormal-current.md`. The symmetric
Clark channel is the scalar value, while the antisymmetric channel is its
conormal derivative. A simple zero therefore transfers rank from value into a
nonzero reciprocal-odd boundary current. This current is covariant under every
nonvanishing source multiplier, and its quadratic energy transforms by the
determinant-line square. Squaring, however, erases reciprocal side
orientation. The singular remaining gate is a completed global boundary law
that couples the oriented conormal current to horizontal displacement from the
seam; the local adapter alone is universal.

The smallest hostile source for that global law is exact in
`a-positive-two-cell-source-uses-the-conormal-to-support-an-off-seam-zero.md`.
For every positive parameter, a two-cell positive source has a simple
off-seam zero, and its odd doubled forcing equals the divisor conormal
identically. At the zero their oriented product has exactly the sign permitted
by the Green identity. Positive autocorrelation, nonzero transverse current,
and determinant-line covariance therefore coexist with off-seam zeros. Any
surviving theta theorem must use a genuinely global labelled modular
cancellation absent from this two-cell source.

That theta-specific correction is isolated exactly in
`prime-scale-recursion-isolates-one-signed-autocorrelation-seam-window.md`.
Lifting the prime-label recursion to the separation autocorrelation yields
three positive labelled correlation cells and one negative finite moving-seam
window. The signed term is forced by interval translation rather than fitted
after scalarization, and its odd transform is the first native candidate for
the missing Green boundary current. The remaining gate is whether the
prime-labelled window variations telescope into the primitive, square, and
archimedean boundary packet under completion.

The window variation itself closes in
`the-moving-seam-derivative-is-exactly-the-local-green-forcing.md`.
Differentiating its odd transform with respect to seam position gives exactly
the local doubled Green forcing density, and integrating the seam from zero to
infinity reconstructs the full forcing. The unresolved step is now arithmetic
sampling: prime recursions expose only prime-power logarithms, not the
continuous scale integral. A source-derived quadrature or incidence theorem,
including the archimedean remainder, is required before primewise telescoping
is a defined claim.

The canonical arithmetic quadrature is audited in
`the-canonical-prime-quadrature-residual-already-carries-the-zeta-divisor.md`.
Von Mangoldt prime-power incidence on logarithmic scale differs from the
continuous scale measure by a distribution whose Laplace transform is the
shifted logarithmic derivative of zeta with its pole term removed. Its
remaining poles are exactly the shifted nontrivial zeros. Hence scalar control
of this quadrature residual would import the divisor and become circular. The
surviving options are a pre-aggregation source coboundary, an independent
operator law, or exactness on a separately derived restricted theta function
class.

The pre-aggregation escape is exact in
`valuation-depth-telescopes-the-seam-current-but-leaves-its-terminal-forcing.md`.
For any fixed prime, consecutive valuation depths partition the full scale
axis, so their window increments telescope to the complete autocorrelation
without point quadrature. This corrects the claim that arithmetic sampling
itself blocks reconstruction. The terminal window, however, is the full
forcing and does not vanish. Reciprocal reflection cancels that terminal only
by cancelling the horizontal energy simultaneously. The missing operation is
therefore an independently sourced endpoint incidence that treats those two
terms differently.

The archimedean completion supplies such a comparison map in
`archimedean-completion-retypes-terminal-forcing-into-precursor-bulk-and-origin-jets.md`.
Moving its differential through the half-line autocorrelation expresses
terminal forcing as a precursor odd transform plus two origin Sobolev jets
and a scalar-readout term. The last term vanishes on a zero-state, while the
origin jets have fixed source signs. This treats forcing differently from
horizontal energy, but leaves the oscillatory precursor transform as the next
hostile gate.

That hostile gate fails universally in
`a-positive-gaussian-mixture-falsifies-archimedean-endpoint-dominance.md`.
A two-scale positive Gaussian source has an explicit simple zero away from the
critical ray. Its inverse-completion precursor is also positive, even, and
decaying, so all fixed-sign origin Sobolev jets and the exact archimedean
factorization remain present. The precursor odd bulk can therefore balance
those endpoint terms. Any surviving theorem must use labelled theta lattice
and prime-scale/Poisson relations before aggregation, not archimedean source
shape alone.

The finite scalar-closure escape is excluded in
`the-minimal-theta-source-has-no-finite-constant-coefficient-scalar-closure.md`.
Successive derivatives of the primitive theta label acquire strictly
increasing polynomial degree after its common Gaussian factor is removed, so
no nonzero constant-coefficient differential operator annihilates it. The
first label also dominates the completed positive-chart sum at large scale,
extending the no-go to the aggregate source. Hence a finite scalar jet system
cannot carry the missing conservation law: the labelled or genuinely
infinite-dimensional state is structurally mandatory. Variable-coefficient
and nonlinear closures remain outside this theorem.

The canonical replacement is derived in
`the-theta-source-has-a-canonical-infinite-moment-ladder.md`. Positive moments
(M_k) satisfy the upper-bidiagonal source law
(DM_k=(2k+1/2)M_k-2M_{k+1}), while the theta source is the compression
(4M_2-6M_1). Every finite truncation has exactly one outward defect,
(-2c_NM_{N+1}). The additional wall is therefore a grade-raising incidence,
not another scalar observer. The next theorem must derive reciprocal sewing
and the Green form on two copies of this infinite graded module.

The reciprocal action is computed in
`poisson-reflection-selects-the-first-boundary-free-moment-covector.md`.
Fourier–Poisson transport sends (M_1,M_2) into triangular combinations of
the reciprocal moments plus a zero-lattice vacuum term. The covector
(4M_2-6M_1) cancels both the vacuum and (M_0) leakage and reproduces itself
on the other sheet. It uniquely spans the order-two boundary-free line. Thus
minimal theta completion is the first nontrivial boundary-free reciprocal
readout of the infinite moment ladder, not an externally chosen scalar
normalization.

The ladder's native positive geometry is derived in
`the-gaussian-gram-form-makes-the-moment-ladder-skew-with-a-rank-two-wall.md`.
The Gaussian carrier Gram matrix makes the infinite dilation generator exactly
skew. Every principal cutoff has a rank-at-most-two anomaly supported on its
top-grade wall, matching the linear outward current. This also exposes the
next typing boundary: carrier dilation energy is not spectral-tail energy, and
a unitary carrier matrix coefficient may vanish. The missing construction is
a source-derived intertwiner from this carrier Green form to the doubled
spectral-tail Green form with arithmetic boundary data retained.

That intertwiner is derived in
`the-moment-ladder-collapses-the-spectral-tail-to-one-base-level-set.md`.
Half-line transformation converts the raising law into a bordered recurrence.
Eliminating the first two grades expresses the physical transform through one

Zeros are consequently one affine level set of the bilateral base transform.
The infinite operator ambiguity has collapsed, but orientation of that level
set still requires the exact Poisson lattice structure and is the singular
remaining RH-bearing gate.

The affine constant is fixed in
`poisson-seam-fixes-the-moment-level-set-to-the-neutral-half-carrier.md`.
Differentiating the even full Gaussian lattice amplitude at the self-dual seam
gives (M_0(0)-4M_1(0)=-1/2). The moment-tail reduction therefore becomes

(X_\Phi=1/2+(z^2-1/4)X_0). This identifies the ladder construction as a
source factorization of the already known scalar completion, not independent

The pre-compression reciprocal structure is derived in
`the-theta-moment-ladder-is-a-reciprocally-graded-dilation-module.md`. The
Gaussian moment tower carries a Fourier involution (C) and dilation
generator (A) satisfying (C^2=1) and (CA+AC=0). The physical carrier
(4g_2-6g_1) uniquely spans the degree-two Fourier-even, vacuum-null line.
Thus the two reciprocal sectors are exact grades of one source module, while
the scalar functional equation is their later shadow. This is Clifford-like
but not a full Clifford representation, and grading alone does not orient the

The unitary spectral quotient is typed in
`reciprocal-even-dilation-has-one-spectral-channel.md`. On the even carrier,
logarithmic coordinates turn dilation into translation on one copy of
(L^2(\mathbb R)), so the unitary spectrum has multiplicity one. Seam zeros
cannot be cancellation between two hidden fiber channels. Off-seam zeros
belong instead to nonunitary analytic continuation of the distributional
comb matrix coefficient. The missing Hilbert–Pólya bridge is precisely a
source-derived theorem supporting that continued divisor on the unitary

The projected scalar operator is ruled out in
scalar-dilation-and-real-arithmetic-phase-defects-are-spectrally-free.md.
Free logarithmic momentum has no point spectrum, and every real scalar
potential—including prime-logarithm phase jumps—is removed by a unitary

final multiplicity-one seam with arithmetic inserted diagonally. The minimal

off-diagonally, or introduce independently sourced nonlocal or

The minimal doubled arithmetic constructor is isolated in
prime-reciprocal-exchange-is-positive-locally-and-noncommutative-globally.md.
The off-diagonal exchange \(E_p\) satisfies \(E_p^2=p^{-1}I\); on the unitary
seam, \(I-E_p\) is strictly positive. Distinct prime exchanges have an exact
oriented sine commutator, producing the first source-derived nonabelian
channel that survives the scalar gauge no-go. Its determinant product
collapses through \(\prod_p(1-p^{-1})\), and its ordered product requires a

The finite reciprocal fiber is completed in
two-nonresonant-primes-generate-the-full-reciprocal-matrix-algebra.md.
Normalized prime exchanges are Hermitian reflection axes in the Pauli
\(\sigma_1,\sigma_2\) plane. Two nonparallel axes generate \(\sigma_3\) by

at the resonances \(t\log(q/p)\in\pi\mathbb Z\). Thus two primes generically
supply the entire additional comparison channel; the remaining obstruction

The naive ordered completion fails absolutely in
the-cross-prime-magnus-channel-is-not-absolutely-summable.md. For every fixed

creates a new pair-labelled boundary anomaly not removed by local primitive

The invariant interpretation is corrected in
`prime-reciprocal-noncommutativity-is-an-exact-frame-coboundary.md`. Every

The general obstruction is isolated in
`the-first-genuine-extra-channel-is-a-nontrivial-triangle-residual.md`. Any

No such nonfactorizable theta/Tate pair law has yet been constructed; this is

The primitive and square boundary candidate is eliminated in
`primitive-and-square-euler-jet-currents-have-flat-triangle-transport.md`.
Normalized value-flux transports are commuting lower shears, while primitive,

The next viable audit must mix moving-seam restriction, Clark differentiation,
and Fourier–Tate reflection before endpoint evaluation.

That finite mixed-cut circuit was already proved coherent in

`no-universal-prime-power-quadrature-reconstructs-the-moving-seam-current.md`.
The logarithms of prime powers are locally finite, so a smooth bump between
\(\log2\) and \(\log3\) has positive continuous integral and zero arithmetic

The exact odd-tail family passes that restricted faithfulness gate in
`large-prime-seams-faithfully-detect-the-odd-theta-tail.md`. Boundary-layer
localization at large positive scale gives
\(D(L,z)\sim z\Phi(L)/(2\pi^2e^{4L})\). Thus every fixed nonzero spectral
parameter produces nonzero readout at every sufficiently large prime seam.
Prime sampling detects whether this channel vanishes identically, although it

The aggregation scale is determined in
`theta-prime-seam-forcing-is-superexponentially-small.md`. At
\(L=\log p\), the sampled forcing is asymptotic to a constant times
\(zp^5e^{-2\pi p^2}\). Every polynomially weighted Euler aggregation is

The interpolation premise is corrected in
`poisson-sewing-does-not-factor-through-prime-seam-sampling.md`. Global
Poisson sewing acts on the undecomposed adelic source before Euler projection.
A smooth gap variation lies in the kernel of prime-seam sampling but remains
visible to continuum source readouts, so Poisson cannot factor through that
sampling projection. The continuous seam and arithmetic currents are
independent typed components, not mutually reconstructible presentations.
The next construction must derive the doubled Green boundary form on the full
source before decomposing it into Euler and archimedean coordinates.

The first missing full-source coordinate is identified in
`mellin-radial-compression-forgets-the-norm-one-idele-class-fiber.md`. Mellin
characters factor through the idele norm and are constant on the norm-one
idele-class fiber. Every radial tail Green form therefore annihilates angular
fiber modes and cannot be faithful on the undecomposed adelic source. The
next operator must retain a fiber-valued radial state and rational-boundary
distribution before scalar Mellin compression. The immediate falsifier is
that the standard theta vacuum may occupy only the trivial fiber
representation, in which case the proposed extra channel collapses again.

That falsifier fires in
`the-standard-zeta-vacuum-occupies-only-the-trivial-norm-one-fiber-mode.md`.
Every unramified finite vacuum is invariant under local units, while the real
vacuum is sign-invariant. For \(\mathbb Q\), the standard zeta state therefore
occupies only the trivial representation of the compact norm-one idele-class
fiber. Restoring that ambient fiber adds no activated Riemann channel. The
remaining pre-Mellin object is relational: the moving incidence between the
adelic vacuum and the rational diagonal boundary.

The canonical horocycle completion is identified in
`modular-scattering-unfolds-each-riemann-zero-across-the-unitary-seam.md`.
The one-cusp modular scattering coefficient
\(\Lambda(2s-1)/\Lambda(2s)\) sends each Riemann zero \(\rho\) to a pole at
\(\rho/2\) and a zero at \((\rho+1)/2\). RH becomes confinement to the quarter
and three-quarter lines around the unitary half seam. This explains the
offset fold exactly but does not prove it: one-cusp scattering is scalar, and
self-adjointness controls the unitary seam rather than continued resonance
locations. The next gate is the uncompressed Eisenstein Fourier-mode Green
form before constant-term compression.

That linear-mode gate closes in
`eisenstein-fourier-modes-repeat-rather-than-constrain-the-scattering-divisor.md`.
Every nonconstant mode shares the scalar factor \(1/\Lambda(2s)\), and the
first divisor coefficient is one, so the full vector faithfully repeats the
same pole divisor. Mode ratios cancel it, while the Maass–Selberg norm uses
only \(-\varphi'/\varphi\). Linear mode energy therefore measures the scalar
resonance density without confining it. Any surviving automorphic mechanism
must be nonlinear or relative, with at least two spectral arguments or source
states.

The canonical two-spectral relative pairing is exhausted in
`the-two-spectral-maass-selberg-kernel-is-a-scalar-scattering-bezoutian.md`.
Green reduction expresses the full Eisenstein bulk pairing through the cusp
concomitant, whose exact expansion depends only on
\(s,w,\varphi(s),\varphi(w)\). The diagonal derivative term is its degenerate
limit. Thus constant terms, linear modes, norms, and two-parameter pairings
are presentations of the same scalar one-cusp divisor. The untwisted
automorphic-energy lane has reached a structural boundary; a twisted sector
would require a new source and an independently proved descent back to RH.

The naive twisted enlargement is closed in
`unit-equivariant-character-twists-do-not-constrain-the-trivial-riemann-block.md`.
Every comparison commuting with the compact unit action is block diagonal in
character coordinates. The standard zeta vacuum occupies the trivial block,
so nontrivial Dirichlet or Hecke sectors impose no constraint on it. A useful
cross-character map must arise independently from conductor-changing or
rational-boundary incidence. The active programme therefore returns to the
source-derived Lagrangian boundary relation rather than accumulating twists.

The representation-theoretic return meets a sharper selection obstruction in
`weyl-masa-does-not-select-the-cayley-boundary-phase.md`. The rational phase
lattice may generate a maximal commuting Weyl algebra, but that algebra and a
cyclic theta state admit many distinct unitary Cayley phases and hence many

not the gluing phase of the two valuation sectors.

Abstract Fourier–Tate sewing does not supply that selector, as proved in
`reciprocal-sewing-does-not-select-the-cayley-phase.md`. A conjugate phase
family remains inside the same maximal boundary algebra with the same
reciprocal-invariant cyclic state. In geometric-algebra terms, sewing fixes
the comparison plane but not the rotor angle. The active construction target
is now an oriented source current or infinitesimal generator that fixes this
angle before determinant compression.

A minimal phase-rigidity mechanism is isolated in
`cyclic-theta-stabilization-rigidly-selects-the-boundary-phase.md`. A unitary
multiplier in a maximal abelian algebra that fixes a cyclic full-support
vector must be the identity. Thus the phase family collapses if rational
boundary transport stabilizes the completed theta vacuum, rather than merely
being observed by it. The remaining source gate is exact: prove cyclicity and
stabilization on the full boundary algebra, with multiplicities and boundary
currents retained.

The direct application is corrected in
`rational-weyl-fixed-theta-state-is-not-cyclic.md`. Rational translations and
modulations genuinely fix the adelic theta comb, so its orbit under that same
boundary algebra is one-dimensional rather than cyclic. Stabilization and
cyclic generation must therefore belong to two different actions: a rational
stabilizer and a noncommuting transverse Weyl polarization. Since a maximal
abelian stabilizer equals its commutant, another commuting port cannot help.
The active rigidity target is the Heisenberg compatibility of the joint
action.

That joint rigidity is realized exactly in
`full-weyl-compatibility-reduces-phase-ambiguity-to-a-scalar.md`. The
simultaneous commutant of an irreducible clock–shift Weyl pair is scalar, and

polarizations before scalar compression.

The selector is correctly typed as an intertwiner in
`weyl-intertwining-uniquely-selects-the-fourier-phase.md`. Exact clock–shift
relations make the intertwiner space one-dimensional, and the source vacuum
normalization fixes its scalar. This solves phase selection in the finite
Weyl model without zero data. The active obstruction has moved to the
operator bridge: prove that the normalized adelic Fourier graph is a maximal
