The finite operator bridge succeeds only after an essential source
restriction, as shown in
`reflection-even-completion-makes-the-fourier-graph-lagrangian.md`. The full
Fourier graph is not isotropic because Fourier squares to reflection. On the
completed reflection-even theta sector, Fourier becomes a self-adjoint
involution and its graph is Lagrangian; the odd sector retains the
quarter-turn obstruction. The next exact audit is whether typed adelic seam
and prime-current corrections preserve this even-sector Green isotropy.

The linear zero-to-incidence bridge has an exact minimal rank gap, recorded in
`a-scalar-zero-is-one-channel-short-of-even-fourier-graph-incidence.md`. In
the smallest two-dimensional even boundary space, Fourier-graph membership
has two independent rows while a linearly compressed scalar supplies only
one. A genuine relative determinant can encode the full rank loss in one
nonlinear scalar, so the active gate is now a typing fork: derive \(\xi\) as
the Fourier-graph determinant, or supply a second source-derived value–flux
observer. Discrete prime-seam samples cannot reconstruct the latter
universally.

The first direct construction of the missing spectral Cauchy family meets an
exact typing obstruction in
`the-tail-boundary-system-is-a-singular-pencil-not-a-deficiency-family.md`.
Homogeneous tail augmentation has spectral weight
\(P=\operatorname{diag}(1,0)\); its null direction is the essential constant
source channel. Reciprocal doubling remains singular. Thus the tail states
are not yet deficiency vectors of one fixed symmetric operator. The next
operator theorem must treat a self-adjoint singular pencil/linear relation or
derive an additional dynamical source partner that regularizes the weight.
The required symmetric completion adds precisely the reverse coupling missing
from the earlier triangular accumulator system, so the Lagrangian route and
the canonical-system route converge on the same one-way-incidence frontier.

Fourier completion does not itself repair that frontier, as proved in
`even-fourier-completion-does-not-supply-reverse-source-incidence.md`. In the
minimal even chart, conjugating the triangular source coupling by the
self-adjoint Fourier involution leaves a full-rank residual against its
adjoint. Thus boundary Lagrangian selection and bulk symmetric completion are
independent constructors. The missing operation is irreducibly a
source-derived reverse incidence into the primitive/source channel.

Carrier reflection recovers part of that incidence in
`the-tail-is-a-fixed-resolvent-but-its-zero-is-only-a-cross-port-cancellation.md`.
The tail is a resolvent of the fixed translation generator, and the reflected
head is its Hilbert adjoint. The remaining obstruction is port-level: the
theta source is \(f\), while the scalar observer is \(\delta_0\). A
self-adjoint three-point carrier with distinct real ports has a
cross-resolvent zero at \(\pm i\). Thus the missing constructor is now a
positive/conservative source–observer colligation, not the adjoint carrier
evolution itself.
This converges with the earlier two-port cross-transfer and local-metric
no-go packets; the added evidence is the explicit tail–head resolvent
derivation and minimal self-adjoint nonreal cross-zero witness.

The positive circle heat construction cannot directly identify the two ports,
as proved in
`circle-heat-preparation-cannot-collocate-the-logarithmic-endpoint-port.md`.
The circle Laplacian has pure point spectrum, while logarithmic translation
has no \(L^2\) eigenvectors; every bounded generator-intertwiner between them
is zero. Heat-and-trace preparation is therefore a cross-carrier
correspondence, not a reversible port transport. A surviving colligation must
retain both carriers and its adjoint incidence explicitly.

Nima's Fourier-saturated boundary topology succeeds only as a typed family,
as shown in
`fourier-saturation-preserves-but-does-not-merge-the-tate-boundary-grades.md`.
At every finite cutoff the primitive and square Gramians saturate exactly and
become nondegenerate on both reciprocal sheets. Their smallest generalized
eigenvalue is the inverse square of the largest prime at the displayed
Mellin grade and therefore tends to zero. The connected-tail port is natively
absolute-summable rather than Hilbertian. The canonical completion candidate
is consequently a Fourier-invariant pro-Gram topology, with seam and
archimedean graph seminorms still to be derived. A single five-channel Gramian
would erase source-required regularity types.

The first two-carrier categorical calculation is recorded in
`the-first-two-carrier-naturality-square-closes-only-before-cutoff.md`.
Labelled circle preparation and multiplication on the logarithmic chart form
an exact naturality square, and its adjoint square also closes, without
identifying the circle Laplacian with log translation. The first
Fourier–Poisson Beck–Chevalley square fails at finite Euler cutoff: truncated
lattice combs are not Fourier-stable, and the residual is
(P_XF(I-P_X)). This Poisson cutoff leakage is a typed anomaly, not a scalar
error. The remaining coherence cell must be constructed on the full
restricted source before diagnostic Euler projection.

The required global source cell is constructed in
`the-global-metaplectic-theta-cell-exists-but-is-observer-incomplete.md`.
Normalized Gaussian dilation satisfies
\(\mathcal Fg_q=g_{-q}\), while the full integer comb is Fourier-fixed as
a tempered distribution. Their pair therefore defines a genuine global
rigged-space two-cell whose scalar shadow is theta modular reflection. The
cell is nevertheless observer-incomplete: no functorial lift to the typed
primitive, square, connected, seam, and archimedean ports has been derived,
and the degree-twelve self-Fourier hostile shows that metaplectic coherence
alone cannot confine zeros. The next exact square is trace naturality from the
additive Schwartz carrier into the Fourier-saturated multiplicative boundary
vessel.

The real-place trace square is made explicit in
`fourier-conjugated-tate-trace-is-hankel-and-generates-the-full-moment-boundary.md`.
After retaining both multiplicative signs and writing (x=\sigma e^q),
additive Fourier transform becomes a Hankel operator with kernel
(e^r\exp(-2\pi i\sigma\tau e^{q+r})). It reverses Mellin translation
exactly. Its endpoint Taylor coefficients are all global moments of the
logarithmic trace, so no finite archimedean jet packet is Fourier-closed. The
scalar archimedean determinant line is therefore a compression of an
infinite-rank moment boundary. The next assembly must join this moment
rigging to the separately typed primitive, square, connected, and seam ports.

The moment-boundary correction is proved in
`the-full-moment-boundary-is-still-not-faithful.md`. A nonzero Schwartz state
whose Fourier transform is smoothly supported away from zero has every
moment equal to zero. Hence even the entire endpoint moment tower is only a
Taylor germ and has an infinite-dimensional kernel on the ambient Schwartz
source. The faithful archimedean port must retain the full two-sign
function-valued Fourier trace, unless the theta constructor module is
independently proved quasianalytic. The observer hierarchy is now full trace,
then moment germ, then scalar determinant line, with faithfulness lost at
each unsupported compression.

The apparent Gaussian-quasianalytic escape is closed for ordinary Hilbert
completion in `gaussian-dilations-fill-the-even-hilbert-carrier.md`. In the
logarithmic coordinate the Gaussian dilation orbit becomes the translation
orbit of (e^{q/2}e^{-\pi e^{2q}}), whose Fourier–Mellin multiplier is a
nonvanishing Gamma factor. The orbit is therefore cyclic and its (L^2)
closure is the entire even carrier, including moment-invisible states.
Gaussian provenance does not make moment compression faithful after Hilbert
completion. A surviving quasianalytic construction must use a strictly finer
source-derived topology and prove continuity of every arithmetic boundary
constructor.

A first such finer topology is constructed in
`a-fourier-stable-gaussian-rigging-restores-moment-faithfulness.md`. The
Roumieu Gaussian Gelfand–Shilov family is stable under Fourier parameter swap
and reciprocal dilation scaling, contains the theta Gaussian orbit, makes the
integer-comb pairing continuous, and restores moment faithfulness through
entire continuation. It excludes the flat Fourier-bump hostile without
collapsing to the full even Hilbert carrier. The remaining admission gate is
adelic: primitive, square, connected, seam, Hankel-trace, and arithmetic Fock
constructors must all extend continuously without merging their grades or
introducing cutoff-dependent topology.

The first constructor gate already forces a boundary extension, as shown in
`the-tail-constructor-forces-a-four-component-fourier-boundary-cell.md`. The
self-Fourier Gaussian tail has a nonzero left asymptote and therefore leaves
every Gaussian decay step. Its Fourier transform is exactly a half-delta plus
an odd Gaussian principal-value distribution. Consequently the minimal
Fourier-stable tail cell has four typed components: Gaussian bulk, one-sided
tail carrier, delta boundary, and odd comparison port. The Gaussian rigging
survives as the bulk object but cannot be the full source domain. Primitive
and prime-square incidence must be tested on this boundary-bearing extension.

Linear closure corrects the component count in
`the-linear-fourier-tail-cell-has-five-components.md`. Reflection of the two
tail orientations reconstructs the constant carrier, and Fourier exchanges
that constant with the delta atom. In the centered basis the cell splits into
a fixed Gaussian line, a constant–delta exchange plane, and an odd
tail–principal-value quarter-turn plane. Thus four nonconstant roles become
five components once the affine relation is linearized. The finite cell is
closed under Fourier and reflection but not automatically under repeated
boundary differentiation; the next gate is whether the authorized
constructor algebra forces an infinite jet prolongation.

The jet gate is resolved conditionally by constructor order in
`clark-before-tail-closes-the-five-cell-the-reverse-order-creates-jets.md`.
Source multiplication followed by tail completion preserves the five-type
cell; for the Gaussian its first Clark tail is again Gaussian. Reversing the
operations multiplies a nonzero tail asymptote by the scale coordinate and
therefore creates a linear boundary mode and a delta-prime Fourier jet. The
commutator is a capability-changing braid residual, not a scalar anomaly.
The native spectral Clark derivative has the safe source-first order, so a
sixth simultaneous wall is unnecessary provided this precedence survives the
adelic assembly.

Prime transport on the corrected cell is computed in
`prime-shifts-act-unipotently-on-the-five-cell.md`. A logarithmic translation
sends the centered tail to itself plus a Gaussian bulk cocycle. The phase
difference cancels the Fourier principal-value singularity, so translations
act trivially on the boundary quotient and unipotently on the full extension.
For a finite prime packet, the quotient coefficient is the raw coefficient
sum while every label also contributes a distinct bulk cocycle. Hence the
five-cell does not regularize the primitive or prime-square divergence. The
combined source must retain two independent axes: the archimedean five-cell
and the Fourier-saturated arithmetic pro-Gram family.

Reciprocal sewing sharpens the obstruction in
`reciprocal-prime-pairing-cancels-the-seam-but-windows-converge-to-the-unit.md`.
For each finite logarithmic label, a translated centered tail plus its
reflected partner cancels the seam quotient and leaves an even Gaussian bulk
window of width twice the label. At prime label \(\log p\), these windows
broaden and converge locally to the negative unit as \(p\) grows. They escape
every fixed Gaussian step. Thus finite reciprocal sewing is exact, but global
completion can recreate the constant boundary mode from nominally bulk
vectors. The arithmetic grades must control this widening-window family.

The required pullback is constructed in
`scale-pullback-closes-prime-window-escape-onto-the-fifth-wall.md`. Using
\(r=1/p\) compactifies large-prime scale by the boundary point \(r=0\), and
the reciprocal-window family extends there with value \(-1\). Fourier sends
that boundary fiber to \(-\delta_0\), so the escape closes onto the existing
constant–delta plane: the fifth wall is precisely the Fourier-stable boundary
at infinite prime scale. Completing the labelled graph \((r,W_r)\), rather
than its unlabelled image, preserves scale provenance. Primitive and square
summability is not automatic; their remaining defect is now typed as
incidence in this boundary fiber.

The scale-boundary orders are classified in
`primitive-and-square-currents-have-half-order-and-zero-order-scale-boundaries.md`.
On test functions vanishing like \(r^\alpha\), the primitive seam current is
absolutely continuous only for \(\alpha>1/2\), while the square current needs
\(\alpha>0\). Their boundary cases both reduce to divergent prime exponent
one. Because the reciprocal window has boundary value \(-1\), subtracting
that source-declared fifth-wall value isolates the divergent coefficient and
leaves a rapidly boundary-vanishing remainder. The remaining theorem is a
source-derived finite-part sewing of the primitive, square, and archimedean
coefficients in the constant–delta fiber.

The existing scalar theta-heat finite part cannot supply that lift, as shown
in `theta-heat-finite-part-does-not-lift-through-the-euler-logarithm.md`.
When a regulated scalar has asymptotic form \(B_\varepsilon+\zeta+o(1)\)
with divergent \(B_\varepsilon\), the finite part recovers \(\zeta\), but the
finite part of its logarithm is controlled by \(\log B_\varepsilon\), not by
\(\log\zeta\). At a zero the latter is undefined. Hence scalar heat
regularization cannot be pulled backward through the Euler logarithm to
recover primitive and square boundary grades. The next gate is a pre-scalar
determinant/Fock lift, expressed as a Beck–Chevalley square between the scale
pullback and valuation filtration.

That square is computed in
`scale-and-valuation-commute-up-to-a-canonical-bulk-two-cell.md`. Pulling back
at primitive scale and then moving to valuation depth \(k\) retains
\(W_{\log p}\), while valuation-first transport correctly produces
\(W_{k\log p}\). Their difference is a smooth Gaussian bulk 2-cell. Both
paths have the same fifth-wall boundary class, adjacent depth cells telescope,
and the residual vanishes at infinite prime scale. Thus the square is
coherently lax rather than strictly commutative. Primitive-to-square sewing
must retain this scale-widening bulk cell before scalar heat pushforward.

Global weighting reopens the boundary in
`weighted-scale-valuation-cells-recreate-the-two-arithmetic-boundary-grades.md`.
Although each adjacent scale cell vanishes on fixed compact sets as the prime
grows, its support travels with \(\log p\). Prime density turns the
primitive-weighted sum into an \(e^{q/2}/q\) wall and the square-weighted sum
into a constant plateau asymptotic to \(-\log2\). Thus the two arithmetic
completion grades reappear as distinct archimedean growth types. The next
source object must retain the joint prime-scale/observation correspondence,
naturally in the comoving coordinate \(u=q-\log p\), before pushforward.

The comoving refinement itself requires two charts, as proved in
`the-comoving-scale-valuation-cell-has-two-boundary-charts.md`. An adjacent
valuation cell has moving fronts at \(q=j\log p\) and
\(q=(j+1)\log p\). Centering on them separately gives limiting profiles
\(H(u)-1\) and \(-H(u)\). Their sum is the negative unit, so the fifth
constant–delta wall is precisely their Čech overlap datum. The raw traveling
window was a projection of these two complementary localizations into one
observation coordinate. The next gate is a relative two-front pushforward
that pairs their currents before integrating prime scale.

The relative chart incidence becomes positive in
`oriented-two-front-pushforward-has-positive-gaussian-density.md`. The sum of
the inner and outer profiles is the negative unit overlap, while their
oriented difference is \(1-2H(u)=\operatorname{erf}(\sqrt\pi u)\). Its
derivative is the strictly positive density \(2e^{-\pi u^2}\). Thus the
two-chart pushforward splits into a constant fifth-wall coefficient and a
universal positive Gaussian bulk current before prime-scale integration. The
remaining sign problem is entirely in the arithmetic scale coefficient.

That coefficient is nonnegative after the source filtration, as proved in
`valuation-excess-times-oriented-front-density-is-universally-positive.md`.
The operator \(E_p-I+N_p\) has eigenvalue zero at valuations zero and one and
eigenvalue \(r-1\) at valuation \(r\ge2\). Coupling it to the oriented front
density gives \(2(r-1)(\log p)e^{-\pi u^2}\ge0\). This is the first universal
coupled arithmetic–archimedean positivity law. Its exact global kernel is the
squarefree sector, so the next theorem must supply a complementary
source-derived pairing or transversality law there.

The squarefree kernel is repaired in
`the-primitive-two-front-cell-repairs-the-squarefree-kernel.md`. The missing
depth \(0\to1\) reciprocal window has fronts at \(-\log p\) and \(+\log p\)
with the same two limiting profiles and positive oriented Gaussian density.
Its coefficient is the occupancy projector \(P_p=I-E_p\). Adding it to
valuation excess \(N_p-P_p\) gives the full number operator \(N_p\), hence
coupled energy \(2v_p(n)(\log p)e^{-\pi u^2}\). Summed over primes this is
\(2(\log n)e^{-\pi u^2}\), whose only label-space kernel is the vacuum
\(n=1\). The next gate is source-derived vacuum transversality in the
zero-endpoint domain.

Vacuum transversality closes the finite algebraic kernel in
`finite-zero-packets-have-strict-full-interval-energy.md`. The Mellin readout
sends the vacuum label to one, so its kernel has zero intersection with the
vacuum line. Primitive occupancy plus valuation excess gives energy
\(2\sum_n|c_n|^2\log n\). Hence every nonzero finite-support Mellin-null
packet has strictly positive full interval energy. This is not RH: the
distinguished completed theta packet has not been placed in a strict
completion of this energy domain. The remaining obstruction is the
completion-stable zero-to-state bridge and exclusion of energy escape through
the typed boundary grades.

The interval energy receives a crucial scope correction in
`full-interval-energy-is-state-norm-not-riemann-overlap.md`. For the
transported coefficient state \(c_s=(n^{-s})\), it equals
\(-\partial_\sigma\zeta(2\sigma)\) and is independent of spectral height.
Packets with identical magnitudes can have equal energy but readouts zero and
nonzero. Thus the two-front tower proves positive source-state geometry, not
Riemann-overlap orientation. The next object must be a mixed
source–observer kernel retaining Mellin phases, with the interval energy as
its diagonal and the completed theta section as its boundary contraction.

The canonical polarization is identified in
`the-riemann-readout-is-an-exterior-boundary-value-of-the-polarized-interval-kernel.md`.
On \(\Re s,\Re w>1/2\), the positive Gram kernel is
\(K(s,w)=\zeta(s+\bar w)\), and its logarithmically weighted polarization
\(-2\zeta'(s+\bar w)\) has the full interval energy on the diagonal. The
Riemann readout is formally \(K(s,0)\), but \(w=0\) lies outside the Hilbert
parameter domain and corresponds to the nonnormalizable constant observer.
Thus RH-bearing content is the source-authorized relative boundary morphism
to that exterior observer, with all five walls retained.

The heat approach to that observer is resolved in
`the-polarized-heat-boundary-morphism-is-canonical-but-not-positive.md`.
Nonnegative heat profiles give positive regulated polarized kernels, and
Mellin finite part canonically recovers \(\zeta(s+\bar w)\). But this boundary
map is not positivity preserving: at the exterior diagonal the positive
Gaussian theta sum has canonical finite part \(\zeta(0)=-1/2\). Hence the
observer is canonical only as a relative boundary object. The next Green
construction must retain the positive bulk and signed heat wall as separate
coordinates; scalar finite part cannot carry the interior Gram order.

The boundary wall is already nonscalar in
`the-exterior-zeta-kernel-needs-more-than-one-negative-boundary-channel.md`.
On the exact exterior probe packet \(0,-1,-2\), the continued Hankel kernel
\(\zeta(s+\bar w)\) has LDL pivot signs \((-,+,-)\), hence two negative
directions. A single compensating boundary scalar cannot realize this
continuation. The faithful fifth wall must be a transported boundary module,
possibly a pro-object, whose reciprocal completion is performed before
scalar finite part.

The requested next pullback is identified in
`the-exterior-zeta-boundary-module-is-the-jet-pullback-of-the-todd-normalization.md`.
For the Todd germ \(\tau(t)=t/(e^t-1)\), its logarithmic tangent
\(L=(\log\tau)'\) satisfies the exact identity
\(L^{(n)}(0)=\zeta(-n)\). Hence the exterior Hankel kernel on nonpositive
integral probes is the jet prolongation of one multiplicative
Euler–Maclaurin normalization germ. This reconciles the rank-two negative
witness with a single source: one scalar value is insufficient, while one
formal germ generates all translated boundary directions. Reciprocal sewing
must now be sought before logarithmic differentiation, at the multiplicative
germ or determinant-line level.

Reciprocal action on that germ is computed in
`reciprocal-sewing-uniquely-centers-the-todd-germ-by-a-half-density.md`.
The exact transition is \(\tau(-t)=e^t\tau(t)\). Among exponential
renormalizations \(e^{at}\tau(t)\), reflection invariance uniquely forces
\(a=1/2\), producing the even germ
\(t/(2\sinh(t/2))\). Its logarithmic tangent is \(L+1/2\), so the
\(\zeta(0)=-1/2\) anomaly cancels and the remaining boundary current is the
odd Bernoulli tower. This is a source-local model of the critical
half-density offset; the next gate is a Mellin naturality square proving that
this formal centering transports to the global coordinate \(s-1/2\).

That naturality square is not automatic, as shown in
`todd-half-cell-translation-is-not-yet-the-mellin-half-density.md`. Under the
standard Euler–Maclaurin realization, the Todd variable is \(D_x\), so its
half-density is additive half-cell translation. The Mellin spectral shift is
generated by multiplication by \(\log x\), and
\([D_x,\log x]=x^{-1}\). The shared coefficient \(1/2\) therefore does not
authorize identification of the two operations. The next pullback must add
the operator realization of the formal germ and derive the exponential
coordinate comparison, including its Jacobian and boundary anomaly.

That exponential comparison is now exact in
`the-exponential-half-density-centers-mellin-transport-but-leaves-an-additive-todd-anomaly.md`.
The unitary pullback \((Uf)(q)=e^{q/2}f(e^q)\) rewrites Mellin transform in
the character coordinate \(z=s-1/2\), and conjugates the centered dilation
generator \(xD_x+1/2\) to \(D_q\). This derives the spectral half-offset from
the Jacobian rather than from zeros. Additive Todd transport still carries
the exact residual \(UD_xU^{-1}=e^{-q}(D_q-1/2)\). The next source object is
therefore a two-axis operator cell retaining this multiplier and its endpoint
current.

The complete power law of that cell is derived in
`exponential-pullback-resolves-additive-todd-jets-into-the-full-scale-grade-tower.md`.
For \(A=e^{-q}(D_q-1/2)\), one has
\(A^k=e^{-kq}\prod_{j=0}^{k-1}(D_q-1/2-j)\). At \(q=\log p\), the
coefficient is \(p^{-k}\). Thus primitive \(k=1\), square \(k=2\), and
connected \(k\geq3\) currents are generated by one pulled-back additive Todd
jet tower, with ordering recorded by a centered falling factorial. The next
test is typed termwise reciprocal sewing of this series.

That interpretation receives a necessary correction in
`todd-normalization-does-not-populate-the-full-euler-grade-carrier.md`.
The power law supplies an ambient slot at every \(k\), but the uncentered
Todd series occupies only \(k=1\) and even grades, while centered Todd
occupies only even grades. The Euler logarithm has nonzero coefficients at
every positive grade and is the actual source of the full prime-power tower.
Reflection preserves grade, so missing odd Todd grades cannot be recovered by
sewing. The next level is a typed comparison between Todd boundary
normalization and Euler-logarithm occupation on their shared operator
carrier.

The required Todd–Euler comparison is identified in
`todd-and-euler-are-two-boundary-localizations-of-one-global-scale-current.md`.
With \(E(r)=-\log(1-r)\), \(r=e^{-t}\), and
\(\tau(t)=t/(e^t-1)\), the exact overlap identity is
\(\tau(-\log r)=(-\log r)rD_rE(r)\). Euler grades are the expansion at
\(r=0\); Todd/Bernoulli jets are the regularized expansion at \(r=1\).
Their different coefficient supports arise because they are complementary
boundary localizations, not series in one local ring. The next source object
is the compactified scale correspondence retaining both ends and their
logarithmic transition.

Reciprocity of the global scale object is computed in
`reciprocal-euler-transport-has-a-unit-current-anomaly-uniquely-centered-at-one-half.md`.
The involution \(r\mapsto r^{-1}\) exchanges the two scale sectors across
\(r=1\). The Euler potentials differ by \(\log r-i\pi\), and their oriented
currents satisfy \(-J(r^{-1})=J(r)+1\). Hence \(C=J+1/2\) is the unique
reciprocal-centered current. The unit defect is a primitive boundary current;
the constant imaginary defect is seam monodromy. Their compatibility with
the Mellin Jacobian half-density is the next source naturality gate.

The prime-labelled specialization is analyzed in
`local-reciprocal-euler-currents-are-seam-oriented-but-have-no-ordinary-global-sum.md`.
With \(r=p^{-z}\), the centered local current is
\(C_p(z)=\frac12\coth(z\log p/2)\), odd under \(z\mapsto-z\), with all of
its zeros and poles on \(\Re z=0\). But its right-chart expansion contains
the half-charge \((\log p)/2\), whose prime sum diverges. Removing that term
restores the sectoral Euler current while breaking manifest reciprocity.
Thus global assembly is necessarily relative: the primitive half-charge,
Euler tail, archimedean channel, and seam phase must remain separately typed.

The prime specialization receives a typing correction in
`the-seam-oriented-coth-current-is-a-tate-ratio-comparator-not-an-euler-factor.md`.
The coordinate \(p^{-z}\) is the square-root ratio of the two centered Tate
weights \(p^{-1/2}p^{\mp z}\), not either Euler occupation weight. Therefore
the coth current is a reciprocal comparison-frame current, and its seam
zeros are not local zeta zeros. Its oddness and divergent half-charge remain
valid, now correctly interpreted as a global normalization-line obstruction.
The next square must retain both occupation determinants and their ratio
comparator.

The ratio–product proposal is itself only birational, as proved in
`the-tate-ratio-and-product-are-birational-shadows-of-two-faithful-quadratures.md`.
At \(D_+=0\), both \(P=D_+D_-\) and \(\Gamma=D_+/D_-\) vanish for every
nonzero \(D_-\), so they erase the surviving amplitude exactly on the
divisor. The faithful coordinates are the even sum \(T=D_++D_-\) and odd
difference \(A=D_+-D_-\), which reconstruct \(D_\pm=(T\pm A)/2\). On the
seam these are the two real quadratures. All RH-bearing comparisons must be
formed before compressing this pair to ratio, product, or a scalar section.

The seam geometry of the faithful pair is computed in
`each-prime-traces-a-nonvanishing-circle-in-the-faithful-seam-quadratures.md`.
For \(B=-iA\) on \(z=it\), one has
\((T-2)^2+B^2=4/p\). Each prime therefore traces a circle centered at the
identity component \((2,0)\) with radius \(2/\sqrt p<2\), excluding the
origin and carrying zero local winding. Any completed scalar zero must arise
from cross-prime coupling, completion, or projection rather than local loss
of the faithful Tate relationship.

Finite global composition is resolved in
`no-finite-euler-cutoff-can-null-the-faithful-two-sector-amplitude.md`.
The inverse-Euler amplitudes \(F_X^\pm\) have divisor walls
\(\Re z=\mp1/2\), so they never vanish simultaneously. Their faithful
quadratures \(T_X=F_X^++F_X^-\) and \(A_X=F_X^+-F_X^-\) consequently have
no common zero anywhere, and on the seam their squared norm is
\(4|F_X^+|^2>0\). Any new divisor relevant to the completed scalar problem
must be created by infinite completion, inversion, archimedean augmentation,
regularization, or scalar projection rather than by a finite prime packet.

The first explicit lossy arrow is isolated in
`scalar-functional-equation-completion-collapses-the-faithful-odd-quadrature.md`.
For \(X(z)=\xi(1/2+z)\), scalar completion gives the diagonal pair
\((X(z),X(-z))=(X,X)\), so the odd quadrature vanishes identically and the
projective direction is frozen at \([1:1]\). Any even hostile multiplier
changes the common divisor without changing either datum. Thus scalar
functional-equation completion is a genuine quotient, not a presentation
change. RH-bearing information must reside in a pre-scalar Fourier–Tate
sewing correspondence retaining the odd comparison channel.

That pre-scalar lift is constructed in
`the-completed-theta-half-lines-form-the-canonical-pre-scalar-two-quadrature-lift.md`.
Splitting the fixed even theta source at \(u=0\) gives
\(H_+(z)\) and \(H_-(z)=H_+(-z)\). Their even quadrature is
\(T=H_++H_-=X\), while their odd quadrature \(A=H_+-H_-\) retains the
comparison erased by scalar completion. At a scalar zero, \(A=2H_+\), so the
full relation generically survives. A true null requires the stronger common
zero \(H_+(z)=H_+(-z)=0\). The next theorem is common-zero exclusion for this
source-derived pair in both open sectors.

That exclusion is not a consequence of positivity and reflection. Three
positive half-line atoms with weights (1,3,1) give the palindromic transform
(1+3e^{dz}+e^{2dz}), whose reciprocal roots produce exact common off-seam
zeros of (H_+(z)) and (H_+(-z)). The next theorem must identify a genuinely
theta-specific modular or transport law that rejects this packet before its
zeros are inspected.

The common-null target is corrected in
`the-theta-halfline-lift-never-vanishes-but-its-scalar-projection-can.md`.
Strict decrease makes (H(z)) minimum-phase in the left half-plane; reflection
protects (H(-z)) in the right half-plane; the seam sine orientation protects
the boundary. Hence the faithful pair never vanishes anywhere. A completed
zero is instead the antipodal relation (H(-z)=-H(z)\ne0). The RH frontier is
to confine this projective incidence to the seam, not to exclude common zeros.

The strength gap is isolated in
`rh-is-one-projective-antipode-exclusion-not-full-de-branges-separation.md`.
RH asks the nonnull projective route ([H(z):H(-z)]) to avoid only
([1:-1]) off the seam. De Branges magnitude separation excludes the entire
unit circle in the ratio chart and is therefore substantially stronger. A
source law may fail global coercivity while still protecting the unique
scalar-null incidence.

The apparent fourth evaluation wall is removed in
`the-four-corner-real-reciprocal-orbit-adds-no-scalar-channel.md`. The orbit
under (z\mapsto-z) and (z\mapsto\bar z) is merely
((a,b,\bar a,\bar b)), so conjugation adds no scalar degree beyond the
faithful ((T,A)) pair. The next nonredundant rung is instead a source-level
2-cell comparing reciprocal-then-conjugate with conjugate-then-reciprocal
before labelled boundary currents are aggregated.

The first source-level audit is
`the-bare-fourier-real-2-cell-collapses-before-character-transport.md`.
Complex conjugation and Fourier transform obey
(C\mathcal F C=\mathcal F^{-1}), with discrepancy controlled by parity.
The completed theta vacuum is real, even, and Fourier-fixed, so the bare
2-cell becomes identity there. The only nontrivial candidate compares the two
paths after Mellin/character transport and before scalar readout.

The transported comparison is computed in
`the-transported-reciprocal-real-2-cell-is-an-exact-seam-detector.md`.
For (M_z\Omega=e^{zu}\Omega), reciprocal parity and real conjugation differ
by (-2e^{-i\Im(z)u}\sinh(\Re(z)u)\Omega(u)). Its norm vanishes exactly on
the critical seam. The sole missing bridge is now explicit: prove from the
labelled theta/Tate constructors that a scalar null forces this distinction
effect to vanish. Hostile sources show the implication is not universal.

The categorical form is isolated in
`rh-is-factorization-of-the-null-pullback-through-the-comparison-equalizer.md`.
The scalar readout defines a null pullback (Z_0), while the independently
derived reciprocal–real 2-cell defines an equalizer (S), proved to be the
critical seam. RH is exactly the unique factorization (Z_0\to S) over the
parameter plane. The remaining theorem must construct this factorization
from the full labelled observer before scalarization.

The strongest easy realization of that factorization is ruled out in
`the-null-to-seam-factorization-cannot-be-an-ambient-continuous-multiplier.md`.
At a simple seam zero, (d/X) tends to zero tangentially because the
comparison effect vanishes along the whole seam, but tends to a nonzero source
vector normally. Thus no continuous ambient identity (d=KX) can exist. The
factorization must live only on the null pullback and use an incidence-specific
Green or boundary-state law.

The ambient category is corrected in
`the-null-to-seam-factorization-lives-in-a-real-not-holomorphic-category.md`.
The seam is the fixed locus of (R(z)=-\bar z), an antiholomorphic
involution. The null divisor is holomorphic but (d=(P-C)M_z\Omega) is
Real-analytic and semilinear. Hence the required support factorization belongs
in a Real/equivariant boundary category; it cannot be an ambient holomorphic
divisibility or retraction theorem.

  if the common mode vanishes along the whole path, whereas scalar nullity
  supplies only an endpoint condition. The Green programme and interval-path
  lift are therefore the same remaining obstruction.

- [Pointwise common-path cancellation is incompatible with a nonconstant shared forcing](pointwise-common-path-cancellation-is-incompatible-with-a-nonconstant-shared-forcing.md)
  — closes the apparent path-lift shortcut. For the same-source reciprocal
  flows, imposing `v=-u` forces both `zu+cf=0` and `u'=0`, hence forces `f` to
  be constant when the amplitude is nonzero. The theta source is nonconstant.
  The dual forcing incidence must therefore be derived afresh, or an
  additional current must absorb the common residual.

- [Fourier--Tate reflection reverses drift, not forcing variance](fourier-tate-reflection-reverses-drift-not-forcing-variance.md)
  — audits the actual centered theta equations: reflection changes the
  spectral drift but retains the same upper-right forward forcing arrow. The
  common-path no-go therefore applies to the native reciprocal double.
  Conservative closure requires a distinct contravariant lower incidence
  `B*`; covariance between sectors cannot manufacture variance reversal.

- [The source forcing has a canonical rigged transpose before any Hilbert adjoint](the-source-forcing-has-a-canonical-rigged-transpose-before-any-hilbert-adjoint.md)
  — constructs the missing variance reversal at the correct regularity level.
  For `B_f(c)=cf` into the test space, the canonical transpose on the dual is
  `lambda -> lambda(f)`. Hilbert adjoints are metric-dependent shadows obtained
  through different Riesz embeddings. The next gate is whether every boundary
  grade embeds into the dual and whether reciprocal theta incidence realizes
  this transpose dynamically.

- [The theta test vector lies in the common domain of all three arithmetic boundary grades](the-theta-test-vector-lies-in-the-common-domain-of-all-three-arithmetic-boundary-grades.md)
  — uses the source's superexponential positive-chart decay to prove absolute
  evaluation by the non-tempered primitive current, tempered square current,
  and finite-measure connected tail. Their topologies remain distinct, but
  all lie in the domain needed for the single rigged transpose. The remaining
  gate is equality with the independently constructed reciprocal incidence.

- [Arithmetic reciprocal incidence matches the rigged transpose but is not a core for the seam](arithmetic-reciprocal-incidence-matches-the-rigged-transpose-but-is-not-a-core-for-the-seam.md)
  — proves conjugate-reflection compatibility on every primitive, square, and
  connected-tail packet using the real-even theta vector. But prime-power
  supports begin at `log 2`, so they annihilate every test function supported
  in `(0,log 2)` and cannot be a core for the continuum seam dual. The seam
  return incidence must be constructed independently.

- [Reflection gives the exact H1 seam transpose with value and flux traces](reflection-gives-the-exact-h1-seam-transpose-with-value-and-flux-traces.md)
  — constructs the missing continuum return map directly. Interval reflection
  is unitary for the `H1` graph norm, exchanges endpoint values, and preserves
  outward-normal flux while reversing coordinate derivatives. With the
  real-even theta source it intertwines the rigged transpose. Combined with
  the previously established archimedean--Poisson reciprocity theorem, this
  completes boundary transpose matching; bulk dynamical realization remains.

- [Canonical adjoint completion realizes source autocorrelation, not the Evans section](canonical-adjoint-completion-realizes-source-autocorrelation-not-the-evans-section.md)
  — separates the two lower arrows. Endpoint evaluation of the tail is linear
  in the source and produces the Evans transform; returning through the
  canonical source transpose is quadratic and produces the ordered
  autocorrelation/separation transform. They already differ for the constant
  interval source. Adjoint completion therefore constructs the curvature
  object, not a determinant realization of the Riemann section.

- [The source Hankel correspondence unifies Evans and curvature, but selfadjointness does not confine zeros](the-source-hankel-correspondence-unifies-evans-and-curvature-but-selfadjointness-does-not-confine-zeros.md)
  — constructs the larger history operator with kernel `f(q+a)`. Endpoint
  evaluation gives the Evans transform and source pairing gives the
  autocorrelation. Theta decay makes the carrier Hilbert--Schmidt and
  selfadjoint. Yet a positive two-cell real symmetric Hankel carrier has an
  off-seam endpoint zero, proving that carrier selfadjointness alone has no
  zero-confinement force.

- [Complete strict Hankel positivity does not confine the Evans character orbit](complete-strict-hankel-positivity-does-not-confine-the-evans-character-orbit.md)
  — strengthens the finite selfadjoint hostile to a positive continuous
  measure whose every ordinary and shifted Hankel moment matrix is strictly
  positive. Its Laplace readout nevertheless has the exact off-seam zero
  family `z=-log(2)+(2k+1)pi i`. Complete Stieltjes moment positivity controls
  the carrier but not the distinguished exponential character orbit; the
  missing theorem must couple that orbit to theta/modular sewing.

- [Zero confinement is not stable under positive even convolution](zero-confinement-is-not-stable-under-positive-even-convolution.md)
  — identifies an exact hostile constructor rather than another carrier. The
  positive even packet `(2/5)delta_-1+delta_0+(2/5)delta_1` multiplies every
  source transform by `1+(4/5)cosh(z)` and inserts reciprocal off-seam zeros.
  Therefore an RH-bearing source category cannot be closed under arbitrary
  positive even convolution. It must retain non-monoidal arithmetic
  provenance or a connected extraction that detects the inserted divisor.

- [The prime-square current is the first port that rejects the positive convolution hostile](the-prime-square-current-is-the-first-port-that-rejects-the-positive-convolution-hostile.md)
  — applies connected extraction to the exact hostile factor. Its primitive
  coefficient is positive, but its grade-two coefficient is `-17/8`; all
  later signs alternate. Thus the separately retained square current is not
  bookkeeping overhead: it is the first source-provenance port that rejects
  this divisor-bearing positive convolution before zeros are inspected.

- [The connected square current is the order parameter for the minimal reciprocal zero split](the-connected-square-current-is-the-order-parameter-for-the-minimal-reciprocal-zero-split.md)
  — upgrades the single hostile to the full positive reciprocal three-cell
  family. If `c` is the center-to-side ratio, then the connected square
  coefficient is `a_2=1-c^2/2` and the reciprocal-root discriminant is
  `-2(a_2+1)`. Thus `a_2=-1` is exactly the double seam collision, while
  crossing below `-1` produces the symmetric off-seam split. The primitive
  grade remains positive throughout and cannot see the bifurcation.

- [The primitive-square pair types the reciprocal split while the square current alone forgets phase and incidence](the-primitive-square-pair-types-the-reciprocal-split-while-the-square-current-alone-forgets-phase-and-incidence.md)
  — stress-tests the preceding order parameter against both reciprocal phases
  and numerator/denominator presentations. Grade two determines radial
  splitting but forgets which seam point is crossed; grade one restores that
  phase bit, while determinant incidence distinguishes a section zero from a
  carrier pole. The global bridge must therefore retain the coupled
  primitive-square packet and its incidence, not scalarize `k=2` alone.

- [The primitive-square-incidence packet is faithful for one split but not for multiple factors](the-primitive-square-incidence-packet-is-faithful-for-one-split-but-not-for-multiple-factors.md)
  — gives two positive rational three-factor packets with identical first and
  second connected grades and the same incidence, but different third grades
  and divisors. Thus the low boundary packet classifies one reciprocal
  quadratic but not a global factorization. The `k>=3` carrier connection is
  the next coherence rung distinguishing multi-factor packets that collide
  after low-current projection.

- [The RH coherence architecture is three state towers, two directed witness towers, and one mate cell](the-rh-coherence-architecture-is-three-state-towers-two-directed-witness-towers-and-one-mate-cell.md)
  — records the operator's `3+2+1` correction. Input, output, and control remain
  the three primary state towers. The overloaded comparison tower splits into
  a forward source-to-section witness and a backward null-state-to-defect
  witness. A final cutoff-natural mate/equalizer cell must identify the
  backward defect with the native input Ward packet while preserving all
  boundary grades and determinant incidence.

- [The backward Hankel witness is a rigged transpose, not an executable reverse incidence](the-backward-hankel-witness-is-a-rigged-transpose-not-an-executable-reverse-incidence.md)
  — uses the `3+2+1` variance split to retype the earlier no-path theorem.
  Endpoint evaluation pulls back through the topological transpose of the
  source Hankel history map to the source covector itself. Thus forward Evans
  construction and backward source evaluation are exact mates without an
  unauthorized tail-to-forcing feedback arrow or chosen Hilbert metric. The
  remaining cell must compare this covector to the arithmetic defect tower.

- [The prime-scale recursion constructs the backward equalizer through the square grade](the-prime-scale-recursion-constructs-the-backward-equalizer-through-the-square-grade.md)
  — retypes the exact prime recursion as the first explicit `3+2+1` mate
  cell. Eliminating the one-cell seam gives
  `(1+q)R_p-qA_1-q^2B_2=(1-q^2)F`. At a scalar zero, the prime-exclusion
  channel balances typed primitive and square seam currents rather than
  vanishing. The remaining gate is a sesquilinear or exterior-square lift of
  this linear equalizer into the arithmetic Ward defect module.

- [The scalar dagger lift of the backward equalizer is rank one and cannot recover the prime-exclusion Ward form](the-scalar-dagger-lift-of-the-backward-equalizer-is-rank-one-and-cannot-recover-the-prime-exclusion-ward-form.md)
  — constructs the canonical positive square of the linear equalizer, then
  finds its exact rank obstruction. One scalar covector yields a rank-one Gram
  form, while prime-two exclusion on labels `(1,2,3)` has rank two. The
  backward witness must therefore retain labelwise or pro-valued mates before
  dagger formation; scalarize-first cannot reach the Ward tower.

- [The labelwise backward equalizer leaves a cross-fiber augmentation kernel](the-labelwise-backward-equalizer-leaves-a-cross-fiber-augmentation-kernel.md)
  — lifts the prime recursion independently to every `p`-free valuation
  fiber. A global scalar zero kills only the augmentation of this vector
  packet, leaving a `(d-1)`-dimensional cancellation fiber at `d` retained
  bases. Thus labelwise retention repairs rank but does not authorize
  componentwise nullity. The final mate cell needs a source-derived
  cross-fiber comparison, and consequently has an internal coherence tower.

- [The augmentation bivector is faithful on the cross-fiber zero space](the-augmentation-bivector-is-faithful-on-the-cross-fiber-zero-space.md)
  — constructs the missing cross-fiber comparison in geometric algebra. For
  augmentation vector `Omega` and labelwise packet `F`, the identity
  `||Omega wedge F||^2=||Omega||^2||F||^2-|<Omega,F>|^2` recovers the full
  label norm on the scalar-null pullback. Applied to the backward equalizer,
  this yields a faithful positive bivector energy. The remaining gate is a
  cutoff-natural Green law coupling that energy to the centered real part.

- [The augmentation-bivector Green identity retains a nonzero zero-state boundary flux](the-augmentation-bivector-green-identity-retains-a-nonzero-zero-state-boundary-flux.md)
  — derives the natural exterior Green law and supplies an exact two-fiber
  canonical-tail hostile with positive off-seam real part and identically
  zero scalar augmentation. The bivector endpoint energy survives and is
  balanced by the forcing overlap. Exterior faithfulness therefore does not
  orient the seam by itself; a modular current must retype or cancel this
  specific boundary-forcing balance.

- [The primitive and square currents live in the dual of the arithmetic test rigging](the-primitive-and-square-currents-live-in-the-dual-of-the-arithmetic-test-rigging.md)
  — passes the finite Fourier-stable pro-Gram packet to the projective
  arithmetic rapid-decay completion. Augmentation, primitive, and prime-square
  rows extend by explicit cutoff-uniform estimates, but the primitive current
  is a dual covector rather than a test-state vector. The remaining completion
  gate is a common graph domain carrying the theta zero-state and the
  dual-valued Green identity without an unauthorized Riesz identification.

- [The reciprocal tail domain is a trace pullback but dual times dual has no canonical Green pairing](the-reciprocal-tail-domain-is-a-trace-pullback-but-dual-times-dual-has-no-canonical-green-pairing.md)
  — identifies each half-line maximal tail domain with `H1` and the reciprocal
  sewing with their trace pullback, canonically `H1(R)`. This closes the
  archimedean domain problem. The first genuine obstruction appears after
  arithmetic dualization: completed states and boundary currents may both be
  dual-valued, while no canonical dual-times-dual Green pairing exists. The
  next object must be a source-derived intermediate dual pair, not a chosen
  Hilbert pivot.

- [The reciprocal weighted sectors form a canonical dual pair but augmentation remains an external port](the-reciprocal-weighted-sectors-form-a-canonical-dual-pair-but-augmentation-remains-an-external-port.md)
  — replaces the forbidden dual-times-dual self-pairing by the canonical
  coefficient pairing between the complementary weighted spaces `H_a` and
  `H_-a`. Reciprocal reflection exchanges them, Mellin transport preserves
  their pairing, and the seam is their self-dual locus. Primitive and square
  currents internalize for every `a>0`, but the augmentation row does so only
  for `a>1/2`; its independent control port is therefore forced throughout
  the critical strip.

- [The theta zero-state and the Euler currents live in different towers and meet only through source incidence](the-theta-zero-state-and-the-euler-currents-live-in-different-towers-and-meet-only-through-source-incidence.md)
  — corrects the proposed common-domain construction. Theta labels, Euler
  prime-power currents, and archimedean Green states occupy different source,
  boundary, and tail towers. Their authorized composition passes through the
  transpose evaluation on the fixed theta forcing and the common scalar
  control port. This removes the artificial dual-times-dual pairing problem;
  the next exact gate is the Fourier–Tate mate square of the complete
  source-to-tail correspondence.

- [The source-evaluation square commutes but unilateral tail propagation leaves a seam cocycle](the-source-evaluation-square-commutes-but-unilateral-tail-propagation-leaves-a-seam-cocycle.md)
  — computes the corrected Fourier–Tate mate square. Source evaluation is
  covariant, but transporting an outgoing half-line tail and reconstructing
  its native reciprocal tail differ at the seam by
  `C_f(z)=A(-conjugate(z))-conjugate(A(z))`. The residual persists generically
  even on the seam. A completed scalar zero imposes an anti-diagonal endpoint
  relation rather than two zero traces. The live gate is now an independent
  modular sewing cell whose boundary equals this cocycle.

- [The seam cocycle has a universal resolvent homotopy but it is a bulk, not modular, repair](the-seam-cocycle-has-a-universal-resolvent-homotopy-but-it-is-a-bulk-not-modular-repair.md)
  — applies the resolvent identity to construct a canonical second-order
  homotopy whose endpoint is the mate-square cocycle. The construction is
  universal and occupies the full scale bulk, so it supplies no RH force by
  itself. The coherence cell has become another state tower. Theta arithmetic
  must identify, cancel, or annihilate the whole homotopy, not merely reproduce
  its endpoint value.

- [The resolvent homotopy realizes the third sector and its two directed faces](the-resolvent-homotopy-realizes-the-third-sector-and-its-two-directed-faces.md)
  — validates the operator's proposed enlargement at the analytic level. The
  direct tail, second-order resolvent homotopy, and reciprocal tail form three
  sectors; `D+z` and `D-z` are their two source-local faces; endpoint
  augmentation is the common mate. This proves the outer `3+2+1` skeleton.
  The full `3(3+2+1)+2+1` claim is reduced to lifting every arithmetic and
  archimedean boundary grade onto the homotopy sector and checking the two
  grade-preserving face maps.

- [The homotopy sector lifts the decaying boundary grades but not the constant–delta control orbit](the-homotopy-sector-lifts-the-decaying-boundary-grades-but-not-the-constant-delta-control-orbit.md)
  — performs the first inner-packet lift. Superexponential theta decay carries
  primitive, square, connected, and seam value/flux grades through the double
  resolvent and both faces. The archimedean `1 <-> delta_0` orbit has no common
  outgoing `L2` graph lift. Thus the three bulk packets must share one
  external rigged control correspondence; they cannot be three identical
  Hilbert blocks.

- [The external five-cell controller closes both resolvent-face squares](the-external-five-cell-controller-closes-both-resolvent-face-squares.md)
  — splits the controller into its Fourier-fixed theta line and four-dimensional
  boundary representation. The theta line maps to the direct, homotopy, and
  reciprocal bulk states; the remaining generators stay in typed boundary
  ports. Extending both resolvent faces by identity on those ports makes the
  two controller squares commute exactly. The full three-sector incidence
  architecture now closes; the live gate moves to its combined Green form and
  theta-specific boundary coherence.

- [The three-sector face pair is a tight frame and erases the orientation defect](the-three-sector-face-pair-is-a-tight-frame-and-erases-the-orientation-defect.md)
  — derives the exact polarization identity for the two homotopy faces. Their
  summed energy equals twice the natural homotopy energy, so the three-sector
  positive defect vanishes universally and contains no RH information. The
  discarded observable is the cross-face interference
  `Re <DK,zK>`. The next required rung is a source-derived instrument for this
  oriented pairing, not another copy of the same positive state packet.

- [The cross-face orientation is a two-port endpoint–Wronskian instrument](the-cross-face-orientation-is-a-two-port-endpoint-wronskian-instrument.md)
  — decomposes the discarded interference exactly as
  `2 Re(z)|K(0)|^2 + 4 Im(z)J(K)`. The endpoint amplitude is reciprocal-even;
  the bulk Wronskian phase current is reciprocal-odd. These independent ports
  are the minimal faithful orientation instrument. The next theta-specific
  gate is to identify `J(K)` with the primitive, square, odd-PV, or modular
  seam current before zero specialization.

- [The Wronskian orientation current is the principal-value Fourier centroid of the tail port](the-wronskian-orientation-current-is-the-principal-value-fourier-centroid-of-the-tail-port.md)
  — identifies the sheet-odd Wronskian exactly with the PV first spectral
  moment of the zero-extended tail. The endpoint jump supplies the even delta
  port while the odd PV plane supplies orientation. Thus the existing
  five-cell is observationally complete; no sixth linear generator is needed.
  The remaining theorem is a theta-specific law coupling the even endpoint
  amplitude to this odd centroid.

- [The positive-mode Wronskian is a uniformly oriented pair-separation current](the-positive-mode-wronskian-is-a-uniformly-oriented-pair-separation-current.md)
  — expands the PV/Wronskian current before label aggregation. For a positive
  exponential packet every unordered pair contributes
  `-Im(z^2)c_m c_n(alpha_m-alpha_n)^2` divided by positive resolvent weights.
  The diagonal is neutral and every pair has the same orientation. The next
  gates are the explicit endpoint-dominance inequality and whether the actual
  theta forcing lies in this positive cone after its authorized source
  differentiations.

- [The positive-mode endpoint dominance is an exact rank-two Gram factorization](the-positive-mode-endpoint-dominance-is-an-exact-rank-two-gram-factorization.md)
  — proves the remaining inequality on the positive exponential cone. The
  endpoint-minus-pair expression is the squared norm of the two-vector with
  coordinates `sum w_n(alpha_n^2-|z|^2)` and `2 Im(z) sum w_n alpha_n`.
  It is strictly positive for nonreal spectral parameters, so the complete
  cross-face orientation has the sign of `Re(z)`. Conditional zero confinement
  now holds on this source cone; the remaining transfer is theta cone
  membership.

- [The completed theta source is transverse to the positive exponential cone at the seam](the-completed-theta-source-is-transverse-to-the-positive-exponential-cone-at-the-seam.md)
  — closes that transfer gate negatively at the first two seam jets. A smooth
  positive exponential mixture with zero first derivative is constant, and
  every such mixture is log-convex; the nonconstant even theta source has zero
  first derivative and strictly negative logarithmic curvature at the seam.
  The next viable extension must retain the signed `4 M_2-6 M_1` moment grades
  and their raising incidence rather than enlarge the positive scalar census.

- [The dilation coordinate turns each theta label into a positive length-three Jordan packet](the-dilation-coordinate-turns-each-theta-label-into-a-positive-length-three-jordan-packet.md)
  — rotates the signed moment readout into its source-native dilation frame.
  With `y=e^(2u)-1` and the common quarter-density removed, every label is a
  positive combination of `1,y,y^2` times `exp(-pi n^2 y)`. Positivity follows
  from the arithmetic wall `pi n^2>3/2`, and differentiation closes in a
  three-dimensional Jordan block. The remaining gate is the Green form after
  transporting the logarithmic tail operator into this frame.

- [The quarter-density makes the dilation generator skew but restores the infinite raising wall](the-quarter-density-makes-the-dilation-generator-skew-but-restores-the-infinite-raising-wall.md)
  — performs that transport exactly. The quarter-density produces the unique
  half-connection making `2(1+y) partial_y+1/2` skew modulo seam evaluation,
  but the same generator sends every top polynomial grade to the next one
  with coefficient `-2 lambda`. The positive three-state source packet is not
  a finite physical invariant module; it generates an infinite control tower.

- [The infinite dilation control tower closes as a weighted Sobolev boundary system](the-infinite-dilation-control-tower-closes-as-a-weighted-sobolev-boundary-system.md)
  — separates finite nonclosure from analytic completion. The quarter-density
  map is unitary and conjugates the full tridiagonal tower to ordinary
  logarithmic translation. Its pulled-back `H^1` graph domain is closed, seam
  evaluation is a continuous trace, and every theta label is an analytic
  vector because it extends through the strip `|Im u|<pi/4`. The remaining
  gate is reciprocal-sheet comparison, not archimedean tower completion.

- [Reciprocal Sobolev doubling cancels the zero-state seam flux but leaves one oscillatory bulk current](reciprocal-sobolev-doubling-cancels-the-zero-state-seam-flux-but-leaves-one-oscillatory-bulk-current.md)
  — constructs that comparison. A scalar zero gives anti-diagonal endpoint
  data, so the oppositely oriented seam norms cancel exactly. The summed Green
  identity retains one bulk term `2 Re <f,G_- - G_+>`, whose source expansion
  has kernel `-4 sinh(a d) cos(t d)`. Completion and boundary typing are now
  closed; the remaining RH content is theta-specific orientation of this
  oscillatory separation current.

- [The reciprocal tails are one forced Dirac system and a zero is its symmetric Dirichlet state](the-reciprocal-tails-are-one-forced-dirac-system-and-a-zero-is-its-symmetric-dirichlet-state.md)
  — rotates the two sheet tails into symmetric and antisymmetric channels.
  The scalar zero is exactly `P(0)=0`, and elimination gives the forced
  Dirichlet problem `(-partial_q^2+z^2)P=sqrt(2)f'`. The critical line is the
  locus where the exchange coupling `-z sigma_x` is skew-Hermitian. All
  remaining indefiniteness is the forcing overlap with the antisymmetric
  channel.

- [Raw Sobolev completion admits a homogeneous zero forger that the source infinity trace excludes](raw-sobolev-completion-admits-a-homogeneous-zero-forger-that-the-source-infinity-trace-excludes.md)
  — corrects the zero-to-Dirichlet equivalence. In either open half-plane one
  decaying homogeneous tail lies in `H^1` and can force the symmetric endpoint
  to zero without changing the differential equation. The Volterra source
  rejects it through a weighted infinity trace, but that trace is not closed
  in the raw graph topology. The faithful object must retain infinity
  normalization as an independent, sheet-dependent boundary port.

- [Opposite exponential sheet weights close the infinity port and turn real drift into source tilt](opposite-exponential-sheet-weights-close-the-infinity-port-and-turn-real-drift-into-source-tilt.md)
  — repairs the raw completion using the moduli of the reciprocal Mellin
  characters. Conjugation gives fixed seam operators `partial_q+it` and
  `-partial_q+it`; both homogeneous forgers become constant-modulus modes and
  leave `L^2`. The real displacement becomes opposite source tilts, reducing
  RH to strict modulus dominance between their Fourier amplitudes.

- [The opposite-weighted completion selects a two-quadrature antipode test, not full modulus dominance](the-opposite-weighted-completion-selects-a-two-quadrature-antipode-test-not-full-modulus-dominance.md)
  — corrects the final scope. Modulus dominance is sufficient but stronger
  than RH. The exact zero condition is simultaneous vanishing of the symmetric
  cosine-cosh and antisymmetric sine-sinh quadratures, equivalently arrival at
  one projective antipode. On the seam the antisymmetric quadrature vanishes by
  symmetry; off the seam RH requires only that both channels never vanish
  together.

- [The two weighted quadratures are one harmonic gradient and RH forbids its interior critical points](the-two-weighted-quadratures-are-one-harmonic-gradient-and-rh-forbids-its-interior-critical-points.md)
  — integrates the completed readout to the source potential
  `Y(z)=integral f(q)sinh(zq)/q`. Its real part vanishes identically on the
  seam, while its gradient is exactly `(C_a,-S_a)`. Thus zeros are critical
  points of one harmonic relationship field: boundary-authorized on the seam,
  but forbidden interior saddles under RH. Simple punctures have positive
  two-channel Jacobian `|X'(z)|^2/4`.

- [A simple line zero is a quadratic boundary fold while an off-line zero would be an interior branch point](a-simple-line-zero-is-a-quadratic-boundary-fold-while-an-off-line-zero-would-be-an-interior-branch-point.md)
  — gives the local normal form of the potential at a simple zero. On the seam
  the quadratic coefficient is purely imaginary, so the real-potential level
  set splits into the seam and its normal ray at ninety degrees. The potential
  squares the local coordinate and doubles angles. RH becomes confinement of
  this branch divisor to boundary folds, excluding interior branch points.

- [A seam fold has half-index per sector and reciprocal doubling restores integrality](a-seam-fold-has-half-index-per-sector-and-reciprocal-doubling-restores-integrality.md)
  — makes the integrality intuition precise. A simple boundary fold contributes
  half of the derivative winding to each reciprocal sector, and sewing restores
  unit index. Interior branch points already carry full positive index, so
  integrality alone does not exclude them. The needed theorem is a
  source-derived relative index budget saturated by the seam folds and
  infinity current.

- [The Newman threshold has a cubic local collision model but may be set at infinity](the-newman-threshold-has-a-cubic-local-collision-model-but-may-be-set-at-infinity.md)
  — identifies the universal transition `X_tau(w)=w^2-tau`: two seam folds
  collide in the cubic potential `Y_tau(w)=w^3/3-tau*w` and then leave as an
  off-seam conjugate pair. If RH holds, the Rodgers--Tao lower bound makes the
  physical source exactly the Newman threshold. The decisive correction is
  that the threshold need not be witnessed by a finite-height collision;
  cubic events may escape to spectral infinity. The missing global theorem is
  therefore a source-derived no-incoming-index-flux law at infinity.

- [Compact-open coherence cannot see a symmetric divisor escaping through infinity](compact-open-coherence-cannot-see-a-symmetric-divisor-escaping-through-infinity.md)
  — gives the exact hostile multiplier
  `Q_R(w)=(((w-R)^2+1)((w+R)^2+1))/R^4`. It carries an off-seam quartet but
  converges to one uniformly on every compact set. Hence no finite jet,
  bounded-height packet, or compact-open coherence tower can control the
  global divisor. The missing outer channel is divisor properness with a
  typed, one-sided infinity incidence—not vanishing infinity flux.

- [Aspect's germ theorem retypes divisor escape as a projective infinity germ](aspects-germ-theorem-retypes-divisor-escape-as-a-projective-infinity-germ.md)
  — applies Aspect's full-fiber and native-arity theorem to the escaping
  quartet. After projective homogenization the hostile degree-four sections
  converge to `Z^4`: the affine chart sees the constant one, while the marked
  infinity germ retains multiplicity four. The missing comparison port is
  therefore forced by carrier degree. Aspect's ternary apparatus suggests the
  next audit: Newman transport before versus after projective divisor
  completion, with affine restriction as the destructive control.

- [The primitive and augmentation currents do not form a dual--dual Green port](the-primitive-and-augmentation-currents-do-not-form-a-dual-dual-green-port.md)

- [The two boundary covectors form a canonical bivector before they form a scalar current](the-two-boundary-covectors-form-a-canonical-bivector-before-they-form-a-scalar-current.md)

- [The local valuation two-cycle is canonical but its global volume is zeta-one null](the-local-valuation-two-cycle-is-canonical-but-its-global-volume-is-zeta-one-null.md)

- [The prime-square countercurrent renormalizes the canonical boundary volume](the-prime-square-countercurrent-renormalizes-the-canonical-boundary-volume.md)

- [The archimedean dilation area is a nonvanishing reciprocal unit in the critical strip](the-archimedean-dilation-area-is-a-nonvanishing-reciprocal-unit-in-the-critical-strip.md)

- [The completed theta section is a three-channel codiagonal, not a product of local determinants](the-completed-theta-section-is-a-three-channel-codiagonal-not-a-product-of-local-determinants.md)

- [A scalar zero retains one antisymmetric channel and splits the carrier at one quarter](a-scalar-zero-retains-one-antisymmetric-channel-and-splits-the-carrier-at-one-quarter.md)

- [The faithful theta packet is a Wick-rotated hyperbolic moment pair](the-faithful-theta-packet-is-a-wick-rotated-hyperbolic-moment-pair.md)

- [The faithful theta chart lies on a source Lorentz conic](the-faithful-theta-chart-lies-on-a-source-lorentz-conic.md)

- [At a Mellin zero the symmetric value transfers to the antisymmetric current channel](at-a-mellin-zero-the-symmetric-value-transfers-to-the-antisymmetric-current-channel.md)
