  zero of the global integral does not darken those local ports; the two-cell
  witness `(+1,-1)` is exact. For the theta commutator, a scalar zero kills
  only the aggregate sine-weighted port, while the cell packet remains bright
  off seam. The remaining theorem must actively transport scalar nullity into
  the joint incidence kernel; static joint faithfulness cannot do so.

- [Static linear port refinement cannot both add information and inherit every scalar null](static-linear-port-refinement-cannot-both-add-information-and-inherit-every-scalar-null.md)
  — proves the exact obstruction behind the preceding witness. For a nonzero
  scalar functional `L`, every linear port satisfying `ker L subset ker M`
  factors through `L`. Hence no static linear refinement can both distinguish
  information erased by the scalar port and automatically darken at all of
  its zeros. The missing RH action must be a source restriction, generated
  transport, nonlinear conservation law, or higher comparison coherence—not
  an additional passive wall.

- [Team transfer audit: the active rank-one repair is covariant but not yet null transport](team-transfer-audit-the-active-rank-one-repair-is-covariant-but-not-yet-null-transport.md)
  — combines Aspect's minimum source-aligned positive balance update, Nima's
  downstream-forgetting theorem, and Strominger's dependent action witness.
  The update is exactly reciprocal-sheet covariant and changes the generator
  upstream, but its repaired determinant is `f^2>0`; it repairs local
  colligation feasibility without propagating a completed scalar zero into
  the full incidence kernel. The best transfer is to seek a source-authorized
  active connection whose solution law transports the null boundary
  condition. Static observation remains ruled out.

- [The parallel-kernel criterion is the exact active null-transport law](parallel-kernel-criterion-is-the-exact-active-null-transport-law.md)
  — identifies the precise differential action that would exclude zeros from
  an open sector. For parallel states `v'=Av`, the scalar-null hyperplanes of
  `L` are invariant exactly when `L'+LA=alpha L`. A zero then propagates to a
  known nonzero sector anchor, giving a contradiction. The transverse class
  of `L'+LA` modulo `L` is the finite-cutoff falsifier. The connection must be
  constructed from labelled theta/Tate data and must remain sectorwise: a
  regular extension across the seam would wrongly propagate the known seam
  zeros off it.

- [Native Mellin transport has a transverse first-moment defect](native-mellin-transport-has-a-transverse-first-moment-defect.md)
  — applies the criterion to the unfitted source flow. Horizontal Mellin
  transport has generator `A h=u h`; with scalar port `L(h)=integral h`, its
  active defect is the first moment `L A`. The two-cell witness has total
  integral zero and first moment `-1`, so the scalar-null hyperplane is not
  parallel. Any successful completed connection must cancel this transverse
  class through independently derived boundary or sewing structure. A
  correction manufactured from a splitting of `L` would be tautological.

- [Native Mellin observation closure is the infinite moment tower](native-mellin-observation-closure-is-the-infinite-moment-tower.md)
  — transfers Nima's kernel-reference theorem to the first-moment defect and
  proves that retaining one extra port is still insufficient. The Krylov
  covectors `L A^k` are all polynomial moments and are linearly independent,
  so native Mellin transport has no finite observation closure. The correct
  carrier is the already constructed weighted-Sobolev infinite tower together
  with both the finite seam trace and the independent sheet-dependent
  infinity trace. Aspect's finite `3+2+1` count and Strominger's braid witness
  remain warnings about other fibers; neither replaces this infinite closure.

- [The missing Evans information is the Hardy inner factor](the-missing-evans-information-is-the-hardy-inner-factor.md)
  — identifies exactly why canonical adjoint, autocorrelation, and Gram
  positivity cannot reconstruct the Evans divisor. A half-plane Blaschke
  factor inserts an interior zero while preserving the full boundary modulus,
  so every magnitude-only construction is blind to it. Nima's primitive phase
  reference and Strominger's path-coherence witness transfer as the missing
  inner-factor record. The revised source target is to construct and trivialize
  that record in each open sector; calling the transform outer without such a
  constructor would only rename RH.

- [An off-sector zero is a quantized Poisson phase delay](an-off-sector-zero-is-a-quantized-poisson-phase-delay.md)
  — turns the missing Hardy inner factor into an explicit boundary observable.
  A right-half-plane zero contributes the oriented phase derivative
  `-2a/(a^2+(t-b)^2)`, integrates to one winding unit, and changes no boundary
  magnitude. The residual between actual boundary phase velocity and the
  Hilbert-transform phase predicted by log-magnitude is therefore the missing
  path record. Equivalently, one may seek a source-authorized stable inverse;
  reciprocal reflection alone is not inversion.

- [The topological Möbius inverse needs a cross-label differential to survive completion](the-topological-mobius-inverse-needs-a-cross-label-differential-to-survive-completion.md)
  — transfers Nima's divisor-chamber topology into the stable-inverse target.
  Each Möbius coefficient is source-constructible as the reduced Euler
  characteristic of a proper-divisor complex, but those complexes are
  separate integer-label fibers. Their internal boundaries supply no analytic
  cancellation across `n`. A noncircular inverse programme therefore needs a
  source-derived cross-label differential with completion-stable contraction;
  the scalar supertrace should be taken only afterward.

- [A Mellin-covariant cross-label differential must vanish](a-mellin-covariant-cross-label-differential-must-vanish.md)
  — attacks the proposed differential immediately. The label Hamiltonian
  `H e_n=(log n)e_n` has simple spectrum, so every commuting operator preserves
  each `n`-fiber; a parity-changing cross-label differential commuting with
  `H` is zero. Prime attachment instead has weight `log p`. A viable complex
  must therefore include an energy-bearing seam or reservoir arrow with the
  opposite weight. The Euler product is a fermionic graded partition function,
  not yet a protected supersymmetric index.

- [Prime creation and opposite seam translation form an exact weight-zero bicomplex](prime-creation-and-opposite-seam-translation-form-an-exact-weight-zero-bicomplex.md)
  — constructs the compensating arrow demanded by the preceding no-go.
  Fermionic prime creation has weight `+log p`; backward seam translation has
  weight `-log p`; their tensor product commutes with total label-position
  energy and the finite sum squares to zero. The full-line complex is
  contractible. On the half-line its contraction fails by exactly the
  projection onto `[0,log p)`, identifying the moving seam window as the
  canonical boundary defect of one Koszul-Toeplitz complex.

- [The finite prime-seam bicomplex is flat even after one global source weight](the-finite-prime-seam-bicomplex-is-flat-even-after-one-global-source-weight.md)
  — performs the two- and three-prime hostile test. Half-line defect
  projections obey the exact cocycle
  `P_(a+b)=P_a+S_a P_b S_a^*`, so all attachment orders give the same total
  window after common refinement. Adding one global theta weight produces
  finite differences `delta_a f`, but these commute and remain iterated
  coboundaries. No finite prime cube carries inner-factor holonomy; any new
  class must enter through completion, archimedean sewing, or genuinely
  noncommutative coefficients.

- [The fixed-prime homotopy survives graph completion](the-fixed-prime-homotopy-survives-graph-completion.md)
  — closes the simplest completion-anomaly hope. The algebraic homotopy
  satisfies `d h_p+h_p d=1-E_p` with bounded `h_p` and moving-window
  projection `E_p`. The identity itself bounds `d h_p` in the graph norm, so
  `h_p` extends to the graph closure uniformly. Infinite primes cannot create
  new cohomology away from the already explicit seam window. Euler-product
  trace divergence is therefore not cohomology of this complex.

- [Invertible archimedean or reciprocal sewing cannot create the missing class](invertible-archimedean-or-reciprocal-sewing-cannot-create-the-missing-class.md)
  — transports the homotopy through an arbitrary completed-domain
  isomorphism: conjugation preserves `d h+h d=1-E`. Fourier, reciprocal,
  metaplectic, exponential-weight, and output-frame operations cannot create
  RH cohomology when they are genuinely invertible. The only surviving locus
  is a nonexact operation such as a domain intersection, boundary pullback,
  quotient, or relative completion. The natural candidate is the pullback of
  the two individually contractible sector complexes over their interface.

- [The derived sector pullback is the shifted interface](derived-sector-pullback-is-the-shifted-interface.md)
  — computes the candidate exactly. For contractible sector complexes, the
  homotopy pullback over the common interface `B` is quasi-isomorphic to
  `B[-1]`. Any class created by two-sector sewing is therefore shifted
  interface cohomology, not new bulk information. The next RH gate is the
  source-derived construction and off-seam acyclicity of `B_s`.

- [The interface complex is normalized source boundary data](the-interface-complex-is-normalized-source-boundary-data.md)
  — transfers the team's latest mechanisms into the RH lane. The interface
  should be a frozen simplicial or Čech-type boundary object whose genuine
  residues are simultaneous face cycles modulo source-authorized fillings.
  Adaptive cells are forbidden; wall-only cancellation is insufficient; and
  scalar detection requires coherent target/control comparison with a proved
  common mode.

- [The RH interface needs phase-framed local coefficients](the-rh-interface-needs-phase-framed-local-coefficients.md)
  — removes the hidden scalar-coefficient assumption. Source routes may mix
  amplitude fibers non-diagonally, and phase-sensitive control cannot be
  reconstructed after projective quotienting. The candidate is therefore a
  normalized interface complex with source-derived local transport
  `N(I_s; rho_s)`, built before determinant scalarization.

- [The smallest controlled local Tate carrier has three states and is flat](the-smallest-controlled-local-tate-carrier-has-three-states-and-is-flat.md)
  — derives a native finite local coefficient system. The normalized
  valuation-ball states `b_-1, b_0, b_1` are closed under local Fourier
  transform; the outer pair is exchanged and the unramified middle state is
  a fixed common-mode control. The transport is involutive, so the one-place
  twisted interface is flat and cannot carry the RH obstruction.

- [The first nonflat interface is the Fourier–trace descent defect](the-first-nonflat-interface-is-the-fourier-trace-descent-defect.md)
  — isolates the exact quotient obstruction `D = tau F | ker(tau)`. When it
  vanishes, Fourier descends and remains flat; on an injective trace range it
  is merely conjugated Fourier. When it does not vanish, the erased trace
  kernel must be retained as an interface sector. The current injective trace
  construction moves the RH-bearing locus to the later completion, Mellin,
  restricted-product, or determinant boundary.

- [Unilateral polarization is the first nonexact RH interface map](unilateral-polarization-is-the-first-nonexact-rh-interface-map.md)
  — locates that later boundary precisely. Flat Fourier sewing and reciprocal
  source evaluation cease to commute only after half-line projection:
  reflection exchanges `P_+` and `P_-`. The known seam cocycle is the endpoint
  shadow of this noncommutation, and a completed zero is an anti-diagonal
  augmentation state rather than two unilateral zero states.

- [The unilateral seam cocycle is filled by the Mellin orbit before completion](the-unilateral-seam-cocycle-is-filled-by-the-mellin-orbit-before-completion.md)
  — proves that the explicit post-polarization cocycle is the boundary of the
  source-derived reciprocal character orbit. It is therefore not an
  algebraic interface residue. The surviving RH locus is non-descent of this
  canonical filling under the singular boundary completion, especially in
  the primitive and prime-square channels.

- [Moving-weight fibers make the Mellin filling unitary](moving-weight-fibers-make-the-mellin-filling-unitary.md)
  — removes a false completion obstruction. Exponential Mellin transport is
  unbounded only when different spectral weights are forced into one fixed
  `L^2` fiber; between the natural weighted fibers it is unitary and flat.
  The live analytic obstruction moves to endpoint evaluation, graph-domain
  intersection, and restricted-product exactness.

- [The covariant graph norm makes the endpoint pullback strict](the-covariant-graph-norm-makes-the-endpoint-pullback-strict.md)
  — closes the scalar endpoint gate. The weighted covariant derivative
  `nabla_a=f'+af` makes every graph domain unitarily equivalent to ordinary
  `H^1`; endpoint evaluation is uniformly bounded, Mellin transport preserves
  it, and the anti-diagonal plus/minus pullback is closed and complemented.
  The next live gate requires arithmetic boundary coordinates or the
  operator-valued Green response.

- [The RH completion gate is authorized quasidiagonality over a resolved corner](the-rh-completion-gate-is-authorized-quasidiagonality-over-a-resolved-corner.md)
  — transfers Nima's cutoff-leakage theorem, Aspect's resolved-direction
  falsifier, and Benincasa's endpoint-sewing order. The exact global gate is
  decay of the two moving cutoff leakage blocks for the source-authorized
  filtration, tested uniformly over the resolved joint corner of spectral

  typed primitive or square boundary port.

- [The global observer lift precedes authorized quasidiagonality](the-global-observer-lift-precedes-authorized-quasidiagonality.md)
  — corrects the gate ordering. Continuous log-window Fourier and finite
  arithmetic sheet transport live on different carriers and give opposite

  the global additive-to-boundary observer lift and prove componentwise
  Fourier–Poisson naturality in the primitive, square, tail, seam, and
  archimedean grades. Quasidiagonality is the second gate.

- [The square observer lives on the cyclic two-copy source](the-square-observer-lives-on-the-cyclic-two-copy-source.md)
  — retypes that observer lift. The primitive trace is linear on the one-copy
  source, while the square current linearizes only on its symmetric cyclic
  two-copy lift; its polarization term is mandatory. Prime dilation transports
  both copy carriers, but the source-derived Fourier action on the completed
  cyclic tower remains the first missing global cell.

- [Fourier exchanges two cyclic valuation towers](fourier-exchanges-two-cyclic-valuation-towers.md)
  — resolves the type of that missing action. Fourier reverses normalized
  dilation, so it preserves cyclic arity but exchanges the positive and
  negative valuation cones. The minimal stable label object is the
  Grothendieck completion of the prime monoid, and observer naturality must be
  a relative correspondence between two polarized cyclic towers rather than
  an endomorphism of one positive Euler tower.

- [The Toeplitz boundary index is the Euler logarithmic derivative](the-toeplitz-boundary-index-is-the-euler-logarithmic-derivative.md)
  — identifies the arithmetic content of unilateral polarization. A depth-`k`
  valuation shift loses `k` boundary states, cancelling the connected Euler
  weight `1/k`; Mellin differentiation then gives the uniform von Mangoldt
  weight `log p` at every prime power. This explains the local current while
  leaving its two-cone archimedean sewing as the RH-bearing gate.

- [The two-cone Toeplitz current is relative flux, not unsigned density](the-two-cone-toeplitz-current-is-relative-flux-not-unsigned-density.md)
  — distinguishes three formerly conflated sewings: direct-sum density,
  vacuum-identified union, and oriented anti-diagonal flux. The logarithmic
  derivative selects the last. Its total index cancels under Fourier while
  its relative boundary class survives; the shared vacuum is seam incidence,
  and the live gate is its archimedean pairing.

- [The Mellin position row positively observes relative prime flux](the-mellin-position-row-positively-observes-relative-prime-flux.md)
  — constructs that finite archimedean pairing. An even theta scalar kills the
  odd flux `delta_L-delta_-L`, while its source-derived position row pairs as

  completion and the zero-state bridge remain open.

- [The critical primitive current escapes the Mellin–de Rham graph dual](the-critical-primitive-current-escapes-the-mellin-derham-graph-dual.md)
  — resolves that uniformity gate negatively and corrects the preceding
  scope. A translated-shell witness has graph norm `O(R)` but primitive

- [The sheet-aware comoving lift fixes support, not prime multiplicity](the-sheet-aware-comoving-lift-fixes-support-not-prime-multiplicity.md)
  — constructs that comoving coordinate with reciprocal typing. Fourier acts
  by `(epsilon,u) -> (-epsilon,-u)` and every paired prime-power boundary lies

- [The primitive current is the connection of the cutoff anomaly line](the-primitive-current-is-the-connection-of-the-cutoff-anomaly-line.md)
  — gives that exponential grade its correct completion type. Finite primitive
  cumulants exponentiate to nowhere-zero cutoff transition units satisfying
