  von Mangoldt current. Primitive, square, and tail are respectively line
  connection, graph-grade transition, and determinant-three section.

- [The cutoff anomaly connection is flat and divisor-blind](the-cutoff-anomaly-connection-is-flat-and-divisor-blind.md)
  — corrects the stale next-gate pointer above. Reciprocal and archimedean
  descent already close, while every finite primitive or square transition is
  a global nowhere-zero exponential. Its connection has zero residue and no
  divisor support. The anomaly tower is therefore a flat provenance vessel,
  not the zero-confining current. The live gate is a source-derived coupling
  of the four-channel additive Poisson section to a phase-sensitive
  connection that detects inner-factor winding and survives the primitive and
  square completion grades.

- [Reciprocal symmetry neutralizes total divisor charge but preserves relative charge](reciprocal-symmetry-neutralizes-total-divisor-charge-but-preserves-relative-charge.md)
  — identifies the missing section-sensitive comparison channel. A reciprocal
  off-seam zero pair has opposite sectorwise winding in a common seam frame:
  its codiagonal charge vanishes while its anti-diagonal charge is twice the
  multiplicity. Functional-equation symmetry therefore hides, rather than
  forbids, a neutral off-seam divisor. The required source object is a
  sector-resolved phase observer modulo the exact flat anomaly connections.

- [The anti-diagonal Hardy residual is the minimal divisor observer](the-antidiagonal-hardy-residual-is-the-minimal-divisor-observer.md)
  — constructs that observer under the standard sectorwise bounded-type
  hypotheses. Subtracting the outer phase predicted by boundary magnitude
  leaves reciprocal residuals `kappa_+` and `kappa_-=-kappa_+`; the diagonal
  vanishes and the anti-diagonal equals twice the Blaschke current. Modulo
  exact zero-residue anomaly-frame currents, this is the smallest boundary
  port that detects hostile off-seam divisor packets. The remaining theorem
  is source-derived vanishing of this relative class and exclusion of a
  singular-inner boundary measure.

- [Endpoint-reduced Euler sections are the canonical Hardy carriers](endpoint-reduced-euler-sections-are-the-canonical-hardy-carriers.md)
  — closes the analytic proviso on that observer. The raw completed section
  has excessive gamma growth; the source-derived carriers
  `E_+=(s-1)zeta(s)/s` and `E_-=E_+(1-s)` retain exactly the open-sector
  divisor and are of bounded type. Analytic continuation through the finite
  seam and zero mean type remove the singular-inner ambiguity. The relative
  Hardy current is consequently an exact RH gate; what remains is to derive
  its vanishing from labelled theta--Poisson transport.

- [Sector phase charge is conserved only after retaining the infinity caps](sector-phase-charge-is-conserved-only-after-retaining-the-infinity-caps.md)
  — derives the exact finite-rectangle transport law for the new Hardy port.
  Moving from the zero-free Euler chamber toward the seam changes phase charge
  only through a divisor vortex or through the horizontal infinity caps.
  Conjugation doubles those caps rather than cancelling them. The complete
  comparison object is therefore the anti-diagonal phase current together
  with an independent projective-infinity boundary current.

- [The RH defect is the gap between total infinity charge and seam-fold charge](the-rh-defect-is-the-gap-between-total-infinity-charge-and-seam-fold-charge.md)
  — combines the infinity-cap law with the established half-index theorem.
  Reciprocal pairing gives the exact nonnegative integer defect
  `N_tot(T)-N_seam(T)=2N_+(T)`. The projective-infinity tower carries total
  divisor supply, while the seam germ carries realized boundary folds. RH is
  the assertion that the latter saturates the former at every height. The
  missing constructor is a source-derived, multiplicity-preserving saturation
  map, not another scalar positivity probe.

- [Off-seam divisor charge is the diffuse part of the seam phase measure](off-seam-divisor-charge-is-the-diffuse-part-of-the-seam-phase-measure.md)
  — upgrades the saturation count to a localized measure identity. Completed
  boundary reality and Hardy factorization split the source-visible phase
  supply into atomic seam-fold measure plus positive Poisson profiles from
  open-sector zeros. As an off-seam zero approaches the line its diffuse
  profile collapses to the boundary atom. RH becomes pure atomicity of the
  completed divisor-supply measure; the falsifier is any surviving diffuse
  Poisson component.

- [The divisor supply has a positive atomic--diffuse Lebesgue decomposition](the-divisor-supply-has-a-positive-atomic-diffuse-lebesgue-decomposition.md)
  — proves the first universal positivity theorem in the phase lane. The
  source-visible supply measure is the sum of the atomic seam multiplicity
  measure and positive Poisson densities from every right-sector zero. One
  off-seam zero gives a nonzero diffuse signal on every seam interval. RH is
  not positivity of this measure, which is unconditional, but vanishing of
  its uniquely defined diffuse component.

- [Horizontal zero displacement is Poisson time and RH is compact resolvent](horizontal-zero-displacement-is-poisson-time-and-rh-is-compact-resolvent.md)
  — Fourier-rotates the atomic--diffuse theorem. A zero at horizontal distance
  `a` contributes an undamped frequency multiplied by `exp(-a|x|)`; equivalently
  its boundary profile is a seam atom evolved for Poisson time `a`. The
  positive supply measure defines a canonical self-adjoint multiplication
  operator unconditionally. RH is exactly compact resolvent and pure-point
  spectral type. The missing Hilbert--Polya property is therefore
  source-derived compactness, not self-adjointness.

- [RH is almost periodicity of the regularized divisor-resolution signal](rh-is-almost-periodicity-of-the-regularized-divisor-resolution-signal.md)
  — gives a harmonic source-facing form of compactness. After polynomial
  weighting, atomic seam supply Fourier-transforms to an absolutely
  convergent Bohr almost-periodic character sum, while diffuse off-seam supply
  transforms to a function vanishing at infinity. Since `AP intersect C_0`
  is zero, RH is exactly almost periodicity of the regularized signal. The
  live theorem is preservation of this category by labelled theta--Euler
  completion from the discrete Mellin-character chamber.

- [The half-offset is the first authorized decay threshold](the-half-offset-is-the-first-authorized-decay-threshold.md)
  — corrects literal almost-periodic preservation. Endpoint pole cancellation
  and gamma completion already create declared decaying modes, but their
  nearest rate is exactly `1/2`, the distance from the seam to `s=0,1`.
  Every nontrivial off-line zero produces a slower rate in `(0,1/2)`. The
  correct completion category is almost-periodic signal plus preregistered
  endpoint/archimedean channels, with RH equivalent to absence of a positive
  sub-threshold decay component.

- [The BSY integral is total diffuse divisor entropy](the-bsy-integral-is-total-diffuse-divisor-entropy.md)
  — identifies the one-scalar collapse of the positive decay-gap problem with
  the classical Balazard--Saias--Yor identity. Each right-sector zero adds a
  strictly positive, height-weighted horizontal-displacement cost, so the
  integral is a faithful scalarization of the diffuse divisor measure. The
  live source theorem is the opposite inequality forcing this nonnegative
  entropy to vanish; no infinite localizer census is needed.

- [The BSY bound is an Euler renormalization anomaly](the-bsy-bound-is-an-euler-renormalization-anomaly.md)
  — computes the scale-one-half Cauchy expectation of every finite Euler
  logarithm exactly: it is the positive Mertens sum and diverges as
  `log log X + gamma`. The finite Euler limit therefore cannot be passed
  through logarithm, critical-boundary continuation, completion, and
  expectation. The remaining source object is a boundary-bearing
  renormalization commutator whose typed residual must equal and orient the
  finite BSY entropy.

- [RH is commutativity of the Mertens--completion square](rh-is-commutativity-of-the-mertens-completion-square.md)
  — finite Euler reconstruction and Cauchy pairing produce the positive
  Mertens sum, whose independently normalized finite part is zero. Analytic
  completion followed by critical-boundary expectation gives the BSY entropy.
  Their discrepancy is exactly the positive off-seam divisor sum. RH is thus
  one explicit compatibility theorem between finite labelled reconstruction
  and theta--Tate completion.

- [The Mertens finite part is cutoff-covariant, not absolute](the-mertens-finite-part-is-cutoff-covariant-not-absolute.md)
  — a power reparameterization of the Euler cutoff manufactures a false
  `log alpha` anomaly unless the counterterm is evaluated on the actual
  source norm scale. The finite part is therefore a relative scalar on a
  scale-bearing Euler boundary. This identifies the primitive current as the
  carrier of the counterterm origin and removes regulator artifacts without
  removing the BSY divisor anomaly.

- [Euler's constant sews the three prime-current grades](eulers-constant-sews-the-three-prime-current-grades.md)
  — decomposes `gamma` exactly into the primitive Meissel--Mertens constant,
  the positive prime-square term, and the absolutely convergent connected
  tail. These are precisely the `k=1`, `k=2`, and `k>=3` channels. This closes
  the scalar normalization target for a source-derived third-order relative
  determinant while leaving its operator construction and completion
  compatibility open.

- [Cauchy observation lowers the Euler Schatten threshold](cauchy-observation-lowers-the-euler-schatten-threshold.md)
  — constructs the raw diagonal half-density operator and proves that the
  scale-one-half Cauchy observer maps each of its powers to the corresponding
  power of `diag(p^-1)`. The raw carrier needs order-three regularization;
  the observed carrier needs only order two. This prevents the scalar grades
  of Entry 3924 from being misread as raw traces and identifies the live RH
  cell as observer--completion interchange.

- [The Cauchy observer fails multiplicativity only across sectors](the-cauchy-observer-fails-multiplicativity-only-across-sectors.md)
  — the Cauchy character is exactly multiplicative on each one-sided Mellin
  cone, while every opposite-sign two-step word has a strictly positive
  triangle-inequality defect. Hence neither isolated Euler chart can carry
  the completion anomaly: it is supported on mixed Fourier--Tate sewing
  words. The live target is to pair this universal positive kernel with the
  source-derived mixed-word coefficients and recover the BSY entropy.

- [The mixed Cauchy defect is a Dirichlet Green kernel](the-mixed-cauchy-defect-is-a-dirichlet-green-kernel.md)
  — upgrades pairwise positivity to a positive-definite theorem: the
  opposite-sector Cauchy defect is exactly the Dirichlet Green kernel of
  `-d^2/dx^2 + 1/4` on the half-line. At prime frequencies it is the Gram
  kernel of nested interval indicators. Its strict positivity also rules out
  identifying the raw mixed energy with the BSY entropy; the latter must be a
  relative Green bulk minus a source-derived modular boundary repair.

- [The prime-square current is the rank-one seam image](the-prime-square-current-is-the-rank-one-seam-image.md)
  — identifies the mixed kernel as a method-of-images resolvent. The seam
  correction is the rank-one form `|sum_p c_p/sqrt(p)|^2`; its diagonal is
  exactly the prime-square current and its off-diagonal entries retain
  cross-prime coherence. This separates the universal Dirichlet repair from
  the still-missing modular repair and exposes a concrete double-counting
  hazard.

- [The two-prime cross term forces seam synthesis before squaring](the-two-prime-cross-term-forces-seam-synthesis-before-squaring.md)
  — the full seam form contains `1/sqrt(pq)` cross-prime coefficients that no
  direct sum of prime-local quadratic currents can produce. The source must
  synthesize half-density amplitudes globally before taking their norm.
  Squaring first leaves only the diagonal prime-square current, so the two
  operation orders differ by an explicit comparison capability.

- [The mixed Green kernel is Cauchy character covariance](the-mixed-green-kernel-is-cauchy-character-covariance.md)
  — realizes the Dirichlet kernel as the covariance of unitary Mellin
  characters under the canonical scale-one-half Cauchy law. The missing
  phase-bearing codiagonal is Cauchy expectation, and the seam subtraction is
  orthogonal projection off the constant mode. The unresolved gate is now
  nonlinear: whether Euler exponentiation and theta--Tate completion descend
  through this centering operation with the BSY residual.

- [Cauchy observation commutes with finite Euler exponentiation](cauchy-observation-commutes-with-finite-euler-exponentiation.md)
  — closes the finite nonlinear anomaly candidate. At every finite prime
  cutoff, Cauchy expectation sends both the Euler logarithm and Euler product
  exactly to their value at `s=1`; expectation even commutes with
  exponentiation. The first nontrivial defect is therefore forced into the
  divergent infinite limit, renormalization, continuation, and reciprocal
  completion step.

- [The normalized Euler vacuum is a Calkin corona state](the-normalized-euler-vacuum-is-a-calkin-corona-state.md)
  — transfers Nima's two-port escape theorem into an exact operator statement.
  Normalized prime-harmonic vectors converge weakly to zero, so every
  vector-state cluster annihilates compact operators while retaining unit
  mass. The missing completion datum is therefore a singular state on the
  Calkin image of the source-authorized comparison algebra, not another
  trace-class boundary correction.

- [The prime-harmonic corona state is Haar on the Mellin algebra](the-prime-harmonic-corona-state-is-haar-on-the-mellin-algebra.md)
  — restricts the nonunique Calkin cluster states to the source-derived
  C*-algebra of vertical Mellin transports. Prime-harmonic averaging kills
  every nonzero frequency and retains the constant mode, so all clusters
  induce the unique Haar state on the Bohr phase boundary. This is a positive
  invariant state, not a multiplicative character; the next gate is its
  extension across one non-diagonal source constructor.

- [The Haar corona retains Mellin shifts but destroys their generator](the-haar-corona-retains-mellin-shifts-but-destroys-their-generator.md)
  — computes the GNS geometry of the canonical corona state. Distinct Mellin
  translates of the vacuum are orthogonal, so the parameter group becomes
  `R_d`; finite shifts remain unitary but the action is not strongly
  continuous and has no infinitesimal generator. This is the first exact
  module-closure failure and identifies the missing channel as a
  source-derived first-order vertical germ.

- [The Euler vacuum escapes every fixed Mellin graph norm](the-euler-vacuum-escapes-every-fixed-mellin-graph-norm.md)
  — tests the missing generator on the normalized arithmetic vacuum. Even
  after optimal scalar centering, logarithmic-label variance diverges as
  `(log X)^2/(2 log log X)`. A rescaling that normalizes the first derivative
  makes the standardized third moment diverge. The primitive channel is
  therefore a filtered or distributional jet tower, not one Hilbert graph
  correction.

- [The prime-harmonic tangent blowup restores the Mellin generator](the-prime-harmonic-tangent-blowup-restores-the-mellin-generator.md)
  — rescales prime position by `y=log p/log X`. The unnormalized harmonic
  measures converge vaguely to `dy/y`, whose positive moments are exactly
  `1/r` while grade zero diverges. Microscopic shifts `t=tau/log X` yield a
  strongly continuous tangent action with generator `y`. The primitive
  completion is therefore a two-level corona-plus-tangent object, not one
  graph Hilbert space.

- [The completed Euler boundary is finite-part dy/y plus gamma delta-zero](the-completed-euler-boundary-is-finite-part-dy-over-y-plus-gamma-delta-zero.md)
  — identifies the Mertens finite part as the connecting coefficient between
  the divergent grade-zero corona and the `dy/y` tangent chart. Absolutely
  summable square and higher currents collapse to the endpoint, changing the
  delta coefficient from `B1` to `gamma`. The resulting scalar distribution
  retains every jet moment but remains phase-blind, forcing a doubled
  Fourier--Tate endpoint incidence as the next gate.

- [The first Hermite port points the Mellin endpoint but does not yet orient the Tate sheets](the-first-hermite-port-points-the-tate-endpoint-and-orients-the-sheets.md)
  — answers Nima's translation-stabilizer test on the full source carrier.
  The odd Hermite state separates the endpoint from every nonzero translate,
  and survives rigged completion. Its phase `-i` belongs to ordinary Fourier
  transform in the logarithmic coordinate, not the additive Tate Fourier
  transform. Endpoint pointing survives; sheet orientation remains open.

- [The logarithmic Hermite phase is not the Tate sheet phase](the-logarithmic-hermite-phase-is-not-the-tate-sheet-phase.md)
  — records the coordinate-typing correction explicitly. The logarithmic
  Fourier transform is Mellin spectral duality, while Tate sewing uses
  additive Fourier transform before logarithmic pullback. The actual
  conjugated Tate operator is nonlocal in the log coordinate, so its action on
  the endpoint witness must be derived rather than inferred from Hermite
  eigenphases.

- [Additive Tate Fourier is a Hankel operator in logarithmic coordinates](additive-tate-fourier-is-a-hankel-operator-in-logarithmic-coordinates.md)
  — derives the correctly typed sheet transport. Even and odd additive lifts
  give cosine and sine kernels depending on the sum of logarithmic
  coordinates. The even transform of the first Hermite witness has an
  `exp(r/2)` left tail and therefore cannot be a Hermite eigenvector. Endpoint
  pointing and Tate sheet orientation are separate rungs.

- [The positive Mellin ray has two additive Tate lifts](the-positive-mellin-ray-has-two-additive-tate-lifts.md)
  — identifies the hidden binary port. Even and odd additive extensions of one
  positive-ray state obey `K_+^2=I` and `K_-^2=-I`. The endpoint-pointing
  logarithmic Hermite witness and the phase-bearing additive Gaussian
  derivative are different states. A source-derived incidence between those
  ports, rather than a coordinate identification, is now the missing gate.

- [The archimedean Tate phase is a gamma multiplier times reflection](the-archimedean-tate-phase-is-a-gamma-multiplier-times-reflection.md)
  — logarithmic Fourier transform turns each Tate Hankel operator into
  spectral reflection followed by the corresponding even or odd gamma
  multiplier. The squared parity laws become exact gamma-cocycle identities.
  The phase is frequency-dependent and source-normalized, not a constant
  Hermite phase.

- [The critical line is the equal-weight locus of the two additive Tate lifts](the-critical-line-is-the-equal-weight-locus-of-the-two-additive-tate-lifts.md)
  — the parity-channel ratio is `-i tan(pi s/2)`. In the critical strip its
  modulus equals one exactly at `Re(s)=1/2`; the two open half-strips are the
  opposite dominance chambers. This explains the half-offset independently of
  zeros. RH still requires the new implication that an authorized zero-state
  must balance the two parity channels.

- [Global character descent selects one Tate parity channel](global-character-descent-selects-one-tate-parity-channel.md)
  — supplies the hostile source-authority correction. The trivial global zeta
  character forces even archimedean parity; the odd local lift does not belong
  to the same global scalar section without compensating finite or boundary
  character data. The equal-weight wall is exact local geometry, not yet an RH
  mechanism.

- [The orientation line restores the odd Tate port without changing global character](the-orientation-line-restores-the-odd-tate-port-without-changing-global-character.md)
  — resolves the parity obstruction at the graded level. For an even source,
  the odd derivative coefficient tensored with the odd orientation form is
  globally even. The local parity double can therefore live in one trivial-
  character source complex, but only before scalar projection erases form
  degree. Global completion and the zero-to-state bridge remain open.

- [The local even--odd Tate complex is strictly exact](the-local-even-odd-tate-complex-is-strictly-exact.md)
  — differentiation is a topological isomorphism from even Schwartz functions
  to odd Schwartz one-forms, with endpoint integration as inverse. The two
  parity ports are exact adjacent grades, not independent scalar channels.
  Any unpaired state must therefore be created by global completion or
  boundary sewing; the zero-to-cohomology bridge remains open.

- [The local valuation complex has one augmentation boundary](the-local-valuation-complex-has-one-augmentation-boundary.md)
  — gives the finite-place analogue. The discrete derivative `I-S_p` has the
  coefficient sum as its sole cokernel, while `I-p^(-s)S_p` has an explicit
  geometric resolvent. Direct and reciprocal local complexes are contractible
  throughout the critical strip. The primitive current is the augmentation
  boundary; any unpaired state is irreducibly global.

- [The two Euler Hilbert vacua meet by simultaneous escape on the critical line](the-two-euler-hilbert-vacua-meet-by-simultaneous-escape-on-the-critical-line.md)
  — computes the global state norms exactly as `zeta(2 Re(s))` and
  `zeta(2-2 Re(s))`. The direct and reciprocal endpoint-normalized vacua live
  in complementary open half-planes and both leave Hilbert space on the
  critical line. This explains the line as a simultaneous-loss sewing wall;
  the zero-to-relative-defect bridge remains missing.

- [The local Euler factor is a boundary transfer, not a determinant](the-local-euler-factor-is-a-boundary-transfer-not-a-determinant.md)
  — closes the naive cohomology bridge. Every finite twisted valuation
  differential has determinant one, while its endpoint-to-augmentation
  transfer is the truncated Euler factor. Zeta is therefore natively a scalar
  transfer coefficient of an exact system. A completed conservation or
  minimality theorem, not internal exactness alone, must forbid off-wall
  transmission zeros.

- [The bordered valuation system has all transmission zeros on its stability wall](the-bordered-valuation-system-has-all-transmission-zeros-on-its-stability-wall.md)
  — restores a noncircular zero-to-kernel bridge by retaining the endpoint
  input and augmentation output. The bordered determinant is
  `1+a+...+a^N`; every transmission zero is a nontrivial root of unity and
  lies on `|a|=1`. Equal source augmentation, not generic conservativity,
  forces the local wall theorem. The global cross-prime analogue remains open.

- [Half-density retyping pushes every local Euler zero outside the critical stability disk](half-density-retyping-pushes-every-local-euler-zero-outside-the-critical-stability-disk.md)
  — corrects the wall type. In the source Hilbert coordinate
  `b=p^(1/2-s)`, augmentation weights become `p^(-k/2)`, and all finite local
  transfer zeros satisfy `|b|=sqrt(p)>1`. No prime-local zero reaches the RH
  wall. Any critical-line zero is irreducibly a completed cross-place effect.

- [Hurwitz forces zeta zeros into the completion operation, not the finite Euler systems](hurwitz-forces-zeta-zeros-into-the-completion-operation-not-the-finite-euler-systems.md)
  — a locally uniform limit of zero-free finite Euler transfers times
  nowhere-zero units cannot acquire isolated zeros. Aspect's two-prime hostile
  shows that naive scalar addition fails in the opposite direction by creating
  off-wall zeros. The required completion must retain cross-prime incidence
  and add typed boundary state before scalar compression.

- [Theta completion replaces factorized augmentation by a joint Gaussian observation](theta-completion-replaces-factorized-augmentation-by-a-joint-gaussian-observation.md)
  — identifies the missing operation explicitly. The theta weight is a
  nonlinear function of the total labelled log-scale and has nonzero mixed
  derivatives between every prime pair. Completion synthesizes integer labels
  before Gaussian observation and Poisson sewing; it is not a benign limit of
  factorized Euler output rows. Zero confinement remains a sewing theorem.

- [Integrality makes the rank-one theta log coordinate faithful](integrality-makes-the-rank-one-theta-log-coordinate-faithful.md)
  — `Q=sum k_p log(p)` is injective on integral finite-support valuation
  packets by unique factorization, despite having a huge kernel on the
  continuous label span. This supplies an exact hostile model for the
  operator's integrality intuition: completion can create dark directions if
  it erases the discrete label register. The actual completed topology must
  decide whether that loss occurs.

- [The full theta trajectory is faithful while one Mellin port can be dark](the-full-theta-trajectory-is-faithful-while-one-mellin-port-can-be-dark.md)
  — corrects the strongest integrality-loss reading. Distinct Gaussian label
  trajectories are linearly independent and form a strictly totally positive
  observation family. A Riemann zero is selected Mellin-channel darkness with
  complementary theta ports still bright, not disappearance of the complete
  source packet.

- [Finite theta packets are reachable and jointly observable before Mellin selection](finite-theta-packets-are-reachable-and-jointly-observable-before-mellin-selection.md)
  — prime constructors reach every state of a divisor-closed packet, while
  the full Gaussian port family is jointly faithful. Scalar zeros are
  therefore intrinsic selected-channel dark events, not unreachable or
  unobservable modes. The missing RH mechanism is a source-derived outer or
  minimum-phase law for the Mellin selector.

- [A two-mode Mellin zero line is set exactly by amplitude balance](a-two-mode-mellin-zero-line-is-set-exactly-by-amplitude-balance.md)
  — derives the vertical zero line of two positive Gaussian modes from their
  amplitude-to-rate ratio. Half-density balance places it at `Re(s)=1/2`, and
  an equal centered reciprocal pair becomes a cosh packet with seam-only
  zeros. Aspect's hostile proves that pairwise balance does not survive
  arbitrary scalar aggregation; the missing law is all-label coherence.

- [Distinct reciprocal cosh rates cannot have global common interlacing](distinct-reciprocal-cosh-rates-cannot-have-global-common-interlacing.md)
  — closes the obvious all-label shortcut. Seam-zero density is proportional
  to the rate, so distinct arithmetic packets cannot possess globally
  interlacing zeros. Any variation-diminishing mechanism must arise after
  continuous theta/Poisson regrouping or in a different common spectral
  carrier.

- [The common theta carrier does not have an ordinary arithmetic Fourier factor](the-common-theta-carrier-does-not-have-an-ordinary-arithmetic-fourier-factor.md)
  — performs that regrouping exactly: every label is a translate of one
  archimedean profile. The arithmetic comb has exponential logarithmic growth
  and is not tempered, so its apparent common spectral density does not exist
  under ordinary Fourier rotation. Theta convolution is nevertheless defined
  by super-exponential carrier decay. The live operation is now a
  boundary-bearing Fourier--Laplace or Tate continuation, not another scalar
  interlacing argument.

- [The arithmetic comb has an exact weighted duality threshold](the-arithmetic-comb-has-an-exact-weighted-duality-threshold.md)
  — constructs the first honest test-space scale. The comb
  `sum n^(-beta) delta_(-log n)` is continuous on the exponential
  negative-ray space of decay `a` exactly when `a+beta>1`. Spectral
  characters reach only the corresponding Dirichlet convergence chamber;
  pure Fourier characters lie outside it. Tate completion must therefore be
  a boundary-value correspondence across this domain wall.

- [The first comb boundary quotient is the continuum-density mode](the-first-comb-boundary-quotient-is-the-continuum-density-mode.md)
  — derives the boundary port from weighted counting rather than analytic
  hindsight. Its transform is exactly the pole at the first convergence wall;
  retaining it separately leaves a residual analytic one full strip farther.
  The completed object is the lossless pair of residual and continuum mode.
  The next gate is opposite-incidence identification with the reflected
  archimedean boundary.

- [Poisson sewing closes the first boundary port to a two-endpoint pair](poisson-sewing-closes-the-first-boundary-port-to-a-two-endpoint-pair.md)
  — corrects the anticipated cancellation. Reflection supplies a second port
  at the reciprocal endpoint; together they form the invariant rational
  packet `1/(s(s-1))`. The completion polynomial `s(s-1)` is its scalar
  annihilator. The next gate is a source-derived boundary complex whose
  determinant produces that polynomial before scalar compression.

- [The theta endpoint dilation complex derives both the pole packet and completion polynomial](theta-endpoint-dilation-complex-derives-both-the-pole-packet-and-completion-polynomial.md)
  — supplies that complex noncircularly. The source endpoint modes are
  `1` and `t^(-1/2)`; Mellin-covariant dilation has eigenvalues `s` and
  `s-1`. Its determinant is `s(s-1)`, while its incidence-oriented inverse
  trace is `1/(s(s-1))`. Endpoint poles are now genuine cohomological
  punctures; no such bridge has yet been earned for nontrivial zeros.

- [The finite-seam hostile does not lift to the integer heat tower](the-finite-seam-hostile-does-not-lift-to-the-integer-heat-tower.md)
  — separates finite germ data from the global source constructor. Integer
  heat packets inherit the spectral gap at (pi) and therefore decay like a
  polynomial times (e^{-pi t}). The all-finite-order SCC hostile decays only
  like a Gaussian in (log t), so it cannot lift to that tower. The next
  selector is full labelled reciprocal Poisson sewing, not another jet rung.

- [Full labelled Poisson sewing rigidly selects the unweighted integer comb](full-labelled-poisson-sewing-rigidly-selects-the-unweighted-integer-comb.md)
  — classifies the source-admissible weighted square-spectrum cone at the
  distributional level. The Fourier transform of any integer-supported
  tempered measure is one-periodic; Fourier self-sewing therefore makes the
  original comb one-periodic and forces every weight to be equal. Weighted
  hostiles are excluded before completion. On the even tempered sector, exact
  scalar modularity at every positive scale is already faithful because the
  Gaussian scale family becomes an injective half-line Laplace transform. The
  remaining RH gate is later: completion and Mellin codiagonal compression.

- [Poisson source rigidity does not confine codiagonal crossings](poisson-source-rigidity-does-not-confine-codiagonal-crossings.md)
  — identifies the real post-rigidity obstruction. The fixed endpoint channel
  makes the full completed packet nowhere zero, so every xi zero is a
  codiagonal incidence event. The reciprocal packet
  (v_a(z)=(1,z^2-a^2)) is nowhere zero yet its fixed scalar projection has
  off-seam zeros at (z=\pm a). Source uniqueness and reciprocal symmetry
  therefore cannot orient the divisor; the remaining theorem concerns the
  projective velocity of the actual endpoint–bulk Mellin orbit.

- [The endpoint–bulk projective orbit is an affine repackaging of xi](the-endpoint-bulk-projective-orbit-is-an-affine-repackaging-of-xi.md)
  — closes the two-coordinate projective route. The exact border gives
  (X=1/2+B), so the endpoint-chart coordinate is (r=2X-1), its velocity is
  (2X'), and avoiding the incidence hyperplane (r=-1) is exactly
  zero-freeness. This geometry diagnoses codiagonal cancellation but adds no
  RH force. The next meaningful projective object must retain the full
  labelled heat packet before summation.

- [The three-channel theta packet has one dynamic cokernel coordinate](the-three-channel-theta-packet-has-one-dynamic-cokernel-coordinate.md)
  — identifies the smallest faithful pre-codiagonal packet. With fixed control
  (C=1/2), the coordinates (X=C+U+V) and (A=U-V) recover both reciprocal
  sectors. On (X=0), only (A) remains dynamically free. Every additional
  static linear port restricts to a multiple of (A) plus the fixed control,
  so static tower enlargement is saturated. The next rung must be transport,
  history, derivative, or nonlinear coherence acting on (A).

- [The first Ward current is a coordinate change, not a dynamic law](the-first-ward-current-is-a-coordinate-change-not-a-dynamic-law.md)
  — audits the first dynamic-looking rung. The exact identity
  (J+k(0)=-F/2-zG) makes ((F,J)) an invertible coordinate system for
  ((F,G)) whenever (z\neq0). On (F=0), the current is simply
  (-zG) plus the fixed endpoint. Every differentiated Ward-current jet is a
  triangular repackaging of the value jets, so no finite Ward rung adds
  orientation. The next law must be independently generated and nonlocal,
  adjoint-coupled, or nonlinear.

- [The endpoint face is the unbounded desmoothing of the source-adjoint face](the-endpoint-face-is-the-unbounded-desmoothing-of-the-source-adjoint-face.md)
  — derives the missing covector comparison exactly:
  (mathsf H_f\delta_0=f) and hence (B_f^*=E_0\mathsf H_f).
  The self-adjoint autocorrelation face is the endpoint face after one extra
  Hankel smoothing; recovering the physical Evans face requires the unbounded
  inverse of an infinite-rank compact operator. Curvature positivity therefore
  cannot pass to the xi divisor through a bounded Hilbert comparison. The live
  theorem must control desmoothing on the authorized orbit or retain the
  endpoint distribution independently.

- [Character-orbit desmoothing is exact but endpoint-zero blind](character-orbit-desmoothing-is-exact-but-endpoint-zero-blind.md)
  — sharpens the preceding obstruction. In one open sector,
  (mathsf H_f^{-1}G_z=e_z) is already exact and
  (|e_z|^2=-1/(2\operatorname{Re}z)); nevertheless (G_z(0)=0) is
  compatible with nonzero history and character states. Controlling the
  unbounded inverse explains the curvature/Evans mismatch but does not confine
  zeros. RH force requires an additional reciprocal cross-chart boundary law.

- [Reciprocal desmoothed characters have no common Hilbert fiber](reciprocal-desmoothed-characters-have-no-common-hilbert-fiber.md)
  — types the doubled boundary obstruction exactly. On the positive half-line,
  (e_z) is Hilbert only for (\operatorname{Re}z<0), while (e_{-z}) is
  Hilbert only for (\operatorname{Re}z>0); they never coexist, and neither
  is Hilbert on the seam. Their bilinear product is identically one, leaving
  only a universal cutoff-length current. Reciprocal sewing must therefore
  occur after source smoothing or in a rigged relative category, not through
  a positive doubled-character Gram form.

- [The Green residual is the oriented order port of the source pair](the-green-residual-is-the-oriented-order-port-of-the-source-pair.md)
  — records a mandatory scope correction. Since
  \(\operatorname{sgn}(x)\sinh(zx)=\sinh(z|x|)\), the full one-sided
  autocorrelation reconstructs the residual exactly. It is an exchange-even
  separation port, not an independent orientation channel. A genuine
  exchange-odd candidate requires two independently source-derived histories
  and has the form \(L_{f,g}=-L_{g,f}\).

- [Positive absolute separation does not confine complex zeros](positive-oriented-separation-does-not-confine-complex-zeros.md)
  — supplies the minimal hostile source for separation-transform confinement.
  The residual reduces exactly to the hyperbolic-sine transform of the positive
  absolute-separation measure. Nevertheless the positive source
  \(\delta_0+2\delta_1+\delta_2\) gives
  \(4\sinh z(2+\cosh z)\), with zeros at
  \(z=\pm\operatorname{arcosh}(2)+(2k+1)\pi i\). Simultaneous reciprocal
  reflection preserves the port because it reverses order and separation
  together. The missing law must therefore restrict the theta separation
  measure by labelled arithmetic coherence, not by positivity or reciprocal
  symmetry alone.

- [The Clark cross-history port is an autocorrelation derivative](the-clark-cross-history-port-is-an-autocorrelation-derivative.md)
  — tests the first genuinely exchange-odd candidate on the existing
  source-authorized Clark pair. For
  \(h_{\pm a}(q)=(1\mp aq)f(q)\), the symmetric product terms vanish against
  the odd separation kernel and the survivor is exactly
  \(L_a(z)=aC_f'(z)\). The Clark sheets are independently typed but generate
  only a tangent inside the same autocorrelation tower. A new comparison port
  must therefore come from distinct constructor paths, not opposite affine
  deformations of one source.

- [The primal-dual cross-history port vanishes after Poisson sewing](the-primal-dual-cross-history-port-vanishes-after-poisson-sewing.md)
  — closes the other authorized history pair. In a common Fourier frame the
  exchange-odd comparison factors through the two complete transform vectors.
  Completed theta sewing makes the primal and dual histories equal, so the
  determinant vanishes identically. Before sewing, their mismatch is already
  retained by the four-channel Poisson packet and its product testers. No
  failure of local tomography occurs at this level; a genuinely new carrier
  must live in the labelled arithmetic boundary module beyond the complete
  archimedean transform packet.

- [The Euler-to-Green incidence has rank one](the-euler-to-green-incidence-has-rank-one.md)
  — audits the labelled arithmetic boundary module itself. Primitive,
  prime-square, connected-tail, and archimedean currents are distinct source
  data, but their authorized path to the Green system factors as
  \(T_s(\nu)=\nu(f)G_s\). Even the full filtered packet therefore has
  one-dimensional analytic image. Its evaluation kernel retains provenance
  but is invisible to the zero-state dynamics. New RH force requires a second
  independently sourced response row or an operator-valued incidence, proved
  by a nonzero minor before scalar aggregation.

- [The Mellin position row restores rank-two boundary incidence](the-mellin-position-row-restores-rank-two-boundary-incidence.md)
  — shows that the preceding rank-one neck was partly a lossy even quotient.
  The existing source-derived odd response \(Qf=qf\) separates the relative
  atom \(j_L\), while \(f\) separates the symmetric atom \(s_L\). Their
  response minor is \(4Lf(L)^2>0\). Fourier closure supplies the derivative
  mate \(D\), so the native full observer is the \((Q,D)\) graph pair. The
  remaining obstruction is a common boundary-domain law coupling this
  rank-two observer to the completed scalar zero-state.

- [The Q-D tail mate square closes by the spectral tangent](the-QD-tail-mate-square-closes-by-the-spectral-tangent.md)
  — computes the common-domain coherence exactly. Tail propagation commutes
  strictly with the derivative row, while
  \(R_sQ=QR_s+\partial_sR_s\). Endpoint evaluation turns the residual into
  \(F'(s)\), and on a scalar zero the full graph packet is
  \((F'(s_0),-f(0))\). This is a canonical higher coherence cell lifting
  scalar cancellation to a phase-space boundary relation. It observes
  transversality but supplies no zero confinement until reciprocal copies are
  paired by a theta-specific conserved form.

- [No constant reciprocal form on the endpoint packet confines zeros](no-constant-reciprocal-form-on-the-endpoint-packet-confines-zeros.md)
  — classifies all constant forms under the reciprocal action
  \(S=\operatorname{diag}(-1,1)\). Invariant bilinear or Hermitian forms are
  diagonal and erase side orientation. Anti-invariant forms are off-diagonal
  and reduce to the transversality scalar \(f(0)F'(z)\), with no horizontal
  displacement factor. Hence endpoint symplectic or Clifford geometry cannot
  confine zeros. The required \(2\operatorname{Re}z\) coefficient must arise
  from the dynamic generator and full Green current.

- [The additive Tate incidence generates the horizontal Green coefficient](the-additive-tate-incidence-generates-the-horizontal-green-coefficient.md)
  — derives the missing dynamic cell from the source-native parity-changing
  operator \(P=e^{-q}(\partial_q-\tfrac12)\). Its tail mate square shifts
  Mellin grade exactly, \(R_sP=PR_{s-1}\), and its skew Green formula produces
  the coefficient \(2\operatorname{Re}z\) multiplying the positive weighted
  tail energy. At a scalar zero one explicit forcing pairing remains. The live
  descent gate is whether reciprocal arithmetic sewing makes that pairing an
  exact typed boundary current; prime \(2\) is the first finite falsifier.

- [The prime-two additive incidence does not descend to the valuation seam](the-prime-two-additive-incidence-does-not-descend-to-the-valuation-seam.md)
  — runs that falsifier. The finite-place difference operator obeys the exact
  additive Fourier square, but translation by \(1/2\) sends the
  valuation-equivalent points \(1/2\) and \(3/2\) to points of valuations
  \(0\) and \(1\). Hence no valuation-only \(P_{\mathrm{fin}}\) exists.
  Primitive and square Euler returns cannot directly cancel the archimedean
  forcing through the proposed incidence. The surviving comparison must
  retain additive residue and multiplicative valuation coordinates together.

- [The residue--valuation closure is an infinite local affine tree](the-residue-valuation-closure-is-an-infinite-local-affine-tree.md)
  — constructs that minimal local enlargement. The exact affine relation
  \(D_p\tau_hD_p^{-1}=\tau_{h/p}\) forces every deeper residue translation
  from the first one. At depth \(k\), the unramified vacuum generates the
  \(p^k\) independent cosets of \(p^{-k}\mathbb Z_p/\mathbb Z_p\).
  Consequently no finite repair carrier closes. The dilation--difference
  commutator is the difference of adjacent residue shells; every finite
  cutoff leaves a terminal next-shell term whose completion is now the live
  boundary-current gate.

- [The residue-tree terminal shell converges to a nonzero corona boundary](the-residue-tree-terminal-shell-converges-to-a-nonzero-corona-boundary.md)
  — separates inductive residue states from Fourier-dual projective
  observables through
  \(\mathbb Q_p/\mathbb Z_p\leftrightarrow\mathbb Z_p\). Finite packets pair
  perfectly, but the translated vacuum cosets have constant norm while
  converging weakly to zero. Their differences converge weakly to minus the
  unramified vacuum. Hence conductor telescoping leaves a nonzero corona
  common mode; doubled reciprocal sewing must cancel it source-locally before
  any vanishing-flux Green argument can descend.

- [Reciprocal Fourier sewing preserves the corona sign](reciprocal-fourier-sewing-preserves-the-corona-sign.md)
  — constructs the reciprocal terminal tree independently. Direct translated
  cosets and reciprocal character states are exact Fourier mates, and both
  converge weakly to minus the self-dual unramified vacuum. Fourier sewing
  therefore identifies their corona states with the same sign; summation
  doubles the defect. Cancellation requires an independently source-derived
  alternating Čech or mapping-cone incidence between the two charts.

- [The relative corona complex is split exact and has no zero-selection force](the-relative-corona-complex-is-split-exact-and-has-no-zero-selection-force.md)
  — constructs that alternating incidence from the two independent chart
  restrictions. Compatible Fourier pairs retain twice their bulk norm while
  their oriented boundary flux cancels. But the resulting relative sequence
  is split exact for every Fourier isomorphism and every source state. It
  removes the corona without selecting a spectral parameter. The live top
  cell is equality of the two independently generated Green forcing states,
  not equality of their degree-zero terminal coronas.

- [The relative Green forcing is an oscillatory autocorrelation transform](the-relative-green-forcing-is-an-oscillatory-autocorrelation-transform.md)
  — computes that dynamic top cell. The reciprocal forcing mismatch is the
  cosine transform of the positive weighted autocorrelation \(A_f(d)\), with
  horizontal weight \(\sinh(xd)\). At a paired zero the positive doubled
  Green energy equals minus the same transform divided by \(x\). Formal
  relative exactness therefore does not close RH: the remaining theorem is
  theta-specific orientation of this fixed oscillatory autocorrelation.

- [The mixed primitive-square extension is an exact valuation filtration step](the-mixed-primitive-square-extension-is-an-exact-valuation-filtration-step.md)
  — proves that primitive exclusion plus valuation degree, after removing the
  universal identity line, is exactly the square-and-higher current. Globally
  it acts by \(\log(n/\operatorname{rad}(n))\), so the squarefree sector is
  its kernel. The exceptional currents are filtration coordinates rather than
  an RH-selecting extension.

- [The additive theta--Euler determinant comparison is canonical but divisor-neutral](the-additive-theta-euler-determinant-comparison-is-canonical-but-divisor-neutral.md)
  — constructs the complete comparison in the convergence chamber. Bosonic
  prime occupations become integer labels by unique factorization, their
  trace is both the Euler product and Dirichlet sum, and the theta Mellin
  transform adds the archimedean chart. Analytic continuation rejects every
  hostile divisor multiplier that preserves this source comparison. This
  proves canonical-section rigidity but supplies no zero confinement: the
  remaining RH content is a law orienting the divisor of the already
  authorized section.

- [Hurwitz retypes RH as two-sector strict completion](hurwitz-retypes-rh-as-two-sector-strict-completion.md)
  — proves that locally uniform limits of unit-renormalized finite Euler
  sections remain nowhere zero in each open reciprocal sector. Sectorwise
  source-derived unit completion therefore implies RH immediately, while RH
  gives the backward existence of such units. A completed zero is rigorously
  a failure of strict invertible descent. The constructive frontier is now
  exact: derive the two renormalization families and their normal-family
  compactness without using the completed divisor.

- [The right-sector completion obstruction is exactly the primitive-prime current](the-right-sector-completion-obstruction-is-exactly-the-primitive-prime-current.md)
  — separates Euler grades on \(1/2<\Re s<1\). The square-and-higher
  logarithmic determinant converges locally uniformly to a holomorphic unit;
  only the primitive sum can fail strict completion. Möbius inversion shows
  that its only right-sector branch obstruction is \(\log\zeta(s)\).
  Consequently RH reduces constructively to deriving one source-native
  primitive unit without importing the completed divisor.

- [A Riemann zero is where the additive-to-connected comparison loses meaning](a-riemann-zero-is-where-the-additive-to-connected-comparison-loses-meaning.md)
  — identifies the primitive obstruction in source space. The Mellin
  transform of prime-power density minus continuum density is, up to an
  explicit holomorphic archimedean term, \(-\xi'/\xi\). Thus passing from
  the additive theta section to the multiplicative connected current requires
  inversion and becomes undefined exactly at a zero; the pole residue records
  multiplicity. RH now says that this comparison failure is confined to the
  unitary Fourier--Tate interface.

- [RH is temperedness of the half-normalized prime discrepancy](rh-is-temperedness-of-the-half-normalized-prime-discrepancy.md)
  — changes to logarithmic source coordinate and proves that the connected
  prime current is the Laplace transform of
  \(e^{-u/2}(\psi(e^u)-e^u)\). Temperedness of this boundary state makes the
  transform holomorphic in the right sector and implies RH; RH supplies the
  converse polynomial bound. The half-offset is therefore exactly the
  regularity threshold between tempered boundary data and an off-line
  exponentially growing mode.

- [RH is continuity of connected realization, not existence of the Euler logarithm](rh-is-continuity-of-connected-realization-not-existence-of-the-euler-logarithm.md)
  — separates formal source algebra from analytic descent. The plethystic
  logarithm always produces the prime-power current. Logarithmic atom
  realization, continuum subtraction, and the half-density twist produce a
  boundary distribution whose causal primitive is the normalized
  prime-counting discrepancy. RH is exactly the assertion that this
  connected realization lands continuously in the tempered rigging; formal
  logarithm existence alone is automatic.

- [Poisson sewing of prime exclusion generates the full Ramanujan packet](poisson-sewing-of-prime-exclusion-generates-the-full-ramanujan-packet.md)
  — gives the smallest cross-prime obstruction to a strict local comparison.
  Modulo six, the prime-2 and prime-3 exclusion transforms omit modes that
  appear in the Fourier transform of their product. Generally the squarefree
  unit indicator transforms to the full Ramanujan packet. A source proof of
  primitive-current temperedness must therefore retain the profinite
  residue/character carrier and its mixed-prime modes before scalarization.

- [The normalized Ramanujan packet has a prime-square Hilbert anomaly](the-normalized-ramanujan-packet-has-a-prime-square-hilbert-anomaly.md)
  — computes the exact mean-one fluctuation energy
  \(Q/\varphi(Q)-1\), equivalently the Parseval energy of all nonzero
  Ramanujan modes. It grows like \(e^\gamma\log y\) on primorial cutoffs.
  The divergence is the critical prime-square current. Thus full mode
  retention is faithful but raw Hilbert completion fails; the square
  covariance line must remain explicit inside a weaker tempered rigging.

- [Square-normalized Ramanujan energy escapes to a conductor corona](square-normalized-ramanujan-energy-escapes-to-a-conductor-corona.md)
  — normalizes the exact variance and proves that the resulting unit vectors
  converge weakly to zero against every fixed-conductor cylinder observable.
  They cannot converge strongly because their norms remain one. Thus the
  square line corrects magnitude but leaves a genuine conductor-infinity
  boundary state. Any RH completion must couple this arithmetic corona to the
  archimedean half-density boundary before scalarization.

- [The conductor corona is multiplicative Haar singular to additive Haar](the-conductor-corona-is-multiplicative-haar-singular-to-additive-haar.md)
  — identifies the escaping state exactly. Mean-one sieve densities form a
  positive martingale that converges almost surely to zero but not in
  expectation. Their projective measure limit is Haar measure on
  \(\widehat{\mathbb Z}^\times\), supported on an additive-Haar-null set.
  Thus local density comparisons become globally singular; the RH carrier
  must be an additive--multiplicative measure-class correspondence coupled to
  the archimedean half-density.

- [The additive and multiplicative vacua are disjoint half-density sectors](the-additive-and-multiplicative-vacua-are-disjoint-half-density-sectors.md)
  — takes the canonical square root of each finite measure change. Every
  half-density has norm one, but its overlap with the additive vacuum and
  every fixed-conductor cylinder observable tends to zero. The global local-
  overlap product vanishes. Hence the two vacua lie in disjoint
  infinite-product representations; their bridge must be a correspondence
  with a relative determinant line, not a vector in additive \(L^2\).

- [The prime-square countercurrent restores the relative overlap, not the vector](the-prime-square-countercurrent-restores-the-relative-overlap-not-the-vector.md)
  — multiplies the vanishing half-density overlap by the canonical
  exponential of half the prime-square current. The remainder has an
  absolutely convergent Euler logarithm and tends to a positive,
  exhaustion-independent constant. This constructs the relative determinant
  scalar exactly, while the underlying unit vectors remain weakly escaping.
  Determinant coherence therefore does not imply representation equivalence
  or RH orientation.

- [All Ramanujan packets are shadows of one Schwartz--Bruhat distribution](all-ramanujan-packets-are-shadows-of-one-schwartz-bruhat-distribution.md)
  — places multiplicative unit Haar in the finite-adelic distributional
  rigging. Finite sieve measures converge eventually on every test, and the
  conductor-\(Q\) Fourier coefficients are exactly
  \(c_Q(k)/\varphi(Q)\). Thus the angular conductor completion is canonical;
  it must be paired with the already constructed radial valuation/Fock tower
  before coupling to the half-normalized archimedean tempered boundary.

- [The unramified Tate readout compresses the angular carrier to rank one](the-unramified-tate-readout-compresses-the-angular-carrier-to-rank-one.md)
  — computes the local radial--angular decomposition. Every nontrivial
  multiplicative unit character integrates to zero, while the trivial angular
  mode yields the radial Euler factor. Ramanujan modes certify additive
  Poisson coherence but are invisible to the scalar Tate observer. Any
  RH-bearing angular contribution must therefore couple to radial Mellin flow
  before the rank-one quotient.

- [Diagonal rational incidence makes angular data redundant](diagonal-rational-incidence-makes-angular-data-redundant.md)
  — proves that the complete valuation vector of a positive rational
  determines every local unit coordinate. Global diagonal Poisson incidence
  therefore compares radial and angular presentations of the same source
  label but contributes no independent angular state. The remaining
  RH-bearing object must be a comparison-of-constructions coherence between
  additive theta completion and multiplicative Euler formation.

- [Mellin transport is the additive-to-multiplicative comparison cell](mellin-transport-is-the-additive-to-multiplicative-comparison-cell.md)
  — derives that higher cell exactly. Mellin transport converts the globally
  coupled integer heat weight into the prime-factorized Euler character, with
  the gamma factor retained; Poisson reflection and the two polar currents
  complete the square. The cell explains agreement of the constructions but
  remains compatible with scalar cancellation. The missing rung is a
  source-derived law on this completed comparison itself.

- [Standard Tate constructor closure is hostile-divisor blind](standard-tate-constructor-closure-is-hostile-divisor-blind.md)
  — proves the current comparison machinery is too natural: a
  Fourier-self-dual hostile archimedean substitution preserves restricted
  tensor formation, Poisson sewing, Mellin comparison, finite Fock
  provenance, and boundary completion while introducing off-seam zeros. The
  first genuinely new operation must couple Gaussian annihilation, diagonal
  integer labels, and a retained boundary current nonseparably.

- [Gaussian annihilation and prime dilation form a flat connection](gaussian-annihilation-and-prime-dilation-form-a-flat-connection.md)
  — constructs that first nonseparable operation and proves
  \(A_tD_p=pD_pA_{t/p^2}\). It rejects polynomial--Gaussian hostile profiles
  source-locally, but on the physical Gaussian its curvature vanishes and its
  consequences are only the theta heat equation and gamma recurrence. The
  next viable object is relative boundary curvature of the completed
  Mellin--Poisson square.

- [Mellin--Poisson boundary curvature is the polar packet](mellin-poisson-boundary-curvature-is-the-polar-packet.md)
  — computes that curvature exactly. Completion turns its two currents into
  \(s/2\) and \((1-s)/2\), whose symmetric and antisymmetric coordinates are
  the neutral carrier \(1/2\) and the critical coordinate \(s-1/2\). At a
  nontrivial zero the packet does not vanish; the theta tail cancels its
  symmetric channel. A zero-to-flux theorem must therefore retain a mixed
  tail--carrier boundary state.

- [Theta two-copy labels collapse to divisor-variance blocks](theta-two-copy-labels-collapse-to-divisor-variance-blocks.md)
  — uses the exact translated-label source law to group every pair by
  \(k=mn\). The complete curvature has only two channels: primitive-profile
  curvature weighted by \(d(k)\), and arithmetic log-ratio variance weighted
  by \(V(k)=\sum_{d\mid k}(2\log d-\log k)^2\). This proves representation
  completeness before aggregation; analytic continuation must orient these
  two channels without help from a hidden third bulk.

- [Divisor-split variance is prime-diagonal](divisor-split-variance-is-prime-diagonal.md)
  — proves
  \(V(k)=d(k)\sum_{p^a\parallel k}a(a+2)(\log p)^2/3\).
  Centered divisor exponents kill every cross-prime covariance, so the
  arithmetic two-copy channel is exactly the local prime-square/Fisher layer.
  Any global RH force must arise in its completed aggregation with primitive
  archimedean curvature, not inside the variance packet.

- [The first nonzero zero jet is completion-neutral](the-first-nonzero-zero-jet-is-completion-neutral.md)
  — corrects the last phrase above at a hypothetical zero. For a completed
  factorization \(X=AZ\), product curvature is
  \(A^2\mathscr C_Z+Z^2\mathscr C_A\); hence the archimedean term vanishes
  wherever \(Z=0\). At any multiplicity, the first nonzero jet is changed
  only by the nowhere-zero factor \(A(s_0)\). Completion can confine zeros
  only through nonlocal transport or boundary laws, never pointwise zero-jet
  force.

- [The primitive theta label uniformly dominates the positive chamber](the-primitive-theta-label-uniformly-dominates-the-positive-chamber.md)
  — proves every higher-label ratio to \(n=1\) decreases away from the seam
  and that their total is below \(0.006001\) times the primitive profile on
  all \(u\ge0\). This makes primitive-plus-tail collision analysis canonical,
  while exposing the remaining need for a relative transform margin:
  oscillatory integration does not preserve pointwise dominance.

- [A full theta collision forces a primitive near-collision](a-full-theta-collision-forces-a-primitive-near-collision.md)
  — converts pointwise dominance into uniform absolute transform bounds. Any
  full double zero forces both the normalized primitive cosine value and its
  position-weighted sine tangent below \(0.006001\). The infinite label
  problem is thereby localized to a simultaneous near-collision of one
  explicit incomplete-gamma transform; compact reserve control and
  high-frequency endpoint asymptotics are the remaining gates.

- [The small theta tail carries the entire seam-derivative repair](the-small-theta-tail-carries-the-entire-seam-derivative-repair.md)
  — finds the obstruction to extending primitive dominance through the
  high-frequency regime. The primitive has \(\phi_1'(0)>0\), while completed
  evenness forces \(\Phi'(0)=0\); hence the value-small higher-label tail
  satisfies \(\tau'(0)=-\phi_1'(0)\) and cancels the entire leading seam
  derivative. The next perturbative reference must be seam-matched and its
  norm must control boundary jets.

- [A positive seam-matched primitive split](a-positive-seam-matched-primitive-split.md)
  — uses the bounded radial coordinate
  \(q=(1-e^{-2u})/2\) to construct
  \(R=\phi_1(1-\beta q)\) and
  \(E=\tau+\beta q\phi_1\). Both are positive, both have zero first seam
  derivative, and \(E<0.0292R\) uniformly. The leading asymptotic type is now
  repaired without losing pointwise control; the third odd seam jet is the
  next exact rung.

- [The third seam jet reopens after first-jet repair](the-third-seam-jet-reopens-after-first-jet-repair.md)
- [The odd seam-jet tower is global reflection in germ form](the-odd-seam-jet-tower-is-global-reflection-in-germ-form.md)
- [Value control and seam jets have no common relative-sup topology](value-control-and-seam-jets-have-no-common-relative-sup-topology.md)
- [Reflection averaging destroys primitive theta positivity](reflection-averaging-destroys-primitive-theta-positivity.md)
- [The theta tail is the exact anti-invariant repair current](the-theta-tail-is-the-exact-anti-invariant-repair-current.md)
- [Invariant positivity exists only after label completion](invariant-positivity-exists-only-after-label-completion.md)
- [No finite theta-label cutoff has global invariant positivity](no-finite-theta-label-cutoff-has-global-invariant-positivity.md)
- [The moving theta frontier has exactly zero continuum mass](the-moving-theta-frontier-has-exactly-zero-continuum-mass.md)
- [The moving theta frontier is an exact boundary current](the-moving-theta-frontier-is-an-exact-boundary-current.md)
- [The frontier current is Fourier-fixed and reciprocally positive](the-frontier-current-is-fourier-fixed-and-reciprocally-positive.md)
- [Mellin factorization separates the current from the arithmetic divisor](mellin-factorization-separates-the-current-from-the-arithmetic-divisor.md)
- [The integer comb is the unique positive self-dual affine lattice](the-integer-comb-is-the-unique-positive-self-dual-affine-lattice.md)
- [The frontier current is the unique minimal Fourier-fixed boundary state](the-frontier-current-is-the-unique-minimal-fourier-fixed-boundary-state.md)
- [The centered Euler generator explains the half-offset](the-centered-euler-generator-explains-the-half-offset.md)
- [Graded self-adjoint dilation does not confine overlap zeros](graded-self-adjoint-dilation-does-not-confine-overlap-zeros.md)
- [The canonical quarter-threshold Dirac operator is the carrier, not the zero operator](the-canonical-quarter-threshold-dirac-operator-is-the-carrier-not-the-zero-operator.md)
- [The logarithmic integer comb is not an ordinary Hilbert boundary trace](the-logarithmic-integer-comb-is-not-an-ordinary-hilbert-boundary-trace.md)
- [No single weighted Hilbert space carries both combs and the Gaussian vacuum](no-single-weighted-hilbert-space-carries-both-combs-and-the-gaussian-vacuum.md)
- [Opposite weighted sector riggings carry the relative comb interface](opposite-weighted-sector-riggings-carry-the-relative-comb-interface.md)
- [Relative translation is conserved and the comb defect is the primitive current](relative-translation-is-conserved-and-the-comb-defect-is-the-primitive-current.md)
- [The prime-valuation tower is a geometric boundary resolvent](the-prime-valuation-tower-is-a-geometric-boundary-resolvent.md)
- [The common primitive regulator domain collapses to the critical seam](the-common-primitive-regulator-domain-collapses-to-the-critical-seam.md)
- [The aggregate zero-tail lift is too regular to select the seam](the-aggregate-zero-tail-lift-is-too-regular-to-select-the-seam.md)
  — computes the next rung exactly and proves \(R'''(0)>0\), so the remainder
  carries the entire opposite third derivative required by completed
  evenness. High-frequency boundary type is an infinite odd-jet tower.
  Finite repair controls bounded frequencies only; global perturbation needs
  an even-germ reference or a topology retaining every odd seam jet.

- [Antiunitary reciprocal sewing preserves the order current](antiunitary-reciprocal-sewing-preserves-the-order-current.md)
  — proves that the physical Real reciprocal map preserves the order current
  and its positive slope. Scale reversal and conjugation contribute cancelling
  signs, closing the hoped-for bulk sign contradiction.

- [Hardy cross-incidence vanishes on a scalar zero](hardy-cross-incidence-vanishes-on-scalar-zero.md)
  — computes the half-line cross block as a rank-two incidence channel. Its
  current vanishes under the scalar zero equation \(a+b=0\), so finer
  labelled boundary provenance is mandatory.

- [Sparse Mellin grades have no special parity defect](sparse-mellin-grades-have-no-special-parity-defect.md)
  — proves by generalized Vandermonde orientation that any distinct set of
  Mellin grades has rank equal to its cardinality up to fiber size. Restoring
  one omitted grade removes exactly one blind dimension; parity decimation
  has no exceptional defect. Finite primitive, square, and jet ports cannot
  faithfully observe unbounded valuation fibers.

- [The complete Mellin grade tower is faithful under source decay](complete-mellin-grade-tower-is-faithful-under-source-decay.md)
  — packages all grades as derivatives of one analytic generating function.
  Under the source's exponential decay, vanishing of the complete germ forces
  every prime-power coefficient to vanish. Faithfulness is therefore a
  pro-object property, while every finite boundary packet remains blind.

- [Reciprocal sewing is faithful on germs but does not confine zeros](reciprocal-sewing-is-faithful-on-germs-but-does-not-confine-zeros.md)
  — derives the continuous anti-linear jet action
  \(m_k\mapsto(-1)^k\overline{m_k}\). It faithfully duplicates an off-seam
  zero germ rather than fixing its base point. Generalized-Vandermonde Fitting
  minors also show that no omitted Mellin grade is locally redundant before
  saturation. The remaining gate is a source-derived cross-germ law.

- [The Hilbert--Pólya gap is between exponential and resolvent calculi](hilbert-polya-gap-is-between-exponential-and-resolvent-calculi.md)
  — shows that a positive spectral measure and self-adjoint generator do not
  confine zeros of their exponential overlap, while the resolvent matrix
  coefficient is Herglotz. The missing theorem is a source-derived,
  divisor-preserving bridge from the theta Mellin overlap to a Weyl boundary
  event.

- [The semigroup--resolvent bridge does not preserve the divisor](semigroup-resolvent-bridge-does-not-preserve-the-divisor.md)
  — constructs the canonical sampled-orbit resolvent
  \(\sum_nX(z+n\tau)q^n\), then proves that an exponential zero merely
  kills its constant coefficient and creates no spectral event. The theta
  readout must arise as a boundary determinant before resolvent orientation
  can constrain its zeros.

- [The Evans divisor is a nonselfadjoint source--observer border](evans-divisor-is-a-nonselfadjoint-source-observer-border.md)
  — writes the exact divisor as the Schur complement
  \(\delta_0A_s^{-1}f\). The interior forcing vector and distributional
  endpoint observer are not adjoint ports, and ordinary self-adjointization
  replaces the physical divisor. A rigged conservative boundary system must
  retain both ports separately.

- [Matrix Weyl positivity does not confine cross-port zeros](matrix-weyl-positivity-does-not-confine-cross-port-zeros.md)
  — gives a strict two-by-two Herglotz matrix whose off-diagonal entry vanishes
  in the upper half-plane. Retaining distinct source and observer ports repairs
  typing but not zero confinement; the physical divisor must be a full
  boundary determinant, a source-fixed Schur complement, or a rigid
  lower-rank relation.

- [The full Weyl determinant is nonzero at a cross-port zero](full-weyl-determinant-is-nonzero-at-a-cross-port-zero.md)
  — tests the canonical full boundary determinant and proves that at a
  cross-response zero it equals the product of two nonzero diagonal Herglotz
  responses. Passive completion therefore separates the physical divisor
  from the full determinant. A relative determinant or nonstandard Evans
  boundary relation is required.

- [A native quarter-turn could convert cross zero to boundary rank loss](a-native-quarter-turn-could-convert-cross-zero-to-boundary-rank-loss.md)
  — separates passive orthogonality from symplectic degeneracy. On a
  two-dimensional value--flux boundary plane, a source-derived quarter-turn
  converts the physical cross pairing into an oriented determinant, so its
  zero becomes rank loss. The live audit is whether the doubled theta Green
  trace supplies this plane and divisor identity without an imported phase.

- [The theta-tail Evans readout is a native symplectic determinant](theta-tail-evans-readout-is-a-native-symplectic-determinant.md)
  — closes the audit: the transported source line has endpoint vector
  \((X(s),1)\), the endpoint condition is \((0,1)\), and their determinant
  is \(-X(s)\). A half-trace gauge makes the flow exactly symplectic. The
  zero-to-rank-loss bridge is proved; only a theta-specific reciprocal
  restriction on the symplectic path remains.

- [Traceless symplectic flow has no strict positive local metric](traceless-symplectic-flow-has-no-strict-positive-local-metric.md)
  — proves that a semidefinite Lyapunov form for a traceless generator in any
  fixed positive metric must vanish identically. The theta Evans flow and its
  finite reciprocal doubles therefore admit conservation but no strict bulk
  contraction. The live object is a source-derived boundary crossing form,
  moving metric, or completion anomaly.

- [Local Maslov orientation is universal, so confinement is global winding](local-maslov-orientation-is-universal-so-confinement-is-global-winding.md)
  — proves that every holomorphic Evans zero has positive local intersection
  index, independently of theta arithmetic. Local crossing signs cannot
  select the seam. With the canonical determinant bridge established, the
  live theorem is a source-derived zero-winding law for the transported
  boundary line on each open half-plane.

- [Even positive finite sources can have off-seam winding](even-positive-finite-sources-can-have-off-seam-winding.md)
  — gives the exact reciprocal two-mode falsifier
  \(\cosh z+a\cosh2z\) for \(0<a<1\), whose quadratic in \(\cosh z\)
  has a root below \(-1\). Finite positivity, symmetry, and symplectic Evans
  structure do not imply zero winding. Any confinement law must emerge only
  from the complete infinite theta source.

- [Positive reciprocal mode addition has no monotone zero flow](positive-reciprocal-mode-addition-has-no-monotone-zero-flow.md)
  — derives the exact simple-zero velocity under addition of one positive
  reciprocal mode and gives two-mode families moving zeros both toward and
  away from the seam. Infinite positive completion has no intrinsic
  zero-expulsion direction; a complete label orbit-descent law is required.

- [The remaining finite-rank extension is one Weyl-function identity](the-remaining-finite-rank-extension-is-one-weyl-function-identity.md)
  — freezes the self-adjoint reciprocal Dirac principal operator and its
  Evans maximal-isotropic boundary. Adding the source column and its adjoint
  reduces by Schur elimination to
  `D(lambda)=d-lambda-V*(A0-lambda)^(-1)V`. Self-adjointness confines this
  block's spectral events to the critical seam. The sole remaining bridge is
  to derive the framed Xi section as a nowhere-zero unit times this reduced
  determinant, with both zero directions, every boundary grade, and
  restricted-product completion retained.

- [A single Weyl function cannot be the entire Xi section](a-single-weyl-function-cannot-be-the-entire-xi-section.md)
  — finds the decisive analytic obstruction to the minimal block identity.
  The self-adjoint Dirac principal operator has continuous real spectrum, so
  its Weyl function has a nonzero spectral jump across the seam. Xi is entire,
  and no holomorphic nowhere-zero unit can remove that jump. The corrected
  bridge is a two-chart perturbation-determinant descent whose reciprocal
  transition cancels the cut while retaining every typed boundary current.

- [Ordinary finite theta cutoffs cannot be self-adjoint characteristic determinants](ordinary-finite-theta-cutoffs-cannot-be-self-adjoint-characteristic-determinants.md)
  — applies the finite-cutoff gate to the smallest positive reciprocal packet
  `1+2a cosh(Lz)`, which has explicit off-seam zeros for `0<a<1/2`.
  Consequently ordinary finite theta truncations cannot equal finite
  self-adjoint characteristic determinants. Any global spectral meaning of Xi
  must be born through a changing domain, relative determinant, or genuinely
  nonordinary completion whose removal of the finite off-seam divisor is
  independently source-derived.

- [The spectral and theta approximants are two directed systems with a third comparison tower](the-spectral-and-theta-approximants-are-two-directed-systems-with-a-third-comparison-tower.md)
  — separates ordinary analytic theta cutoffs from source-derived
  self-adjoint compressions; their finite divisors cannot agree. A third tower
  must compare only the completed objects while typing background
  cancellation and escaping zeros. The chart transition determines a line
  but not its section, since a common divisor-bearing factor leaves every
  transition law unchanged. A canonical determinant vector or boundary
  morphism is therefore an independent mandatory constructor.

- [The primitive and square currents are the det3 anomaly coordinates](the-primitive-and-square-currents-are-the-det3-anomaly-coordinates.md)
  — connects the exact arithmetic filtration to regularized determinant
  theory. The connected `k>=3` tail is the logarithm of a third-order
  perturbation determinant, while the separately retained primitive and
  square currents are its omitted first and second trace coordinates. A
  resolvent-trace logarithmic derivative fixes the section and rejects common
  divisor factors; the two anomaly currents fix the remaining normalization.
  The live gate is construction of the source-derived Schatten-three family.

- [The prime-diagonal det3 is canonical but the divisor lives in the primitive current](the-prime-diagonal-det3-is-canonical-but-the-divisor-lives-in-the-primitive-current.md)
  — constructs the exact family `K_s e_p=p^(-s)e_p`, which lies in Schatten
  class three precisely for `Re(s)>1/3` and is not Hilbert--Schmidt on the
  critical seam. Its third-order determinant is the connected `k>=3` Euler
  tail and is zero-free. The zeta divisor instead appears as logarithmic
  branching of the primitive prime current. RH is thereby narrowed to a
  primitive-current monodromy-to-self-adjoint-index theorem.

- [Prime-cutoff holonomy is exact and primitive monodromy is the off-seam divisor](prime-cutoff-holonomy-is-exact-and-primitive-monodromy-is-the-off-seam-divisor.md)
  — proves that normalized prime additions commute with exact identity
  holonomy separately in the primitive, square, and det3 channels. In the open
  half-sector `Re(s)>1/2`, all Möbius-scaled prime-zeta singularities lie
  outside, so primitive-current monodromy counts precisely the off-seam zeta
  divisor. The only remaining force is a source-derived theorem that this
  normalized holonomy stays trivial after reciprocal and archimedean
  completion.

- [The completed divisor is the derived-limit defect of finite trivializations](the-completed-divisor-is-the-derived-limit-defect-of-finite-trivializations.md)
  — applies Hurwitz rigidity to the normalized zero-free Euler sections.
  Their completed divisor can arise only through failure of normal convergence
  or invertibility of the finite trivializations. The resulting logarithmic
  completion anomaly is a derived-limit class whose integer periods count the
  zeros born at completion. RH becomes support of this class only on the
  reciprocal seam; the next genuine theorem is a uniform completed graph-norm
  bound on compact loops in each open sector.

- [The renormalized prime graph bound is RH in disguise](the-renormalized-prime-graph-bound-is-rh-in-disguise.md)
  — separates universal mean escape from divisor-bearing fluctuation escape.
  Raw Euler trivializations degenerate throughout the critical half-strip.
  After subtracting the continuum pole wall, their connection is the Mellin
  transform of `psi(x)-x`; controlling it on every compact open half-sector is
  equivalent in strength to the RH prime-counting estimate. The next
  explanatory move must realize this fluctuation as a source-derived operator
  boundary flux rather than estimate it directly.

- [The prime fluctuation is the required wave trace, not a spectral-shift shortcut](the-prime-fluctuation-is-the-required-wave-trace-not-a-spectral-shift-shortcut.md)
  — identifies the mean-subtracted von Mangoldt measure as the exact
  time-domain current behind the completed logarithmic derivative. Declaring
  it a spectral shift would confuse Laplace and resolvent transforms. The
  correct Hilbert--Pólya target is a source-derived self-adjoint evolution
  whose regularized wave trace equals this prime-power current; logarithmic
  differentiation then turns Xi zeros into its real resolvent poles.

- [Prime circles have exactly the von Mangoldt wave trace](prime-circles-have-exactly-the-von-mangoldt-wave-trace.md)
  — constructs one circle of circumference `log p` per prime. The periodic
  translation wave trace has returns at every `k log p`, each weighted by
  `log p`; summing primes gives the von Mangoldt distribution exactly. Its
  dynamical determinant is the Euler product and repetition depth recovers the
  primitive/square/det3 grades. The uncoupled circle spectra are not Riemann
  zeros, so the remaining gate is an adelic relative coupling that cancels
  local modes while preserving prime-return trace.

- [The critical half-density is the return amplitude of a unitary-dilated prime loop](the-critical-half-density-is-the-return-amplitude-of-a-unitary-dilated-prime-loop.md)
  — corrects the closed-circle amplitude. The local return contraction
  `r_p=p^(-1/2)` has a canonical minimal unitary dilation with one fixed vector
  functional whose `k`-step correlation is `p^(-k/2)`. Suspending it over
  `log p` produces the centered von Mangoldt current and the exact centered
  Euler transfer factor. The remaining obstruction is global implementability
  and source-derived coupling of all local defect channels.

- [The square current is the Kakutani obstruction between prime-dilation and Haar vacua](the-square-current-is-the-kakutani-obstruction-between-prime-dilation-and-haar-vacua.md)
  — computes the local Hellinger affinity as
  `1-1/(4p)+O(p^(-2))`. The prime sum diverges, so the critical arithmetic
  dilation state and Haar reference state are globally disjoint. The square
  current is therefore the exact representation-change anomaly, while the
  primitive channel carries the divisor and the `k>=3` tail is determinant
  class. Global sewing must be a correspondence between GNS sectors, not a
  bounded unitary inside one tensor product.
